# Codebase baseline (pre-ASME-Ops)

Generated 2026-09-08 from a full read of the repository at `b9774ee` (branch `codex/landing-webgl`). Route tables are the legacy compatibility list: every path here must keep working or redirect (exception recorded in CHANGELOG: `/app` -> `/legacy/app`).

## models

32 Flask-SQLAlchemy models across 7 domain modules under asme/models/, all subclassing a single `db.Model` from asme/extensions.py (`db = SQLAlchemy(metadata=MetaData(naming_convention=NAMING_CONVENTION))`, plus `migrate = Migrate()`). No mixins, no custom declarative base, no polymorphic inheritance. Every timestamp is a naive `datetime.utcnow` Python-side `default=` (no `server_default` except `events.kind` which migration 0002 added with `server_default='meeting'`). No relationship declares `cascade`, `lazy`, `backref`, or `passive_deletes`; no FK declares `ondelete`. Enum-like columns are plain `String` with no CHECK constraints. JSON is stored in `Text` columns; three models expose typed property accessors (Phase.grants, Task.rule_config, TaskState.evidence), two do not (Project.gallery_json, OutboxJob.payload_json). Schema is managed by Alembic via Flask-Migrate: baseline `0001_baseline` creates all 32 tables, `0002_launchpad` adds columns/indexes; `asme/services/bootstrap.py::sync_schema()` stamps a pre-Alembic DB at `0001_baseline` (detected by `users` table present and `alembic_version` absent) then upgrades to head. `User` (table `users`) is the canonical identity; `Member` (table `members`) is a legacy roster kept "for one release" and linked from `users.member_id` (nullable, unique FK -> 1:1 optional). Six tables still FK to `members.id`. Legacy tables: `members`, `print_jobs` (PrintJob), `attendance_scans` (AttendanceScan), `meetings` (Meeting). `Loan` is a module-level alias for `Transaction` (`Loan = Transaction`) used by services/inventory.py, blueprints, and onboarding rules.

**Key facts**

- db singleton: asme/extensions.py defines `db = SQLAlchemy(metadata=MetaData(naming_convention=NAMING_CONVENTION))` and `migrate = Migrate()`. Every model imports `from asme.extensions import db`.
- NAMING_CONVENTION = {ix: 'ix_%(column_0_label)s', uq: 'uq_%(table_name)s_%(column_0_name)s', ck: 'ck_%(table_name)s_%(constraint_name)s', fk: 'fk_%(table_name)s_%(column_0_name)s_%(referred_table_name)s', pk: 'pk_%(table_name)s'} -- chosen so Alembic batch mode (needed for SQLite ALTER TABLE) can address every constraint by name. Example generated names seen in 0002: uq_transactions_idempotency_key, fk_transactions_signed_off_by_user_id_users, ix_events_start_time.
- Column(unique=True) alone produces a named UNIQUE constraint (e.g. uq_members_email); Column(unique=True, index=True) produces a UNIQUE INDEX named ix_<table>_<col> instead (e.g. users.email, users.username, users.nfc_uid, nfc_tags.tag_uid, items.item_code, items.treasury_tracker_id, item_tags.tag_value, projects.slug, onboarding_tracks.key, training_modules.key, password_reset_tokens.token). Migration authors must drop the right object type.
- asme/models/__init__.py re-exports all 32 models plus `db` in `__all__`; docstring states table names are unchanged from the original single models.py. bootstrap.py imports `from asme.models import Announcement, Member, Project, User`, so a new model must be imported in __init__.py to be visible to `flask db migrate` autogenerate.
- All timestamps: `db.DateTime` naive UTC via `default=datetime.utcnow` (Python-side ORM default). `onupdate=datetime.utcnow` exists ONLY on: print_requests.updated_at, contact_messages.updated_at, onboarding_task_states.updated_at, onboarding_phase_states.updated_at. attendance_scans.attendance_date uses `default=date.today` (server-local date, not UTC).
- No relationship sets cascade/lazy/backref; all are default lazy='select'. back_populates pairs: PrintRequest.runs<->PrintRun.request (order_by PrintRun.id); Project.memberships<->ProjectMembership.project; Track.phases<->Phase.track (order_by Phase.order); Phase.tasks<->Task.phase (order_by Task.order). Every other relationship is one-directional many-to-one.
- Every multi-FK-to-users relationship specifies foreign_keys=[...] explicitly (User.member, NFCTag.user/assigned_by, Transaction.user/signed_off_by, PrintRequest.user/reviewed_by, PrintRun.started_by, Event.created_by/requested_by, AttendanceRecord.*, WorkLog.user/approved_by, ContactMessage.user, AuditLog.admin_user, TaskState.completed_by, Entitlement.user/granted_by/revoked_by, TrainingCompletion.user/recorded_by). Adding a second FK to users on a model that currently uses bare `db.relationship('User')` (StockLedger.user, StockDiscrepancy.resolved_by, ProjectMembership.user) will break mapper configuration until foreign_keys is added.
- Explicit __table_args__ exist on exactly 6 models: ProjectMembership (UniqueConstraint project_id,user_id name=uq_project_membership); Phase (UniqueConstraint track_id,key name=uq_phase_track_key); Task (UniqueConstraint phase_id,key name=uq_task_phase_key); TaskState (UniqueConstraint task_id,subject_type,subject_id name=uq_task_state_subject + Index ix_task_state_subject on subject_type,subject_id); PhaseState (UniqueConstraint phase_id,subject_type,subject_id name=uq_phase_state_subject + Index ix_phase_state_subject); Entitlement (non-unique Index ix_entitlement_user_key on user_id,key).
- User<->Member: users.member_id Integer NULL unique FK->members.id; `User.member = relationship('Member', foreign_keys=[member_id])`; Member has no reverse relationship. bootstrap.seed_defaults() creates one User per Member when users is empty: username=email local part, password_hash=generate_password_hash(cfg.default_user_password), role='team_leader' if 'lead' in member_class.lower(), 'admin' if asme.auth.session.is_admin_member(member), else 'member'; sets member_id=member.id. If no members exist it creates a bootstrap admin (name='ASME Admin', email=cfg.default_admin_email, role='admin').
- User.role observed values: 'member' (column default), 'team_leader', 'admin' (from bootstrap). Stored as String(30) with no CHECK constraint. User.display_name property = (name or email or f'user-{id}').strip(). The model has no password verify/set helpers; hashing is done by callers with werkzeug.
- Tables that FK to legacy members.id: users.member_id (NULL, unique), transactions.member_id (NULL, index; was NOT NULL in 0001, relaxed in 0002), print_requests.member_id (NULL, index), print_jobs.member_id (NOT NULL), attendance_records.member_id (NULL, index), attendance_scans.member_id (NOT NULL). Dropping `members` requires migrating all six.
- Legacy tables and gating: members (Member) -- compatibility roster; print_jobs (PrintJob) -- 'Retained for ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS=1 deployments only' (config.py: legacy_ops_enabled=_bool('ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS', default=False); routes in asme/blueprints/legacy_ops.py); attendance_scans (AttendanceScan) -- 'Read-only going forward', pre-Event day-based kiosk scan; meetings (Meeting) -- 'Used only by the legacy ops calendar page; the portal books through Event + CalendarSync'.
- Transaction/Loan semantics: one row per checkout, mutated on return. `status` String(20) NULL ('OUT'/'RETURNED') is the real state; `action` String(20) NOT NULL ('checkout'/'return') is retained for historical rows. Properties: is_open (status=='OUT'), is_overdue (is_open and due_date and due_date < utcnow().date()), returned_on_time (status=='RETURNED' and return_time; True if no due_date else return_time.date() <= due_date), borrower_name (user.name, else member.name, else ''). idempotency_key String(120) NULL unique added in 0002 for idempotent checkouts.
- Inventory stock model: items.available_qty is the fast atomic counter; stock_ledger is the append-only audit trail (reason in checkout/return/adjust/import/bootstrap, with delta_available/delta_total and available_after/total_after snapshots); stock_discrepancies is filed by nightly reconciliation when counter and open loans disagree (counter_available vs derived_available vs open_loan_qty, status open/resolved). Item.checked_out_qty = max(total_qty - available_qty, 0). Item also has redundant item_type String(20) default 'tool' AND is_consumable Boolean default False.
- NFC identity is stored in THREE places: members.nfc_tag String(120) NULL unique (legacy), users.nfc_uid String(160) NULL unique index, and nfc_tags.tag_uid String(160) NOT NULL unique index with user_id FK, active flag, assigned/unassigned timestamps and assigned_by. Item NFC is in TWO places: items.nfc_tag String(120) NULL unique and item_tags.tag_value String(160) NOT NULL unique index (source default 'uid').
- Onboarding engine keys state on (subject_type String(20), subject_id String(60)) -- NOT a user FK -- so one engine serves per-user tracks (Track.audience='user') and per-chapter/academic-year tracks (Track.audience='chapter'). subject_id is a STRING; CalendarSync.subject_id is an INTEGER. TaskState status: pending/in_progress/complete with current/target counters; PhaseState status: locked/active/complete. Phase.grants_json lists entitlement keys granted on phase completion; Entitlement.source is 'phase' or 'override', source_ref e.g. 'member:shop_ready'.
- JSON-in-Text accessors: Phase.grants (list[str]; json.loads with bare `except Exception: return []`, filters blank strings; setter json.dumps(list)), Task.rule_config (dict; non-dict -> {}; setter json.dumps(dict)), TaskState.evidence (dict; setter json.dumps(dict, default=str)). No accessor for Project.gallery_json (Text NULL, seeded as '[]') or OutboxJob.payload_json (Text NOT NULL default '{}').
- OutboxJob: kind String(60) index, status pending/running/done/failed, attempts/max_attempts (default 5), run_at index, locked_at (only lock field -- no worker/owner id), last_error String(1000). Config: ASME_OUTBOX_WORKER (default True), ASME_OUTBOX_POLL_SECONDS (default 15, min 1). Docstring: committed in the same transaction as the domain write, picked up by in-process worker.
- Alembic state: head = '0002_launchpad' (down_revision '0001_baseline'). 0001 creates all 32 tables. 0002 adds: users.phone; transactions.idempotency_key (+uq), transactions.signed_off_by_user_id (+fk), relaxes transactions.member_id to nullable, adds ix_transactions_{item_id,member_id,status}; events.kind (server_default 'meeting'), ix_events_{start_time,status}; ix_audit_logs_{action,created_at}; plus batch alters on the Launchpad/stock/outbox/calendar tables. sync_schema() returns 'stamped-baseline+upgraded' or 'upgraded'.
- Column named `order` (Integer NOT NULL default 0) exists on onboarding_phases and onboarding_tasks -- a SQL reserved word; SQLAlchemy quotes it, but raw SQL must quote it too.
- Time-typed columns: meetings.start_time / meetings.end_time are db.Time (with separate meeting_date Date), whereas events.start_time / end_time are db.DateTime. transactions.due_date, print_requests.deadline, work_logs.logged_for, attendance_scans.attendance_date are db.Date.
- No model has a soft-delete column except via domain flags: users.is_active, items.active, nfc_tags.active, onboarding_tracks.is_active, projects.is_joinable, project_memberships.left_at, entitlements.revoked_at.

**Extension points**

- Add a new model: create/extend a module under asme/models/, subclass `db.Model` from asme.extensions, import it in asme/models/__init__.py and append to `__all__` (autogenerate and bootstrap rely on the package import), then `flask db migrate` -> review -> `flask db upgrade`. Migrations must use `op.batch_alter_table` for any ALTER on SQLite; the naming convention guarantees addressable constraint names.
- Schema application at runtime: asme/services/bootstrap.py::sync_schema() (stamp '0001_baseline' if legacy DB, then upgrade to head) and seed_defaults(commit=True) -> projects, welcome announcement, users-from-members or bootstrap admin, seed_default_tracks(). New seed data belongs in seed_defaults or asme/services/onboarding/seeds.py and must stay idempotent (they gate on `.count() == 0`).
- New onboarding rule types: add a Task row with rule_type=<name> and rule_config (dict) and implement the evaluator in asme/services/onboarding/rules.py; state is written to onboarding_task_states keyed by (task_id, subject_type, subject_id[str]). Phase completion grants Entitlement rows via Phase.grants (list of keys).
- New gated capability: define an entitlement `key` string; grant via Entitlement(source='phase'|'override', source_ref, expires_at); check with Entitlement.is_active. Enforcement is behind ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE (default False).
- New background job: insert OutboxJob(kind=<new kind>, payload_json=json.dumps(...), run_at=...) in the same session/transaction as the domain write; add a handler for that `kind` in the outbox worker (asme/jobs/). Retry budget is max_attempts (default 5).
- New calendar provider / mirrored object: CalendarSync is polymorphic via subject_type (default 'event') + subject_id (Integer) + provider ('google'/'outlook'); add a provider string and integration under asme/integrations/. Event.google_event_id/google_calendar_id are legacy inline ids and should not be extended.
- New stock mutation reason: append to StockLedger.reason vocabulary (checkout/return/adjust/import/bootstrap); always write a ledger row alongside any change to items.available_qty/total_qty so the nightly reconciliation (StockDiscrepancy) stays meaningful. Use Transaction.idempotency_key for retry-safe checkouts.
- Per-user tracks vs chapter tracks: Track.audience ('user'|'chapter') selects the subject namespace; TaskState/PhaseState.subject_type/subject_id are free-form strings, so a new subject kind (e.g. 'team') can be added without schema change.
- Typed JSON columns: follow the Phase.grants / Task.rule_config / TaskState.evidence pattern (Text column + @property/@setter with defensive json.loads) rather than adding a JSON type, to stay portable across SQLite/Postgres.
- NFC lookups: nfc_tags (tag_uid unique, user_id, active) is the newest structure with assignment history; users.nfc_uid and members.nfc_tag are older single-slot fields. New code should read/write NFCTag; item side should use item_tags.tag_value.

**Risks / known defects**

- No FK declares ondelete and no relationship declares cascade: deleting a User, Item, Project, Event, Track/Phase/Task, TrainingModule, or PrintRequest with dependent rows raises IntegrityError on FK-enforcing backends (Postgres; SQLite only if PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON) and silently orphans rows otherwise. Every delete path must be explicit.
- All NOT NULL defaults are Python-side ORM defaults (default=), not server_default (only events.kind has one). Core `insert()`/bulk/raw-SQL writes bypass them and hit NOT NULL violations on created_at, role, is_active, status, etc. Adding a NOT NULL column to an existing table requires an explicit server_default in the migration.
- datetime.utcnow() naive timestamps everywhere; deprecated in Python 3.12+ (DeprecationWarning) and incompatible with timezone-aware comparisons. attendance_scans.attendance_date uses date.today() (server-local), so legacy attendance dates and utcnow-based times can disagree across midnight UTC.
- String-typed enums (User.role, Transaction.status/action, PrintRequest.status, PrintRun.status, Event.status/kind, CalendarSync.status/provider, OutboxJob.status, TaskState/PhaseState.status, Entitlement.source, StockDiscrepancy.status, Track.audience) have no CHECK constraints; typos persist silently and queries filtering on exact strings miss them.
- Six tables still FK to legacy members.id (users, transactions, print_requests, print_jobs, attendance_records, attendance_scans). The 'kept for one release' plan requires a data migration for each before `members` can be dropped; print_jobs and attendance_scans have NOT NULL member_id and no user_id at all.
- Transaction.member_id and user_id are both nullable -- a loan row can have neither borrower (borrower_name returns ''). Historical rows may carry action='return' rows that the 'one row per checkout, mutated on return' model does not expect; status is nullable for those.
- Entitlement has only a non-unique (user_id, key) index; 'is user entitled?' must aggregate across rows (any is_active), and a stale active row can linger alongside a revoked one. Similarly attendance_records lacks a (event_id,user_id) unique and training_completions lacks (module_id,user_id) unique.
- TaskState/PhaseState.subject_id is String(60) while users.id is Integer; joining or comparing requires str()/CAST and an integer-typed user id passed directly will silently create a distinct subject (e.g. 5 vs '5' handled by driver coercion differently across backends).
- Default lazy='select' on every relationship: list views over Transaction (member+user+item), PrintRequest (runs+user), TaskState (task->phase->track) are N+1-prone; no selectinload/joinedload hints on the models.
- project_memberships unique (project_id,user_id): a member who left (left_at set) cannot be re-added by inserting a new row; code must reuse/clear the existing row or it raises IntegrityError.
- OutboxJob locking uses only locked_at with no owner/worker id or lease token; multiple app instances (e.g. AWS Elastic Beanstalk scale-out, per recent commit) running the in-process worker can double-execute jobs unless the picker uses a DB-level UPDATE ... WHERE status='pending' guard.
- Items.available_qty counter and stock_ledger can diverge by design; correctness depends on every writer emitting a ledger row and on the nightly reconciliation job running. Item has both item_type and is_consumable, which can contradict each other.
- Three separate NFC fields for people (members.nfc_tag, users.nfc_uid, nfc_tags.tag_uid) and two for items (items.nfc_tag, item_tags.tag_value); all are independently unique so a UID can be assigned to a user in one table and an item in another with no cross-check.
- Bare `except Exception` in the JSON property getters (Phase.grants, Task.rule_config, TaskState.evidence) masks corrupt data as empty values; a bad rule_config silently turns a task into an unevaluable/manual one.
- bootstrap.seed_defaults migrates Members to Users with a shared default password (cfg.default_user_password, env ASME_DEFAULT_USER_PASSWORD default 'ChangeMe123!') and bootstrap admin default 'ChangeMe123!'; both must be overridden in production env.

## migrations

Alembic via Flask-Migrate (installed: Flask-Migrate 4.1.0, alembic 1.19.2, Flask-SQLAlchemy 3.1.1, SQLAlchemy 2.0.52; requirements pin Flask-Migrate>=4,<5). Single MetaData `db.metadata` from asme/extensions.py with a fixed NAMING_CONVENTION; `migrate.init_app(app, db, directory=ROOT/'migrations', render_as_batch=True)` in asme/__init__.py. env.py is the stock Flask-Migrate template (passes target_metadata=db.metadata and configure_args incl. render_as_batch; drops empty autogen revisions). Two linear revisions with hand-set ids: 0001_baseline (down_revision None; 17 tables = the pre-package schema) -> 0002_launchpad (down_revision '0001_baseline'; 15 new tables, 7 altered tables, plus a raw-SQL `_backfill_data()` for legacy data). asme/services/bootstrap.py: `sync_schema()` stamps a legacy DB (tables present, no alembic_version, has `users`) at BASELINE_REVISION='0001_baseline' then `upgrade()` to head; `seed_defaults()` idempotently seeds 3 projects, 1 announcement, the bootstrap admin (or one User per legacy Member), and calls `seed_default_tracks()` (3 training modules, 2 Launchpad tracks, 6 phases, 25 tasks, upserted by key). Entry points: `python manage.py upgrade` (Procfile release, render.yaml startCommand, EB container_commands leader_only), Flask CLI `flask upgrade-db` / `flask seed`, and create_app() auto-run when ASME_AUTO_MIGRATE (default on outside production, never in testing). Empirically verified on a fresh SQLite DB: sync_schema -> 'upgraded', alembic_version='0002_launchpad', alembic compare_metadata diff = 0 (compare_type=True), seed_defaults twice -> {'projects':3,'announcements':1,'users':1} then all zeros. Tests never run migrations (conftest uses db.create_all()). Column notation used in entities: `name Type NN|NULL [PK] [FK->table.col] [UQ:name] [IX:name(U)=unique] [sd=server_default]`.

**Key facts**

- Alembic wiring: asme/__init__.py:66 `migrate.init_app(app, db, directory=str(ROOT / 'migrations'), render_as_batch=True)`; `db = SQLAlchemy(metadata=MetaData(naming_convention=NAMING_CONVENTION))` and `migrate = Migrate()` in asme/extensions.py.
- NAMING_CONVENTION (asme/extensions.py:11-17): ix='ix_%(column_0_label)s' (-> ix_<table>_<col>), uq='uq_%(table_name)s_%(column_0_name)s', ck='ck_%(table_name)s_%(constraint_name)s', fk='fk_%(table_name)s_%(column_0_name)s_%(referred_table_name)s', pk='pk_%(table_name)s'.
- Unnamed constraints inside migration scripts (e.g. `sa.UniqueConstraint('nfc_tag')`, `sa.ForeignKeyConstraint([...])`) receive convention names at execution because env.py passes `target_metadata=get_metadata()` (Alembic copies target_metadata.naming_convention onto op metadata). Verified in migrated SQLite: uq_items_nfc_tag, uq_members_email, uq_members_nfc_tag, uq_users_member_id, uq_meetings_cancel_request_token, fk_transactions_user_id_users, fk_transactions_item_id_items, fk_transactions_member_id_members.
- env.py (stock Flask-Migrate template): fileConfig(alembic.ini) logging; get_engine() handles FSA<3 (`db.get_engine()`) and >=3 (`db.engine`); sets sqlalchemy.url from the engine URL with '%' escaped; get_metadata() -> `target_db.metadatas[None]` (FSA 3.x) else `target_db.metadata`; offline mode uses literal_binds=True; online mode spreads `current_app.extensions['migrate'].configure_args` (contains render_as_batch=True) into context.configure and injects `process_revision_directives` that empties directives when `upgrade_ops.is_empty()` (logs 'No changes in schema detected.'). No compare_server_default, include_object, version_table, or multi-bind customisation.
- alembic.ini contains only logging config (root WARN, sqlalchemy.engine WARN, alembic INFO, flask_migrate INFO; console handler on stderr; format '%(levelname)-5.5s [%(name)s] %(message)s'). `file_template` and `revision_environment` are commented out -> default file_template '%(rev)s_%(slug)s' (slug truncated to 40 chars), which is why files are named `0001_baseline_baseline_schema_before_the_asme_package.py` and `0002_launchpad_launchpad_stock_ledger_outbox_calendar_.py`. No script_location / sqlalchemy.url in the ini (set programmatically by Flask-Migrate / env.py).
- script.py.mako is the stock template: imports `from alembic import op` and `import sqlalchemy as sa`, `${imports}`, module vars revision/down_revision/branch_labels/depends_on, `upgrade()`/`downgrade()` defaulting to `pass`.
- Revision chain (linear, single head): revision='0001_baseline', down_revision=None, branch_labels=None, depends_on=None, Create Date 2026-09-08 12:14:49 -> revision='0002_launchpad', down_revision='0001_baseline'. Head = '0002_launchpad'. Revision ids are hand-chosen `NNNN_word`, not random hashes.
- 0001_baseline creates 17 tables in this order: announcements, items, meetings, members, projects, attendance_scans, item_tags, print_jobs, users, audit_logs, contact_messages, events, nfc_tags, password_reset_tokens, print_requests, transactions, attendance_records. Indexes created via `with op.batch_alter_table(t) as batch_op: batch_op.create_index(batch_op.f('ix_...'))`. No server_defaults anywhere in 0001; all defaults are Python-side in the models. downgrade() drops everything in reverse.
- 0002_launchpad creates 15 tables: calendar_syncs, onboarding_tracks, outbox_jobs, training_modules, onboarding_phases, entitlements, onboarding_phase_states, onboarding_tasks, project_memberships, stock_discrepancies, training_completions, work_logs, onboarding_task_states, print_runs, stock_ledger.
- 0002_launchpad alters 7 existing tables: attendance_records (+ix_attendance_records_checkin_time); audit_logs (+ix_audit_logs_action, +ix_audit_logs_created_at); events (+kind String(40) NN server_default='meeting'; +ix_events_start_time; +ix_events_status); print_requests (+ix_print_requests_status); projects (+is_joinable Boolean NN server_default=sa.true(), reflects as '1' in SQLite); transactions (+idempotency_key String(120) NULL; +signed_off_by_user_id Integer NULL; alter member_id nullable False->True; +ix_transactions_item_id, +ix_transactions_member_id, +ix_transactions_status; +UNIQUE uq_transactions_idempotency_key; +FK fk_transactions_signed_off_by_user_id_users -> users.id); users (+phone String(40) NULL).
- 0002 data backfill `_backfill_data()` runs unconditionally at the end of upgrade() via `bind = op.get_bind()` raw SQL: (1) `UPDATE transactions SET user_id = (SELECT u.id FROM users u JOIN members m ON lower(u.email)=lower(m.email) WHERE m.id=transactions.member_id LIMIT 1) WHERE user_id IS NULL AND member_id IS NOT NULL`; (2) `UPDATE events SET kind='meeting' WHERE kind IS NULL OR kind=''`; (3) `UPDATE projects SET is_joinable = 1 WHERE is_joinable IS NULL`; (4) `UPDATE items SET item_type=COALESCE(NULLIF(item_type,''),'tool'), is_consumable=COALESCE(is_consumable,0), active=COALESCE(active,1), min_stock_threshold=COALESCE(min_stock_threshold,0)`; (5) per item ordered by id: missing item_code -> `ASME-INV-{id:05d}` (de-duplicated case-insensitively with -2,-3... suffix); missing treasury_tracker_id -> `ASME-TRK-{code minus 'ASME-INV-' prefix}` (same de-dup); non-empty whitespace-collapsed items.nfc_tag -> INSERT item_tags(item_id, tag_value, source='legacy_item_tag', created_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) if no row with lower(tag_value) matches. `_norm(value, limit)`: upper-case, non-[A-Z0-9-] runs -> '-', collapse '--', strip '-', truncate to limit (40 for codes, 80 for trackers). downgrade() does not undo the backfill.
- bootstrap.sync_schema() -> str: imports flask_migrate.stamp/upgrade lazily; migrations_dir = current_app.extensions['migrate'].directory; if `_is_legacy_database()` (== tables non-empty AND 'alembic_version' absent AND 'users' present) -> log.warning, `stamp(directory, revision='0001_baseline')`, `upgrade(directory)`, return 'stamped-baseline+upgraded'; else `upgrade(directory)`, return 'upgraded'. An empty DB is not 'legacy' (verified: returns False) so it just upgrades from scratch. Idempotent (second call returns 'upgraded').
- bootstrap.current_revision() -> str|None: None if no alembic_version table, else `SELECT version_num FROM alembic_version` first row. BASELINE_REVISION = '0001_baseline' is a module constant; `_has_alembic_version()` uses sqlalchemy.inspect(db.engine).get_table_names().
- bootstrap.seed_defaults(commit=True) -> dict {'projects': n, 'announcements': n, 'users': n}: (a) if Project.query.count()==0 add 3 projects: slug/title/project_type/status/lead_name = rover/'Rover'/'Rover'/'Active'/'Rover Lead'; arm/'Robotic Arm'/'Arm'/'Prototype'/'Controls Lead'; manufacturing/'Manufacturing'/'Manufacturing'/'In Progress'/'Manufacturing Lead' (gallery_json='[]', timelines as arrows, is_joinable via model default True); (b) if Announcement.query.count()==0 add 'Welcome to ASME @ UIowa' (body 'Spring build season is active. Check project boards and weekly meeting updates.', is_published/show_on_public/show_on_member True, published_at=utcnow); (c) if User.query.count()==0: for each Member ordered by id create User(name, email, username=email local-part or None, password_hash=generate_password_hash(cfg.default_user_password), role='member' -> 'team_leader' if 'lead' in member_class.lower() -> 'admin' if is_admin_member(member), is_active=True, member_id) ; if there are no members add `_bootstrap_admin(cfg)`; elif no User with lower(email)==cfg.default_admin_email add `_bootstrap_admin(cfg)`; (d) always `seed_default_tracks(commit=False)`; (e) db.session.commit() if commit. Verified: first run {'projects':3,'announcements':1,'users':1}, second run all zeros.
- bootstrap._bootstrap_admin(cfg) -> User(name='ASME Admin', email=cfg.default_admin_email, username=local-part or 'admin', password_hash=generate_password_hash(cfg.default_admin_password), role='admin', is_active=True). Config keys: ASME_DEFAULT_ADMIN_EMAIL (default 'admin@uiowa.edu', lower-cased), ASME_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD (default 'ChangeMe123!'), ASME_DEFAULT_USER_PASSWORD (default 'ChangeMe123!'), ASME_ADMIN_EMAILS (comma list, lower-cased frozenset used by is_admin_member).
- auth.session.is_admin_member(member): True if member.email in settings().admin_emails, else True if member_class contains any of 'admin','officer','lead','president','chair'. Consequence in seed_defaults: a legacy Member with member_class 'Team Lead' is first set to 'team_leader' then promoted to 'admin' because 'lead' matches.
- seeds.seed_default_tracks(commit=True): seed_training_modules() (upsert TrainingModule by key, setattr every spec field) then _upsert_track(MEMBER_TRACK) and _upsert_track(CHAPTER_TRACK). _upsert_track: Track by key (create+flush if missing) then overwrite name/audience/description and is_active=True; Phase by (track_id,key) overwrite name/tagline/description/order/est_minutes/grants (grants stored as JSON list in grants_json); Task by (phase_id,key) overwrite name/description/is_required/est_minutes/rule_type/rule_config (JSON in rule_config_json)/cta_route/cta_label/help_url/order. Never deletes phases/tasks removed from the spec (seen_phase_ids/seen_task_ids are collected but unused) and never touches *_states / entitlements rows.
- Seeded Launchpad data (verified counts: 2 tracks, 6 phases, 25 tasks, 3 training modules). Track 'member_onboarding' ('Member Launchpad', audience 'user'): phases signed_up#1 grants ['portal_access','event_checkin'] tasks verify_email(email_domain uiowa.edu), complete_profile(existence major,graduation_year), link_nfc(existence nfc), join_team(existence team); shop_ready#2 grants ['shop_access','print_submit'] tasks shop_safety(training shop_safety min_score 80), first_meeting(count_threshold AttendanceRecord>=1), supervised_checkout(loan_signed_off>=1), machine_training(training machine_basics, optional); contributor#3 grants ['extended_loan_14d','room_booking'] tasks three_meetings(count_threshold AttendanceRecord>=3 window semester), clean_returns(clean_streak 3), ten_hours(hours_threshold 10 semester), first_print(count_threshold PrintRequest status completed, optional); team_lead#4 grants ['role:team_leader','print_approve','checkout_signoff'] tasks nominated(signoff by_role admin), lead_training(training lead_essentials), build_cycle(signoff by_role team_leader). Track 'chapter_setup' ('Chapter setup', audience 'chapter'): phases stand_up#1 (grants []) tasks import_roster(chapter_count User>=20 role_in member,team_leader), define_teams(chapter_count Project>=3 joinable), seed_inventory(chapter_count Item>=40), shop_locations(chapter_count 'ItemLocation'>=5 -> rules.py counts DISTINCT lower(items.location); there is no ItemLocation table), connect_calendar(config_set calendar), provision_kiosk_tag(chapter_count NFCTag>=1); run_semester#2 (grants []) tasks publish_schedule(chapter_count Event>=3 future), open_print_queue(manual note_required), set_loan_periods(manual), safety_coverage(member_phase_ratio shop_ready 0.6). Training modules: shop_safety (required, min_score 80, validity 365d), machine_basics (optional, validity 730d), lead_essentials (required, no expiry).
- Entitlement/subject constants (asme/constants.py): ENT_PORTAL_ACCESS='portal_access', ENT_EVENT_CHECKIN='event_checkin', ENT_SHOP_ACCESS='shop_access', ENT_PRINT_SUBMIT='print_submit', ENT_ROOM_BOOKING='room_booking', ENT_EXTENDED_LOAN='extended_loan_14d', ENT_PRINT_APPROVE='print_approve', ENT_CHECKOUT_SIGNOFF='checkout_signoff'; SUBJECT_USER='user', SUBJECT_CHAPTER='chapter' (values stored in onboarding_tracks.audience and *_states.subject_type).
- Auto-run at boot (asme/__init__.py:86-95): `if cfg.auto_migrate and not cfg.is_testing:` inside app_context -> bootstrap.sync_schema() then bootstrap.seed_defaults(); any Exception is caught, logged via log.exception('automatic schema sync failed; run `python manage.py upgrade`') and the app still starts. Settings.auto_migrate = _bool('ASME_AUTO_MIGRATE', default=(env != 'production')); env from ASME_ENV (default 'development' if FLASK_DEBUG truthy else 'production'; allowed development/production/testing).
- CLI entry points: `python manage.py upgrade` (create_app(auto_migrate=False, outbox_worker_enabled=False) -> sync_schema + seed_defaults), `python manage.py seed`, `python manage.py serve` (upgrade+seed then app.run); Flask CLI (registered in _register_cli): `flask upgrade-db`, `flask seed`, `flask evaluate-launchpad`, `flask reconcile-stock`, `flask run-jobs --loop/--once`; plus Flask-Migrate's `flask db <revision|migrate|upgrade|downgrade|stamp|current|history|check>` group registered by Migrate.init_app. manage.py has NO revision/migrate command.
- Deploy hooks all run `python manage.py upgrade`: Procfile `release:`; render.yaml `startCommand: python manage.py upgrade && gunicorn ...` with ASME_ENV=production and ASME_AUTO_MIGRATE='0'; .ebextensions/03_migrate.config container_commands 01_migrate with leader_only: true. app.py (`app = create_app()`) is the WSGI target; importing it in a non-production env triggers the auto-migrate block.
- Default DB URL 'sqlite:///inventory.db' (ASME_DATABASE_URL); with Flask-SQLAlchemy 3.x a relative sqlite path resolves under app.instance_path (ROOT/instance/, created by create_app). psycopg2-binary is in requirements so PostgreSQL is an intended target.
- db_init.py (dev only): create_app(auto_migrate=False, outbox_worker_enabled=False) -> sync_schema + seed_defaults -> then adds 3 sample Members (+ matching Users with nfc_uid, password 'ChangeMe123!'), 5 Items, 2 Events only if those tables are empty, then runs engine.evaluate_user/evaluate_chapter. Because members are created after seed_defaults, the Member->User conversion branch of seed_defaults is not exercised there.
- tests/conftest.py builds the schema with `_db.create_all()` + `seed_default_tracks()` on sqlite:// with env='testing', auto_migrate=False; migrations are never executed by the test suite. Note create_all yields no server_default on events.kind / projects.is_joinable (Python-side defaults only), whereas migrated DBs have them.

**Extension points**

- Authoring revision 0003 (to fit existing conventions): (1) change the ORM in asme/models/*.py (all models share asme.extensions.db.metadata; export new classes in asme/models/__init__.py __all__); (2) bring the local DB to head first: `python manage.py upgrade`; (3) generate with Flask-Migrate from the repo root using the venv: `set ASME_AUTO_MIGRATE=0` (avoids the import-time auto-migrate/seed in app.py) then `flask --app app db revision --autogenerate --rev-id 0003_<short_word> -m "<message>"`; the file lands at migrations/versions/0003_<short_word>_<slugified-message-truncated-40>.py with revision='0003_<short_word>', down_revision='0002_launchpad', branch_labels=None, depends_on=None; (4) keep the chain linear (one head) because sync_schema()/manage.py upgrade run `upgrade()` to head with no revision argument.
- Inside a new revision: keep every ALTER inside `with op.batch_alter_table('<table>', schema=None) as batch_op:` (render_as_batch=True already renders this; required for SQLite); create indexes with `batch_op.f('ix_<table>_<col>')`; name new constraints explicitly with the convention (uq_<table>_<col>, fk_<table>_<col>_<reftable>, ck_<table>_<name>) so downgrade()/later drops can address them (0002 did exactly this for uq_transactions_idempotency_key and fk_transactions_signed_off_by_user_id_users); when adding a NOT NULL column to a populated table supply server_default (pattern: events.kind sd='meeting', projects.is_joinable sd=sa.true()) or add nullable -> backfill -> alter_column nullable=False.
- Data backfills belong in a module-level helper called at the end of upgrade() using `op.get_bind().execute(sa.text(...))` (pattern: 0002 `_backfill_data()`); write them idempotent, tolerant of empty tables, and portable across SQLite and PostgreSQL (use sa.true()/sa.false() or bound params for booleans, not integer literals).
- Do not modify 0001_baseline or 0002_launchpad or rename revision ids: production DBs are stamped/upgraded on them, and bootstrap.BASELINE_REVISION = '0001_baseline' is hard-coded for the legacy-stamp path.
- Seed data goes in code, not migrations: static defaults in bootstrap.seed_defaults() (guarded by count/presence checks) and Launchpad content in asme/services/onboarding/seeds.py spec dicts (TRAINING_MODULES, MEMBER_TRACK, CHAPTER_TRACK) which are upserted by key on every upgrade/seed; a new rule_type also needs a handler in asme/services/onboarding/rules.py.
- Verification recipe: fresh sqlite file -> create_app(auto_migrate=False) -> bootstrap.sync_schema() -> `alembic.autogenerate.compare_metadata(MigrationContext.configure(conn), db.metadata)` must return []; equivalently `flask --app app db check`. Then run `python manage.py upgrade` against a copy of the production DB (SQLite and PostgreSQL if applicable) and against a legacy DB without alembic_version to exercise the stamp path.
- Alembic env hooks available but unused: env.py `conf_args` can carry compare_server_default / include_object / include_name via `Migrate(... , **kwargs)` or `migrate.init_app(..., compare_server_default=True)`; process_revision_directives is only set when not already supplied in configure_args.
- Entry points that run migrations: Procfile release phase, render.yaml startCommand, .ebextensions/03_migrate.config (leader_only), `flask upgrade-db`, create_app() auto-run (ASME_AUTO_MIGRATE). Any new deploy target should call `python manage.py upgrade` once before serving.

**Risks / known defects**

- 0002 `_backfill_data()` raw SQL assigns integer literals to boolean columns (`UPDATE projects SET is_joinable = 1`, `is_consumable = COALESCE(is_consumable, 0)`, `active = COALESCE(active, 1)`). Valid on SQLite; PostgreSQL rejects integer->boolean in assignment/COALESCE at plan time even for zero rows, so `upgrade` to 0002 is expected to fail on a Postgres database (psycopg2-binary is a declared dependency). Not verified against a live Postgres.
- Legacy stamping is heuristic: `_is_legacy_database()` stamps '0001_baseline' whenever tables exist, alembic_version is absent and a `users` table exists, without checking the legacy schema actually equals 0001 (the old app did runtime ALTER TABLEs, so older DBs may lack item_tags/print_requests/etc. or already have 0002 columns). A mismatch surfaces as a failing 0002 (e.g. duplicate column on add_column, or INSERT INTO missing item_tags) with the stamp already written.
- create_app() swallows migration failures (`log.exception(...)`) when ASME_AUTO_MIGRATE is on, so a dev/staging app can boot on a half-migrated or unmigrated schema and fail later at query time.
- The test suite never executes the migrations (conftest uses db.create_all()), so model/migration drift or a broken upgrade()/downgrade() is only caught by running manage.py upgrade manually. Today the drift is zero (verified), but nothing enforces it.
- seed_defaults() re-creates the bootstrap admin (default password 'ChangeMe123!') on every upgrade/seed whenever no user has email == cfg.default_admin_email; renaming or deleting that account, or changing ASME_DEFAULT_ADMIN_EMAIL, silently provisions a new admin with the configured (possibly default) password. Settings.warnings() only logs a warning in production.
- Legacy Member->User conversion in seed_defaults promotes any member whose member_class contains 'lead' (also 'officer','chair','president','admin') to role 'admin' via is_admin_member(), not 'team_leader'.
- seed_default_tracks() never deletes phases/tasks removed from the spec (seen_*_ids unused) and never deactivates removed tracks, so removing a task from seeds.py leaves the stale row (and its states) in production until a hand-written migration cleans it up.
- compare_server_default is not enabled, and models declare no server_default for events.kind / projects.is_joinable, so `create_all()` schemas (tests) and migrated schemas differ in DB-level defaults; autogenerate will not report this.
- Both 0001 and 0002 downgrade() paths are destructive (0001 drops all tables; 0002 drops the Launchpad/outbox/ledger tables and the added transactions/users/projects/events columns) and 0002's downgrade re-applies NOT NULL to transactions.member_id, which fails if any row has member_id NULL (all new loans do).
- app.py runs create_app() at import; with ASME_ENV unset and FLASK_DEBUG unset env defaults to 'production' (auto_migrate off), but any development shell running `flask --app app db ...` will first auto-migrate and seed the configured DB unless ASME_AUTO_MIGRATE=0 is set.
- Chapter seed task shop_locations references rule_config model 'ItemLocation' which has no table or ORM class; rules.py special-cases it as COUNT(DISTINCT lower(items.location)). Any refactor of item locations into a real table must update both seeds.py and rules.py.

## auth

Session-cookie (Flask signed cookie, no server store) authentication with a canonical `users` table and a legacy `members` compatibility table. Login accepts email OR username (case-insensitive, email match wins) and verifies with `werkzeug.security.check_password_hash`; new hashes use `generate_password_hash` with werkzeug's default method (unpinned) except bulk credential regeneration which uses `ASME_BULK_PASSWORD_HASH_METHOD` (default `pbkdf2:sha256:120000`). `current_auth_user()` (memoized on `g`) is the single loader: it validates `session["auth_user_id"]`, a per-process `app_boot_token`, `User.is_active`, and a role-dependent idle timeout (admin 30 min / member 240 min, refreshed on every non-static request by a global `before_request` hook). Three gates live in `asme/auth/session.py`: `require_login` (plain decorator), `require_role(min_role)` (factory over `ROLE_ORDER = {guest:0, member:1, team_leader:2, admin:3}`), and `require_entitlement(key)` (factory; admins bypass; shadow mode unless `ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE=1`). Denials content-negotiate via `_wants_json()` into `{ok:false, code, error}` JSON (401/403) or flash+redirect. A separate `/admin-login` route accepts a shared env-configured admin (plaintext compare against `ASME_DEFAULT_ADMIN_*`/`ASME_SHARED_ADMIN_*`/`ASME_ADMIN_*`) and upserts that user as admin. Password reset uses `password_reset_tokens` rows (`secrets.token_urlsafe(32)`, 2h expiry, single-use) but the link is flashed back to the browser rather than emailed. Login rate limiting is an in-memory per-process fixed-window limiter keyed by `(client_ip, identifier)` applied only to `/login` and `/admin-login`. No CSRF protection exists.

**Key facts**

- DECORATOR NAMES: the real names are `require_login`, `require_role(min_role)`, `require_entitlement(key)` (asme/auth/session.py). There is NO `login_required` / `role_required` anywhere; `asme/auth/__init__.py` re-exports: current_auth_user, current_user_member, get_active_member, is_admin_member, normalize_role, rate_limiter, require_entitlement, require_login, require_role, role_allows, sign_in_user, sign_out_user.
- ROLE_ORDER (asme/constants.py:5) = {"guest": 0, "member": 1, "team_leader": 2, "admin": 3}; ASSIGNABLE_ROLES = ("member", "team_leader", "admin"). `normalize_role(role)` strips/lowers and returns the value if it is a ROLE_ORDER key, else "member" (unknown/None roles fail OPEN to member level; "guest" is valid but nothing ever assigns it). `role_allows(user_role, required_role)` = ROLE_ORDER[normalize(user_role)] >= ROLE_ORDER[normalize(required_role)].
- LOGIN IDENTIFIER: `identity.find_user_by_login_identifier(identifier)` lowercases/strips; first `User.query.filter(func.lower(User.email) == cleaned)`, then `func.lower(func.coalesce(User.username, "")) == cleaned`. Email match takes precedence over username. `identity.authenticate(identifier, password)` returns None when user missing, `not user.is_active`, or `check_password_hash(user.password_hash, password or "")` fails; returns the User otherwise (no distinction between reasons).
- PASSWORD HASHING: `werkzeug.security.generate_password_hash(password)` with the library default method (not pinned in code; depends on installed werkzeug) for signup, reset, change_password, admin_create_*, admin_reset_password, ensure_shared_admin_user, bootstrap seeds. Bulk regeneration (`build_fresh_member_credentials`) uses `hash_bulk_password` -> `generate_password_hash(pw, method=settings().bulk_password_hash_method or "pbkdf2:sha256:120000")`, falling back to the default method on exception. Column `users.password_hash` is String(260) NOT NULL. Minimum password length is 8 everywhere; no other complexity rules.
- SESSION STORAGE: Flask default signed-cookie session. `sign_in_user(user)` does `session.clear()`, `session.permanent = False`, then sets: `auth_user_id` (int user.id), `auth_user_role` (normalize_role(user.role); WRITTEN BUT NEVER READ anywhere), `auth_boot_token` (= settings().app_boot_token), `auth_login_ts` (int epoch; WRITTEN BUT NEVER READ - no absolute lifetime enforced), `auth_last_seen_ts` (int epoch), and `active_member_id` (linked Member.id via current_user_member, else key popped). It also caches the user on `g`, sets `user.last_login_at = datetime.utcnow()` and commits. `sign_out_user()` = `session.clear()` + cache None.
- CURRENT USER LOADING: `current_auth_user()` memoizes per request on `g._current_auth_user_resolved` / `g._current_auth_user_cached`. Order: (1) no `session["auth_user_id"]` -> None; (2) `session["auth_boot_token"].strip() != cfg.app_boot_token` -> sign_out, None; (3) `db.session.get(User, int(user_id))` failure/None or `not user.is_active` -> sign_out, None; (4) idle: `idle_minutes = cfg.admin_session_idle_minutes if role_allows(user.role, "admin") else cfg.member_session_idle_minutes`; if idle_minutes > 0 and last_seen set and now - last_seen > idle*60 -> sign_out, None; (5) refresh `auth_boot_token` and `auth_last_seen_ts`, cache, return user. Role for authorization is always read from the DB row, never from the session.
- GLOBAL HOOKS (asme/__init__.py `_register_request_hooks`): `before_request _enforce_auth_session_guardrails` calls `current_auth_user()` for every request except paths starting with `/static/` or `/arm-sim`, or exactly `/healthz` - so idle/boot-token enforcement and last_seen refresh happen on ALL routes including public pages and API polling. `after_request _no_cache_for_authenticated`: if `session.get("auth_user_id")` sets Cache-Control `no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, max-age=0, private`, Pragma no-cache, Expires 0, and appends `Cookie` to Vary; always setdefault X-Content-Type-Options=nosniff, X-Frame-Options=SAMEORIGIN, Referrer-Policy=strict-origin-when-cross-origin.
- COOKIE FLAGS (app.config in create_app): SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY=True; SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=cfg.session_cookie_secure (env `ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE`, default True ONLY if env var `RENDER` is set, otherwise False); SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE=cfg.session_cookie_samesite (env `ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE` lax|strict|none -> "Lax"/"Strict"/"None", default "Lax"); SESSION_PERMANENT=False; SECRET_KEY=`ASME_SECRET_KEY` (default "asme-dev-secret"). `Settings.validate()` refuses production startup if secret_key in {"", "asme-dev-secret", "change-this-secret"} or if SameSite=None without secure. `Settings.warnings()` logs if production and cookie not secure.
- IDLE TIMEOUTS: `member_session_idle_minutes` env `ASME_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES` default 240 (min 0); `admin_session_idle_minutes` env `ASME_ADMIN_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES` default 30 (min 0). 0 disables the check for that class. Admin threshold applies to any user whose role >= admin.
- BOOT TOKEN: `app_boot_token = _str("ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN") or uuid4().hex` evaluated once per process in Settings.from_env. Any session whose `auth_boot_token` differs is force-logged-out. `ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN` is not set in .env.example, .ebextensions, or Procfile; Procfile runs `gunicorn --workers 2`, so each worker has a different token unless the env var is pinned.
- DENIAL RESPONSES: `_wants_json()` = request.path.startswith("/api/") OR request.is_json OR ("application/json" in Accept and "text/html" not in Accept). `_deny_login()`: JSON -> `{"ok": false, "code": "login_required", "error": "Login required."}` HTTP 401; HTML -> flash("Please log in to continue.", "error") + 302 to `url_for("auth.login_page", next=request.path)`. `_deny_role()`: JSON -> `{"ok": false, "code": "forbidden", "error": "You do not have permission to do that."}` HTTP 403; HTML -> flash("You do not have permission to view that page.", "error") + 302 to `portal.portal_router` (/portal).
- require_login(view_func): plain decorator (`@wraps`), if not current_auth_user() -> _deny_login(). require_role(min_role): factory; not logged in -> _deny_login(); `not role_allows(user.role, min_role)` -> _deny_role(). require_entitlement(key): factory; not logged in -> _deny_login(); `entitlements.has_entitlement(user, key)` True -> proceed; else if `settings().onboarding_enforce` -> `entitlement_denied_response(key, user)`; else (shadow) append key to list `g.shadow_entitlement_blocks`, `log.info("shadow-block entitlement=%s user_id=%s path=%s")` on logger `asme.auth`, and proceed. Observed stacking order in blueprints: `@bp.route` outermost, then `@require_login`/`@require_role(...)`, then `@require_entitlement(...)` innermost.
- entitlement_denied_response(key, user): `phase = entitlements.phase_that_grants(key)` (first `Phase` ordered by order asc, id asc whose `grants` JSON list contains key). Payload `{"ok": false, "code": "entitlement_required", "entitlement": key, "phase": phase.key|null, "phase_name": phase.name|null, "error": "You need '<key>' to do that. Complete the Launchpad phase that unlocks it."}` -> JSON 403; HTML -> flash (phase-specific or generic) + 302 to `portal.member_launchpad`. `has_entitlement(user, key)`: False if no user; True if role_allows(user.role, "admin"); else any active `Entitlement` row (revoked_at IS NULL and not expired) with that key. `user_entitlements(user)` returns the set of active keys, plus ALL_ENTITLEMENTS for admins.
- ENTITLEMENT KEYS (asme/constants.py): ENT_PORTAL_ACCESS="portal_access", ENT_EVENT_CHECKIN="event_checkin", ENT_SHOP_ACCESS="shop_access", ENT_PRINT_SUBMIT="print_submit", ENT_ROOM_BOOKING="room_booking", ENT_EXTENDED_LOAN="extended_loan_14d", ENT_PRINT_APPROVE="print_approve", ENT_CHECKOUT_SIGNOFF="checkout_signoff"; ALL_ENTITLEMENTS is the 8-tuple. `onboarding_enforce` env `ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE` default False (shadow mode). Also exposed to templates as `onboarding_enforced` via context_processor.
- RATE LIMITER: class `LoginRateLimiter(window_seconds: int, max_attempts: int)` in asme/auth/rate_limit.py; in-memory `dict[str, dict]` guarded by `threading.Lock`; per-process only. `key(client_ip, identifier)` = f"{client_ip}|{identifier.strip().lower()}". `record_failure(ip, id)`: starts `{count:1, reset_at: now+window}` on first failure or after expiry, else increments (fixed window anchored at first failure, not sliding). `clear(ip, id)` removes the row. `is_limited(ip, id) -> (bool, retry_after_seconds)`; limited when count >= max_attempts. `reset_all()` clears. `_sweep` drops expired rows on every call. Registered in create_app as `app.extensions["asme_login_rate_limiter"] = LoginRateLimiter(cfg.login_rate_window_seconds, cfg.login_rate_max_attempts)` with env `ASME_LOGIN_RATE_WINDOW_SECONDS` default 900 (min 1) and `ASME_LOGIN_RATE_MAX_ATTEMPTS` default 8 (min 1). Accessor `rate_limiter()` returns `current_app.extensions["asme_login_rate_limiter"]`. Tests mutate `.max_attempts` directly.
- CLIENT IP: `asme.utils.http.request_client_ip()` returns first comma-separated entry of `X-Forwarded-For` if present (trusted unconditionally), else `request.remote_addr or "unknown"`, truncated to 120 chars. Only `/login` and `/admin-login` consult the limiter; on blocked: flash `Too many login attempts. Try again in about {retry_after} seconds.` and re-render (HTTP 200). Failure recorded on bad credentials, and on /admin-login also when a valid non-admin logs in. Cleared on success.
- /login POST: identifier = form `identifier` or form `email`, stripped+lowercased; password = form `password`. Bad creds -> flash "Invalid email or password." and re-render login (200). Success -> `sign_in_user(user)` then 302 to `safe_next_url(next)` or `portal.portal_router` (/portal). `safe_next_url(raw)` accepts only values starting with "/" and not "//"; `next` is read from query arg or form field `next`. Template field names: login.html -> identifier, password, next; admin_login.html -> identifier (prefilled with shared_admin_email), password, next.
- /admin-login POST: identifier from form `identifier`/`email` lowercased; if blank AND cfg.shared_admin_email set, identifier := shared_admin_email. If already logged in as admin (GET or POST) -> 302 admin.dashboard. Shared-admin branch: if `cfg.shared_admin_email and cfg.shared_admin_password and identifier == cfg.shared_admin_email and password == cfg.shared_admin_password` (PLAINTEXT equality against env) -> `identity.ensure_shared_admin_user(email, password)` -> sign in -> 302 next or admin.dashboard. Otherwise `identity.authenticate`; None -> record_failure + flash "Invalid admin credentials."; valid but `not role_allows(user.role, "admin")` -> record_failure + flash "This account is not an admin account."; else clear + sign_in + 302 next or admin.dashboard. Template context includes `shared_admin_email`.
- SHARED ADMIN CONFIG (asme/config.py): `shared_admin_email` = first non-empty of env `ASME_DEFAULT_ADMIN_EMAIL`, `ASME_SHARED_ADMIN_EMAIL`, `ASME_ADMIN_EMAIL` (lowercased; empty string if none set). `shared_admin_password` = first non-empty of `ASME_DEFAULT_ADMIN_PASSWORD`, `ASME_SHARED_ADMIN_PASSWORD`, `ASME_ADMIN_PASSWORD` (empty if none). Separately `default_admin_email` defaults to "admin@uiowa.edu" and `default_admin_password` defaults to "ChangeMe123!" - these feed `bootstrap.seed_defaults()` (creates/ensures an admin User with that email when the users table is empty or lacks that email) but NOT the /admin-login shortcut unless the env vars are actually set. `admin_emails` = frozenset from comma-separated `ASME_ADMIN_EMAILS` (lowercased), used only by `is_admin_member` (legacy kiosk) and bootstrap role inference.
- identity.ensure_shared_admin_user(email, password): returns None if either blank. Looks up `lower(User.email) == email`; if missing creates User(name="ASME Admin", email, username=local part or "admin", password_hash=generate_password_hash(password), role="admin", is_active=True). If present: forces `role="admin"`, `is_active=True`, REPLACES password_hash if `check_password_hash` fails against the env password, and backfills username via make_unique_username if blank. Commits. No audit record is written.
- /admin GET (auth.admin_home): 302 to admin.dashboard if logged in as admin, else 302 to auth.admin_login_page. /logout accepts GET and POST, calls sign_out_user(), flashes "You have been logged out." (info), 302 to public.public_home with explicit no-store headers. No login check, no CSRF token.
- SIGNUP: /signup POST -> `identity.signup(name, email, password, role)` with role from form select `role` (default "member"). signup(): name strip; email strip+lower; role = normalize_role(role); if role == "admin" -> "member" (team_leader is NOT demoted). Validation: len(name) < 2 -> Validation(field=name); "@" not in email or len < 5 -> Validation(field=email) (EMAIL_RE constant is not used here); len(password) < 8 -> Validation(field=password); existing lower(email) -> Conflict(code="email_taken", 409). Links `member_id` to existing Member with same email. username = make_unique_username(local part of email). Commits, emits `events.USER_CREATED(user_id=...)`. Blueprint catches ServiceError -> `flash_error(exc)` and re-renders signup (200); success flashes and 302 to auth.login_page.
- PASSWORD RESET FLOW: /forgot-password POST: email lower; `User.query.filter(func.lower(User.email) == email).first()`; if user and is_active -> `identity.create_password_reset(user)` and flash `Reset link generated: {external url_for("auth.reset_password_page", token=...)}` (info) - the link is DISPLAYED IN THE RESPONSE, no email is sent; else flash "If this email exists, a reset link has been generated."; always 302 back to /forgot-password. create_password_reset(user, hours=None) -> str: hours = hours or settings().password_reset_hours (dataclass default 2; NOT wired to any env var in from_env); token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32); inserts PasswordResetToken(user_id, token, expires_at=utcnow+hours); commits; returns token.
- /reset-password/<token>: `identity.find_valid_reset(token)` -> `PasswordResetToken.query.filter_by(token=token.strip()).first()`; None if missing, `used_at` not None, `expires_at` None, or `expires_at < utcnow()`. Invalid -> flash "Reset link is invalid or expired." + 302 /forgot-password. POST -> `identity.consume_password_reset(reset_row, password, confirm)`: len<8 -> Validation(field=password); mismatch -> Validation(field=confirm_password); user missing -> NotFound (blueprint redirects to /forgot-password on status 404); else `user.password_hash = generate_password_hash(password)`, `reset_row.used_at = utcnow`, commit, return user. Sibling unused tokens for the same user are NOT invalidated; existing sessions are NOT revoked (cookie sessions; only boot token / is_active / idle can kill them). `admin_invite_link_token(user, actor)` creates a 72-hour token in the same table (audit "create_user_invite_link").
- OTHER CREDENTIAL FLOWS: `change_password(user, current_password, new_password, confirm_password)`: verifies current via check_password_hash (Validation field=current_password), min 8 (field=new_password), match (field=confirm_password), rehash, commit; no audit, no event. `admin_reset_password(user, provided, actor) -> str`: provided stripped; if provided and len < 8 -> Validation(field=new_password); new = provided or f"ASME-{uuid4().hex[:10]}"; rehash; audit "reset_user_password"; commit; returns plaintext. `update_profile(user, name, email, major=None, graduation_year=None, phone=None)`: Validation if no name or no "@"; Conflict(email_taken) if another user has email; truncates name/email to 160, major 120, phone 40; mirrors name/email onto linked Member; commit; emits USER_UPDATED.

| Method | Path | Endpoint | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET, POST | `/signup` | `auth.signup_page` | public; POST -> identity.signup; ServiceError -> flash + re-render 200; success  |
| GET, POST | `/login` | `auth.login_page` | public; LoginRateLimiter keyed (client_ip, identifier); POST fields identifier/e |
| GET, POST | `/admin-login` | `auth.admin_login_page` | public; already-admin -> 302 /portal/admin; rate limited; shared-admin env plain |
| GET | `/admin` | `auth.admin_home` | public redirect: admin -> /portal/admin, else -> /admin-login |
| GET, POST | `/logout` | `auth.logout_page` | public (no auth or CSRF check); session.clear(); 302 public.public_home with no- |
| GET, POST | `/forgot-password` | `auth.forgot_password_page` | public; not rate limited; POST field email; active user -> create_password_reset |
| GET, POST | `/reset-password/<token>` | `auth.reset_password_page` | public; token validated by identity.find_valid_reset (unused, unexpired); invali |
| GET | `/portal` | `portal.portal_router` | @require_login; admin -> 302 /portal/admin else 302 /portal/member (post-login l |

**Extension points**

- New gate decorators: add to asme/auth/session.py following require_role's factory shape (resolve `current_auth_user()`, return `_deny_login()` / `_deny_role()` / a custom JSON-or-redirect response via `_wants_json()`), then re-export in asme/auth/__init__.py. Keep `@wraps` and the outer/inner ordering convention (route -> login/role -> entitlement).
- New entitlement: add `ENT_*` constant and append to `ALL_ENTITLEMENTS` in asme/constants.py, add the key to a Phase's `grants` in asme/services/onboarding/seeds.py, then guard routes with `@require_entitlement(ENT_*)`. Admins bypass automatically; shadow mode logs but allows until ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE=1.
- New role: extend `ROLE_ORDER` and `ASSIGNABLE_ROLES` in asme/constants.py; also update the hard-coded `roles` list in `admin_dashboard_context()` (asme/blueprints/_context.py:273) and the `User.role.in_([...])` filters in identity.reset_members / build_fresh_member_credentials / _context.active_members_count, and bootstrap role inference.
- New config knob: add a field to the `Settings` dataclass AND a mapping in `Settings.from_env` (password_reset_hours demonstrates the trap of doing only the former). Read via `settings()`; tests override through `create_app(**overrides)`.
- Rate limiter replacement (e.g. Redis-backed for multi-worker): implement `is_limited(ip, id) -> (bool, int)`, `record_failure(ip, id)`, `clear(ip, id)`, `reset_all()` and assign to `app.extensions["asme_login_rate_limiter"]` in create_app; `rate_limiter()` and both login routes need no change. To cover /forgot-password or API logins, call `rate_limiter()` there too.
- Sending reset emails instead of flashing the link: change the POST branch of `auth.forgot_password_page` (asme/blueprints/auth.py:139-141); an outbox job system exists (asme/jobs, asme/services/notifications.py - not read here).
- Session policy: absolute lifetime can be enforced inside `current_auth_user()` using the already-stored `auth_login_ts`; server-side revocation would need a new column/table checked there (the function is the single choke point and runs on every non-static request).
- Content negotiation for new API blueprints: anything mounted under `/api/` automatically gets JSON denials from `_wants_json()`; other paths need `Accept: application/json` or a JSON body.
- Post-login redirect target: `safe_next_url` in asme/utils/http.py (relative-only). `next` is carried as a hidden form field and query arg on /login and /admin-login.
- Password hashing policy: `hash_bulk_password` already reads `settings().bulk_password_hash_method`; pinning the interactive method would mean passing `method=` in the ~8 `generate_password_hash` call sites in identity.py and bootstrap.py (check_password_hash is method-agnostic so existing hashes keep working).

**Risks / known defects**

- Account takeover via /forgot-password: the reset URL is flashed into the HTML response for any active account's email, so anyone who knows a user's email can reset their password without access to the mailbox (asme/blueprints/auth.py:139-141). Also not rate limited.
- No CSRF protection anywhere (no Flask-WTF/CSRFProtect, no tokens in templates). All state-changing HTML POSTs (login, logout, signup, reset, portal/admin forms) rely solely on SameSite=Lax. /logout also accepts GET (link prefetch / forced logout).
- Multi-worker session instability: `app_boot_token` defaults to `uuid4().hex` per process and `ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN` is not set in .env.example, .ebextensions, or Procfile (which runs `gunicorn --workers 2`). Requests alternating between workers will fail the boot-token check and be logged out; every restart/deploy also logs everyone out. Pin ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN in the EB environment.
- Rate limiter is per-process, in-memory, fixed-window, and keyed by `X-Forwarded-For` first hop which is trusted unconditionally - trivially bypassed by rotating the header or the identifier casing is normalized but the IP is not. With 2 gunicorn workers the effective attempt budget is roughly doubled.
- Shared admin login compares the env password in plaintext (`password == cfg.shared_admin_password`) and `ensure_shared_admin_user` force-promotes whatever existing User has that email to admin, reactivates it, and overwrites its password hash to the env value. Because `ASME_DEFAULT_ADMIN_EMAIL` is also the first source for `shared_admin_email`, setting the bootstrap admin env vars silently enables this shortcut. No audit row is written.
- Self-service privilege: `identity.signup` demotes role 'admin' to 'member' but accepts 'team_leader' from the public signup form (`<select name="role">`), granting team_leader gates (room booking, print approve routes, api_v1 team_leader endpoints) to anyone.
- `normalize_role` maps unknown/blank roles to 'member' (fail-open to member level) rather than 'guest'; a corrupted or future role string still passes `@require_role("member")`.
- Cookie Secure flag defaults to False unless the `RENDER` env var exists; on AWS Elastic Beanstalk `ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=1` must be set explicitly or the session cookie is sent over HTTP (only a startup warning is logged in production).
- Password reset tokens: sibling unused tokens stay valid after one is consumed; tokens are not revoked on change_password/admin_reset_password; the table is never pruned; `password_reset_hours` cannot be tuned via env. Reset/change does not invalidate existing sessions (no server-side session state).
- User enumeration: /forgot-password returns different flash text for existing-active vs other emails; /signup Conflict says 'An account already exists for that email.'; /admin-login distinguishes 'Invalid admin credentials.' from 'This account is not an admin account.'
- Read-path writes: `current_user_member()` commits an auto-link of users.member_id during `sign_in_user`; `current_auth_user()` mutates the session on every request (fine for cookie sessions but means every response carries a Set-Cookie).
- Hash method for interactive passwords is werkzeug's default and unpinned; upgrading/downgrading werkzeug changes the algorithm for new hashes (existing hashes still verify). Bulk export writes plaintext passwords derived from names plus 5 digits (`password_from_name`) - low entropy by design for handout sheets.
- `users.phone` exists on the model but not in the 0001 baseline migration DDL read here; confirm migration 0002 adds it before relying on it (only `graduation_year`, `major`, etc. were verified).
- The every-request `before_request` guard calls `current_auth_user()` (a DB hit) on all public pages for logged-in users and refreshes last_seen on any request, so background API polling keeps sessions alive indefinitely; idle timeout is not 'user inactivity'.
- Legacy `is_admin_member` grants kiosk admin by substring match on `member_class` ('lead', 'chair', etc.) - a roster class like 'Cheerleader' or 'Lead Guitarist' becomes kiosk-admin. Only affects legacy_ops/api_legacy paths.

## services-core

The services-core layer is a thin, convention-driven domain layer under asme/services plus dependency-free helpers under asme/utils. Errors: a single ServiceError(Exception) base carrying message/code/status/extra with four subclasses (NotFound 404, Conflict 409, Forbidden 403, Validation 400 with optional field); JSON blueprints (api_v1, api_legacy) translate via @bp.errorhandler(ServiceError) -> jsonify(exc.to_dict()), exc.status producing {"ok": false, "code", "error", ...extra}; HTML blueprints catch and flash(exc.message, "error") (helper: asme/blueprints/_helpers.py flash_error / on_service_error). Audit: asme/services/audit.record(action, details="", actor=None) -> AuditLog adds a row to the audit_logs table (admin_user_id FK users.id nullable, action String(160) NOT NULL indexed, details Text, ip_address String(120), created_at DateTime NOT NULL indexed default utcnow) WITHOUT committing; actor defaults to current_auth_user() when in a request context; audit.recent(limit=150) lists newest first. Serializers: pure dict builders for Member, User(+entitlements), Item, Transaction/Loan (alias serialize_transaction), open checkout, AttendanceScan, PrintJob (embeds url_for legacy_ops.open_print_job/download_print_job), PrintRequest(+runs), Event(+CalendarSync), AttendanceRecord; all timestamps via iso_or_none. Notifications: one synchronous SMTP path (send_meeting_cancel_confirmation_email returns an error string or None, never raises) and one durable path queue_email -> outbox enqueue("mail.send") handled by asme/jobs/handlers.mail_send via asme/integrations/mail.send_email (STARTTLS, smtp_host/port/user/pass from Settings; ASME_SMTP_* env). queue_email currently has no callers. Roster: parse_roster_pdf_entries(pdf_bytes) (pypdf, optional import) -> list of {name, first_name, last_name, email}; import_roster_entries(entries, default_role="member", member_class="Member", reset_existing_passwords=True, actor=None) upserts Member + User per entry (idempotent on email then name), never assigns admin role, never downgrades admin/team_leader, generates username/password via identity helpers, placeholder emails @asme.local, flushes but DOES NOT commit and does NOT audit; caller must commit then call emit_import_events(result) which emits USER_CREATED per new user id and CHAPTER_CHANGED(reason="roster_import"). credentials_pdf_bytes(rows) uses reportlab (lazy import); save_roster_credentials_csv has no callers. Content: projects/memberships/work logs/announcements/contact inbox; every mutating function commits itself (db.session.commit inside the service) and admin-facing ones call audit.record before the commit; events emitted after commit (CHAPTER_CHANGED, TEAM_JOINED, USER_UPDATED, HOURS_LOGGED). Utils: http (api_ok/api_error JSON envelopes, idempotency_key from header/JSON/form, safe_next_url open-redirect guard, redirect_to_next, request_client_ip honoring X-Forwarded-For, value_from_request JSON-or-form), parsing (lenient int/float/bool/date/time/datetime/json-list parsers that return defaults instead of raising, iso_or_none, academic_year_key rolling on 1 Aug, semester_start), text (clean_tag_value, normalize_text_key NFKD->ascii [a-z0-9], split_name_parts, slugify max 150, csv_stream_from_rows, truncate). Commit convention is mixed: content.py commits internally; roster.py never commits; audit.record never commits; outbox enqueue never commits; notifications sync path touches no DB.

**Key facts**

- asme/services/__init__.py is a docstring-only module: 'Domain services. Blueprints call these; nothing else touches db.session.' It exports nothing; submodules are imported explicitly (from asme.services import audit, content, roster, ...).
- ERRORS (asme/services/errors.py): class ServiceError(Exception).__init__(self, message: str, code: str = 'bad_request', status: int = 400, **extra) sets .message, .code, .status, .extra (dict). to_dict() -> {'ok': False, 'code': code, 'error': message} updated with extra keys.
- ERRORS subclasses and HTTP mapping: NotFound(message='Not found.', code='not_found', **extra) -> 404; Conflict(message='Conflict.', code='conflict', **extra) -> 409; Forbidden(message='Not allowed.', code='forbidden', **extra) -> 403; Validation(message, code='validation', field=None, **extra) -> 400, and if field is truthy it is added to extra as extra['field']=field (so it appears in to_dict()). Subclasses do not accept a status kwarg; status is fixed per class.
- ERRORS translation: api_v1 (asme/blueprints/api_v1.py:40) and api_legacy (asme/blueprints/api_legacy.py:36) both register @bp.errorhandler(ServiceError) returning jsonify(exc.to_dict()), exc.status. api_v1 also registers @bp.errorhandler(404) -> {'ok': False, 'code': 'not_found', 'error': 'Not found.'}, 404. HTML blueprints (admin, auth, portal, public, legacy_ops) wrap calls in try/except ServiceError and flash(exc.message, 'error'); asme/blueprints/_helpers.py provides flash_error(exc) (flashes exc.message as 'error' plus each string in exc.extra.get('errors') as 'info') and decorator on_service_error(default_endpoint, **values) which catches ServiceError, flashes, and redirect_to_next(default_endpoint, **values).
- ERRORS: auth-layer denials in asme/auth/session.py do NOT use ServiceError; they return jsonify directly: login required -> {'ok': False, 'code': 'login_required', 'error': 'Login required.'} 401; role denial -> {'ok': False, 'code': 'forbidden', 'error': 'You do not have permission to do that.'} 403; entitlement denial -> {'ok': False, 'code': 'entitlement_required', 'entitlement': key, 'phase': ..., 'phase_name': ..., 'error': ...} 403. JSON vs HTML decided by _wants_json(): path startswith '/api/' OR request.is_json OR Accept contains application/json without text/html.
- AUDIT API (asme/services/audit.py): record(action: str, details: str = '', actor=None) -> AuditLog. If actor is None and has_request_context(), actor = asme.auth.session.current_auth_user() (lazy import). ip_address = (request.remote_addr or '')[:120] or None when in a request context (NOTE: uses remote_addr, not X-Forwarded-For; differs from utils.http.request_client_ip). action = (action or '').strip()[:160] or 'action'; details = (details or '').strip()[:4000] or None. Does db.session.add(log) and returns the row; NEVER commits. recent(limit: int = 150) -> AuditLog.query.order_by(created_at desc, id desc).limit(limit).all().
- AUDIT TABLE audit_logs (asme/models/audit.py class AuditLog): id Integer PK; admin_user_id Integer FK users.id NULLABLE (no index); action String(160) NOT NULL index=True; details Text NULLABLE; ip_address String(120) NULLABLE; created_at DateTime NOT NULL default=datetime.utcnow index=True. relationship admin_user -> User. Constraint names follow asme/extensions.py NAMING_CONVENTION (fk_audit_logs_admin_user_id_users, ix_audit_logs_action, etc.).
- AUDIT usage convention: services call audit.record(...) inside the same unit of work right before their own db.session.commit() (content.py: save_project, update_announcement, delete_announcement, update_contact_status). Blueprints that own the transaction (admin.import_roster, admin.export_fresh_credentials, admin.export_fresh_credentials_pdf, api_v1.roster_import) call audit.record then db.session.commit() themselves. Audit action strings seen in this subsystem: 'save_project', 'update_announcement', 'delete_announcement', 'update_contact_status', 'bulk_roster_import', 'export_fresh_member_credentials', 'export_fresh_member_credentials_pdf'. Other services (identity, inventory, fabrication, attendance, scheduling, onboarding/engine, onboarding/entitlements) also call audit.record (49 total references). audit.recent(150) is consumed only by asme/blueprints/_context.py:261 as context key 'audit_logs'.
- SERIALIZERS (asme/services/serializers.py) - all return plain dicts; serialize_member and serialize_user return None for falsy input, the others assume a row. Import site: from asme.auth.session import normalize_role; from asme.utils import iso_or_none. Used only by api_v1.py and api_legacy.py.
- serialize_member(member) -> {id, name, email, member_class, nfc_tag, created_at(iso)}. Model: members table (Member): name String(120) NN, email String(160) NN UNIQUE, member_class String(80) NN, nfc_tag String(120) NULL UNIQUE, created_at DateTime NN.
- serialize_user(user, entitlements=None) -> {id, name, email, username ('' if None), role (normalize_role: lowercased, must be in ROLE_ORDER {guest,member,team_leader,admin} else 'member'), is_active (bool), member_id, major, graduation_year, nfc_linked (bool(user.nfc_uid)), created_at, last_login_at}; if entitlements is not None adds 'entitlements': sorted(entitlements). Does NOT expose phone, exec_title, exec_message, headshot_url, password_hash. Model users: name String(160) NN; email String(160) NN UNIQUE idx; username String(80) NULL UNIQUE idx; password_hash String(260) NN; role String(30) NN default 'member'; is_active Boolean NN default True; nfc_uid String(160) NULL UNIQUE idx; major String(120); graduation_year Integer; phone String(40); exec_title String(160); exec_message String(500); headshot_url String(500); member_id Integer FK members.id NULL UNIQUE; created_at DateTime NN; last_login_at DateTime NULL.
- serialize_item(item) -> {id, name, description, category, location, item_code, item_type, active(bool), total_qty, available_qty, min_stock_threshold, nfc_tag, low_stock: available_qty <= max(min_stock_threshold or 0, 2), out_of_stock: available_qty <= 0, created_at}. Note the hard floor of 2 in low_stock. Not exposed: item_condition, notes, photo_url, is_consumable, treasury_tracker_id.
- serialize_loan(tx) (alias serialize_transaction = serialize_loan) -> {id, timestamp, member_id, member_name (tx.borrower_name: user.name else member.name else ''), user_id, item_id, item_name (tx.item.name or ''), action, status, state: 'open' if status == 'OUT' else 'returned', qty, checkout_time, return_time, due_date, overdue (tx.is_overdue property: open and due_date < utcnow().date()), notes, checkout_notes, return_condition, return_notes, return_photo_path, signed_off_by_user_id}. Model: transactions table (Transaction, alias Loan); idempotency_key String(120) NULL UNIQUE is NOT serialized.
- serialize_open_checkout(tx) -> {transaction_id, item_id, item_name, item_category, item_location, qty, checkout_time (checkout_time or timestamp), checkout_notes (checkout_notes or notes), due_date, status}.
- serialize_attendance_scan(scan) -> {id, member_id, member_name, uid (scan.scanned_uid), attendance_date, scanned_at}. Legacy attendance_scans table.
- serialize_print_job(job) -> {id, member_id, member_name, printer_type, file_name, status, notes, submitted_at, started_at, completed_at, open_url: url_for('legacy_ops.open_print_job', job_id=job.id), download_url: url_for('legacy_ops.download_print_job', job_id=job.id)}. Requires the legacy_ops blueprint (Blueprint('legacy_ops', __name__), no url_prefix, always registered by asme/blueprints/__init__.register_blueprints) and a request/app context for url_for. Endpoints exist at legacy_ops.py:476 (download_print_job) and :485 (open_print_job). file_path is NOT serialized.
- serialize_print_request(row) -> {id, user_id, printer_type, file_link, has_file: bool(row.file_path), filament, material, color, infill_percent, priority, deadline, notes, admin_notes, status, reviewed_at, created_at, runs: [{id, status, started_at, completed_at} for run in row.runs]}. row.runs relationship ordered by PrintRun.id. Not exposed: member_id, file_path, reviewed_by_user_id, updated_at, run.error, run.printer_type.
- serialize_event(event, sync=None) -> {id, title, kind, location, status, start_time, end_time, calendar_link: event.calendar_event_link or (sync.link if sync else None), sync_status: sync.status if sync else None}. sync is a CalendarSync row (status values pending/synced/failed/deleted). Not exposed: description, requested_by_user_id, created_by_user_id, google_event_id, google_calendar_id.
- serialize_attendance_record(row) -> {id, event_id, event_title, user_id, method (row.checkin_method), checkin_time}. member_id and tag_uid NOT exposed.
- NOTIFICATIONS (asme/services/notifications.py): send_meeting_cancel_confirmation_email(meeting, confirm_url, reject_url) -> str | None. Reads cfg = settings(); notify_to = cfg.cancel_notify_to or cfg.smtp_user. Returns error strings (never raises): 'SMTP is not configured. Set ASME_SMTP_USER and ASME_SMTP_PASS.' when mail.is_configured(cfg) is False; 'ASME_CANCEL_NOTIFY_TO is not configured.' when notify_to empty; 'Failed to send cancellation email: <exc[:250]>' on any exception from mail.send_email. Subject: 'ASME Meeting Cancellation Request: {team_name} ({room})'. Body is plain text listing Team/Room/Date/Time/Requested by/Notes and the confirm/reject URLs. Uses legacy Meeting model fields team_name, room, meeting_date (Date), start_time/end_time (Time), requester_email, notes. Single caller: legacy_ops.request_meeting_cancel (legacy_ops.py:345), which on a non-None return calls scheduling.legacy_clear_cancel(meeting) and flashes the error.
