- NOTIFICATIONS: queue_email(to, subject, body) -> OutboxJob. Lazy-imports asme.jobs.outbox.enqueue and calls enqueue('mail.send', {'to': to, 'subject': subject, 'body': body}). enqueue adds an OutboxJob row (kind String(60), payload_json Text default '{}', status 'pending', run_at utcnow, max_attempts default 5) to the session WITHOUT committing; the caller's commit makes it durable. Handled by asme/jobs/handlers.py @handler('mail.send') mail_send(payload) -> mail.send_email(settings(), payload['to'], payload.get('subject') or '(no subject)', payload.get('body') or ''). queue_email currently has NO callers anywhere in asme/ or tests/.
- MAIL ADAPTER (asme/integrations/mail.py): is_configured(cfg) = bool(cfg.smtp_user and cfg.smtp_pass). send_email(cfg, to, subject, body) raises MailNotConfigured(RuntimeError) if not configured, ValueError if recipient blank, else builds EmailMessage From=cfg.smtp_user To=recipient, smtplib.SMTP(cfg.smtp_host, cfg.smtp_port, timeout=30) -> starttls -> login -> send_message. Plain text only; no HTML, no attachments, no CC/BCC.
- MAIL CONFIG KEYS (asme/config.py Settings): smtp_host (env ASME_SMTP_HOST, default 'smtp.office365.com'), smtp_port (ASME_SMTP_PORT, default 587, min 1), smtp_user (ASME_SMTP_USER), smtp_pass (ASME_SMTP_PASS), cancel_notify_to (ASME_CANCEL_NOTIFY_TO). Settings.validate() adds 'ASME_SMTP_USER and ASME_SMTP_PASS must be set together.' when only one is set; in production validate() problems abort startup (RuntimeError in create_app). settings() returns current_app.config['SETTINGS'] (frozen dataclass).
- OUTBOX worker: started lazily on first request by _start_worker_once in asme/__init__.py when cfg.outbox_worker_enabled (ASME_OUTBOX_WORKER, default True) and not testing; polls every cfg.outbox_poll_seconds (ASME_OUTBOX_POLL_SECONDS default 15). Retry backoff min(2**attempts*15s, 3600s); after max_attempts status='failed'. Jobs stuck 'running' > 10 min are released. Tests call process_pending() directly.
- ROSTER parse: parse_roster_pdf_entries(pdf_bytes) raises RuntimeError('pypdf is not installed. Add pypdf to requirements and redeploy.') if pypdf import failed at module load (PdfReader = None). Per page extract_text, per whitespace-collapsed line: skips blank lines, lines starting with 'first name last name', and lines containing 'engage general teams project teams'. Finds the LAST token matching constants.EMAIL_RE (stripped of ';,'), lowercases it and removes it; then pops trailing tokens in ROSTER_BOOL_WORDS {'yes','no'}; requires >= 2 remaining tokens; first token = first_name, rest joined = last_name. Dedupes on (normalize_text_key(full_name), email). Returns list of {'name','first_name','last_name','email'} (email may be '').

| Method | Path | Endpoint | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/v1/roster/import` | `api_v1.roster_import (asme/blueprints/api_v1.py:397)` | require_role('admin'); multipart field roster_file (.pdf); Validation 400 on mis |
| POST | `/admin/members/import-roster (bp prefix inferred from endpoint admin.*; verify url_prefix in admin.py)` | `admin.import_roster (asme/blueprints/admin.py:589)` | require_role('admin'); form fields roster_file (.pdf), role, member_class; rollb |
| GET | `/admin/members/import-credentials/<filename>` | `admin.download_roster_credentials (asme/blueprints/admin.py:643)` | require_role('admin'); serves files from roster.roster_credentials_dir(); Path(f |
| POST | `/admin/members/export-credentials` | `admin.export_fresh_credentials (asme/blueprints/admin.py:661)` | require_role('admin'); identity.build_fresh_member_credentials(); audit 'export_ |
| POST | `/admin/members/export-credentials-pdf` | `admin.export_fresh_credentials_pdf (asme/blueprints/admin.py:681)` | require_role('admin'); roster.credentials_pdf_bytes(rows) wrapped in try/except  |
| POST | `/calendar/meeting/<int:meeting_id>/cancel` | `legacy_ops.request_meeting_cancel (asme/blueprints/legacy_ops.py:330)` | _gate('portal.member_calendar') (legacy ops feature gate); scheduling.legacy_req |
| GET | `/print-jobs/<job_id> download + open (exact paths at legacy_ops.py:476 and :485)` | `legacy_ops.download_print_job / legacy_ops.open_print_job` | referenced by serializers.serialize_print_job via url_for; legacy_ops blueprint  |

**Extension points**

- Add a new error type: subclass ServiceError in asme/services/errors.py with a fixed status and code; both JSON blueprints already translate any ServiceError subclass via to_dict()/status, and HTML blueprints flash .message. Put structured extras (e.g. 'field', 'errors': [..]) in **extra so they surface in JSON and in _helpers.flash_error.
- Add an audited privileged action: call asme.services.audit.record('<snake_case_action>', 'k=v k=v', actor=actor) BEFORE the db.session.commit() that persists the change so log and change commit atomically. Do not commit inside record. Actor may be omitted inside a request (defaults to current_auth_user()); pass it explicitly from CLI/jobs.
- Add a JSON shape: add serialize_<model>(row) to asme/services/serializers.py returning a plain dict, using iso_or_none for all date/datetime fields, guarding relationships with `x.rel.name if x.rel else ''/None`; import it explicitly in api_v1.py/api_legacy.py.
- Send email durably: call notifications.queue_email(to, subject, body) inside the same unit of work as the domain write and let that write's commit persist the OutboxJob; the 'mail.send' handler in asme/jobs/handlers.py delivers it with retries. Add new job kinds with @handler('<kind>') in asme/jobs/handlers.py and enqueue('<kind>', payload) from a service.
- Add a notification channel: wrap the transport in asme/integrations/<channel>.py (mirroring mail.py: is_configured(cfg) + send_*(cfg, ...)), add Settings fields + env keys in asme/config.py (and validate() pairing rules), then expose a service function in asme/services/notifications.py.
- Extend roster import: parse_roster_pdf_entries returns plain dicts {name, first_name, last_name, email}; any new source (CSV, XLSX) only needs to produce that list and feed import_roster_entries. Extra per-entry fields (major, graduation_year, phone) would be added inside the user-create/update branches of import_roster_entries. Remember: caller commits and then calls emit_import_events(result).
- Add content-domain behaviour: follow content.py pattern — validate via Validation/Conflict/NotFound, mutate, audit.record for admin actions, db.session.commit(), then events.emit(...) after commit. New event names must be added to asme/events/__init__.py ALL_EVENTS (tests assert on that list).
- Add API helpers: asme/utils/http.py api_ok/api_error are the single JSON envelope; idempotency_key() is the standard way to read client idempotency tokens (transactions.idempotency_key UNIQUE column backs it in inventory).
- Add a lenient parser: asme/utils/parsing.py functions never raise; follow the default-return contract and re-export from asme/utils/__init__.py.

**Risks / known defects**

- Mixed commit ownership: content.py services commit internally, but roster.import_roster_entries and audit.record do not. Calling roster.import_roster_entries without a subsequent commit silently loses the import; calling emit_import_events before the commit lets onboarding subscribers see uncommitted state. Tests (tests/test_roster.py) encode the caller-commits contract.
- import_roster_entries accepts `actor` but never uses it and never writes an audit row; audit is the caller's responsibility (admin.py and api_v1.py do it; a new caller could forget).
- audit.record uses request.remote_addr, not X-Forwarded-For; behind a proxy/load balancer (Elastic Beanstalk per recent commit) every audit row will record the proxy IP. utils.http.request_client_ip already handles X-Forwarded-For but audit does not use it.
- Roster import writes plaintext generated passwords into the HTTP response (CSV download in admin, JSON payload in POST /api/v1/roster/import) and, via save_roster_credentials_csv (currently unused), to disk under instance/roster_credentials. New users get werkzeug's default hash method rather than Settings.bulk_password_hash_method.
- content.save_project sets project.slug = slugify(form slug or title); projects.slug is UNIQUE, so renaming a project to a title whose slug already exists raises sqlalchemy IntegrityError at commit (not a Conflict ServiceError) -> 500 unless the blueprint catches it. Also slugify keeps Unicode alphanumerics, so slugs may be non-ASCII.
- update_announcement treats a missing form key as '1' (True) for is_published/show_on_public/show_on_member; unchecked HTML checkboxes are omitted from form posts, so a checkbox-style form cannot turn these flags off unless the template posts an explicit '0'. published_at is re-stamped to utcnow on every save while published.
- serialize_item.low_stock uses max(min_stock_threshold or 0, 2): items with threshold 0 or 1 are still reported low_stock at available_qty <= 2. Changing this changes API semantics for kiosk clients.
- serialize_print_job calls url_for('legacy_ops.open_print_job'/'download_print_job') — it will raise BuildError if the legacy_ops blueprint is ever conditionally unregistered, and requires an application/request context.
- send_meeting_cancel_confirmation_email performs a blocking SMTP round trip (30s timeout) inside the request; the durable queue_email path exists but nothing uses it yet. Any exception is swallowed into an error string (no logging).
- create_contact_message performs no email-format validation and does not validate `kind` against CONTACT_KINDS; update_contact_status silently coerces unknown statuses to 'new' rather than raising Validation.
- parse_positive_int(default=1) and parse_int(default=1) return 1 (not 0/None) for garbage input; callers that use them for optional ids must pass default=0 explicitly (content.save_project does). parse_float accepts 'inf'/'nan' strings (float('inf') succeeds); log_hours' range check rejects inf but nan passes `hours <= 0 or hours > 24` as False -> a NaN WorkLog could be stored if a client posts 'nan'.
- safe_next_url only checks the '/' and '//' prefixes; it does not reject backslash variants ('///evil.com') which some browsers normalise to protocol-relative URLs.
- There is no app-level rollback on unhandled exceptions; teardown_appcontext only calls db.session.remove(). A service that raises after db.session.add but before commit leaves nothing committed (safe), but a blueprint that catches the exception and continues in the same request may commit partial state.
- pypdf and reportlab are optional/lazy imports; missing pypdf surfaces as RuntimeError from parse_roster_pdf_entries (admin catches generic Exception; api_v1 does not — it would 500), missing reportlab is caught only in admin.export_fresh_credentials_pdf.
- events.emit swallows subscriber exceptions; a bug in the onboarding engine will not surface in the request, only in logs ('asme.events').

## services-domain (asme/services/inventory.py, fabrication.py, scheduling.py, attendance.py)

Four Flask-SQLAlchemy service modules own all writes for inventory loans/stock, 3D-print requests, room scheduling, and attendance. Blueprints call them; each public write function validates, mutates ORM rows, calls db.session.commit() itself, and then (after commit) fires an in-process domain event via asme.events.emit(). Errors are subclasses of asme.services.errors.ServiceError (Validation=400, NotFound=404, Conflict=409, Forbidden=403) carrying a machine `code` and optional extras; JSON blueprints serialize them as {"ok": false, "code", "error", ...extra} with exc.status, HTML blueprints flash exc.message and redirect. Inventory: `items.available_qty` is a cached atomic counter (never derived at read time); every mutation of available_qty/total_qty in inventory.py also inserts a `stock_ledger` row in the same transaction via `_ledger()` (reasons: checkout, return, edit, adjust, bootstrap, import, reconcile). `checkout()` decrements with a single conditional UPDATE (`WHERE available_qty >= qty`), supports an optional idempotency_key (transactions.idempotency_key UNIQUE; replay returns the existing Loan), blocks a second open loan of the same item by the same person (409 already_checked_out), and emits loan.opened. `return_loan()` requires the full quantity (409 qty_mismatch), restores stock with a CASE-capped UPDATE (never above total_qty), and emits loan.returned with on_time. `reconcile_stock()` compares available_qty to max(total_qty - sum(open OUT loan qty), 0) and files/refreshes `stock_discrepancies` rows without correcting; `resolve_discrepancy(apply_derived=True)` applies the derived value with a ledger row. Reconcile runs from the outbox job kind "stock.reconcile" (ensure_recurring every 24h by the worker thread), the CLI `flask reconcile-stock`, or admin POST /portal/admin/stock/reconcile. Loans live in the historical `transactions` table (`Loan = Transaction` alias; status 'OUT'/'RETURNED'). Fabrication: PrintRequest lifecycle is a free-form status field (submitted|approved|printing|completed|rejected) set by `update_print_request()`; entering "printing" inserts a PrintRun(status=printing) and entering completed/rejected closes the latest printing run as done/failed; emits print.submitted / print.updated; a separate legacy PrintJob shell-command queue (H2S/P1S) exists for ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS. Scheduling: `compute_available_slots()` intersects provider free/busy (Google or Outlook via CalendarProvider) with local `events` table conflicts in 30-min steps within configured work hours and returns opaque tokens "room|YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM|minutes"; `book_slot()` parses the token, double-checks room conflict (pre-insert and post-flush), inserts Event(kind=meeting,status=scheduled) plus CalendarSync(pending) and an OutboxJob "calendar.create_event" in the same transaction, then emits booking.created (which has no subscriber). The outbox handler mirrors the event and records CalendarSync status synced/failed. Admin create_event/update_event do not sync to the external calendar; nothing ever enqueues "calendar.delete_event". Attendance: check-ins are AttendanceRecord rows keyed by (event_id, user_id), idempotent in code only; the event is resolved server-side via `candidate_events()` (scheduled events running now or starting within ASME_CHECKIN_SOON_WINDOW_MINUTES) or an auto-created daily kind="open_shop" Event; emits attendance.recorded. RSVP writes an AttendanceRecord with checkin_method="rsvp" and no event. The onboarding engine subscribes to loan.opened, loan.returned, attendance.recorded, print.submitted, print.updated (re-evaluates that user synchronously in-request) and chapter.changed (re-evaluates chapter).

**Key facts**

- Transaction boundaries: every public write in these four modules ends with db.session.commit() inside the service; audit.record() only adds an AuditLog row to the session (no commit) so it commits with the domain write; events.emit() is always called AFTER commit and handler exceptions are swallowed and logged (asme/events/__init__.py emit()).
- Error contract: ServiceError(message, code='bad_request', status=400, **extra); Validation(message, code='validation', field=None) -> 400 with extra['field'] when given; NotFound -> 404 code 'not_found'; Conflict -> 409 code 'conflict'; Forbidden -> 403. to_dict() = {'ok': False, 'code', 'error', **extra}. api_v1 and api_legacy blueprints register @bp.errorhandler(ServiceError) returning jsonify(exc.to_dict()), exc.status. HTML routes use @on_service_error(default_endpoint) (asme/blueprints/_helpers.py) = flash(exc.message,'error') + flash each exc.extra['errors'] as 'info' + redirect_to_next.
- Loan == Transaction: asme/models/inventory.py line 101 `Loan = Transaction`; table 'transactions'. Open loan = status == 'OUT' (LOAN_STATE_OUT); closed = 'RETURNED' (LOAN_STATE_RETURNED). action column is 'checkout' or 'return'.
- available_qty derivation: items.available_qty is a stored counter mutated only by inventory.py (checkout: conditional UPDATE `available_qty = available_qty - qty WHERE id=? AND available_qty >= qty`, rowcount must be 1; return/legacy restock: UPDATE with CASE capping at total_qty; save_item/adjust_counts/bootstrap_counts/bulk_import/resolve_discrepancy: direct ORM assignment clamped to [0, total_qty]). Item.checked_out_qty property = max(total_qty - available_qty, 0) and is NOT loan-derived.
- Stock ledger: inventory._ledger(item, *, delta_available=0, delta_total=0, reason, loan=None, user=None, note=None) adds StockLedger(item_id, delta_available, delta_total, available_after=item.available_qty, total_after=item.total_qty, reason, transaction_id=loan.id|None, user_id=user.id|None, note[:300]) to the current session; callers db.session.refresh(item) after raw UPDATEs so available_after is accurate. Reasons written: 'checkout', 'return', 'edit' (save_item), 'adjust' (adjust_counts), 'bootstrap', 'import', 'reconcile' (resolve_discrepancy apply_derived). The model docstring lists only checkout/return/adjust/import/bootstrap. The ledger is never read by application code (only deleted by reset_inventory).
- Idempotency (checkout): inventory.checkout looks up Loan.query.filter_by(idempotency_key=key).first() and returns that existing loan unchanged if found, regardless of item/user/qty/state in the replay. The lookup happens AFTER borrower/qty/item.active validation. Column transactions.idempotency_key VARCHAR(120) NULL UNIQUE (constraint uq_transactions_idempotency_key, migration 0002). /api/v1/checkouts REQUIRES a key (Idempotency-Key header, else JSON 'idempotency_key', else form field; utils/http.idempotency_key() truncates to 120) and returns 400 code 'idempotency_required' without one; portal form and legacy /api/checkout pass it optionally. return_loan has no idempotency key; it relies on the status != 'OUT' -> 409 'loan_closed' check.
- checkout conflict codes: Validation 'borrower_required' (no user and no member), Validation field='qty' (qty<=0), Validation 'item_inactive', Conflict 'already_checked_out' (get_open_loan for same user or member on that item), Conflict 'insufficient_stock' with extra available=<int> (after db.session.rollback() + refresh). return_loan: Conflict 'loan_closed', Conflict 'qty_mismatch' with extra expected=<loan.qty>; partial returns are not supported.
- Events emitted by this subsystem (asme/events/__init__.py constants): LOAN_OPENED='loan.opened' (checkout and sign_off_loan; payload loan_id,user_id,member_id,item_id,signed_off), LOAN_RETURNED='loan.returned' (loan_id,user_id,member_id,item_id,on_time), CHAPTER_CHANGED='chapter.changed' (save_item reason=item_saved, reset_inventory reason=inventory_reset, bulk_import reason=inventory_import, create_event reason=event_created, update_event reason=event_updated), PRINT_SUBMITTED='print.submitted' (request_id,user_id), PRINT_UPDATED='print.updated' (request_id,user_id=requester,status), BOOKING_CREATED='booking.created' (event_id,user_id), ATTENDANCE_RECORDED='attendance.recorded' (user_id,event_id,method). Subscribers (asme/services/onboarding/__init__.py): USER_EVENTS incl. loan.opened, loan.returned, attendance.recorded, print.submitted, print.updated -> engine.evaluate_user(user by payload user_id); CHAPTER_EVENTS = chapter.changed, user.created, nfc.assigned -> engine.evaluate_chapter(). booking.created has NO subscriber. No event is emitted by adjust_counts, bootstrap_counts, register_item_tag, resolve_discrepancy, reconcile_stock, rsvp, legacy_transact manual-restock path, legacy scheduling functions, or the legacy PrintJob queue.
- Outbox jobs enqueued by this subsystem: only scheduling.book_slot -> enqueue('calendar.create_event', {'event_id': event.id, 'organizer': user.name}) (default max_attempts=5, run_at=now), and only when sync=True and get_provider().status().enabled. It is added to the same session as the Event and CalendarSync(subject_type='event', subject_id=event.id, provider=provider.name, status='pending') and committed together. 'stock.reconcile' is enqueued by the worker loop via outbox.ensure_recurring('stock.reconcile', timedelta(hours=24)) (max_attempts=3), not by inventory.py. Registered handler kinds (asme/jobs/handlers.py): calendar.create_event, calendar.delete_event, mail.send, stock.reconcile, onboarding.evaluate, onboarding.evaluate_all. No producer exists for calendar.delete_event.
- Outbox mechanics (asme/jobs/outbox.py): enqueue(kind, payload=None, run_at=None, max_attempts=5) adds OutboxJob without committing; process_pending(limit=20) releases 'running' jobs locked >10 min, claims with a conditional UPDATE pending->running, runs handler, marks done or increments attempts with backoff run_at = now + min(2**attempts*15s, 3600s), status 'failed' at attempts >= max_attempts; retry(job) resets to pending/attempts=0. Worker thread 'asme-outbox' is started lazily by an app.before_request hook on the first request when Settings.outbox_worker_enabled (ASME_OUTBOX_WORKER, default true) and env != testing; it polls every outbox_poll_seconds (ASME_OUTBOX_POLL_SECONDS, default 15). CLI: `flask run-jobs --loop|--once`.
- calendar.create_event handler: loads Event; finds latest CalendarSync for (event, subject_id) or creates one; returns early if sync.status=='synced' and external_id set (idempotent); calls provider.create_event(event.location or '', event.title, 'Organizer: <organizer or requested_by.name>/n<description>', start_time, end_time); on CalendarError sets sync.status='failed', last_error[:500], commits, re-raises (so the outbox retries); on success sets sync.external_id/calendar_id/link/status='synced'/last_synced_at, event.calendar_event_link=link, and for provider 'google' also event.google_event_id/google_calendar_id.
- Reconciliation algorithm (inventory.reconcile_stock(actor=None) -> list[StockDiscrepancy]): open_by_item = SELECT item_id, COALESCE(SUM(qty),0) FROM transactions WHERE status='OUT' GROUP BY item_id; for every Item ordered by id: derived = max((total_qty or 0) - open_qty, 0); skip if derived == available_qty; if a StockDiscrepancy with status='open' exists for the item, update its counter_available/derived_available/open_loan_qty in place and do NOT include it in the returned list; else insert StockDiscrepancy(item_id, counter_available, derived_available, open_loan_qty) (status default 'open'); audit 'stock_reconciliation' only if any were filed; commit. Never mutates items. Tests pin: second run files 0 (tests/test_inventory.py::test_reconcile_is_idempotent).
- Discrepancy resolution (inventory.resolve_discrepancy(row, actor, note=None, apply_derived=False)): if apply_derived: item.available_qty = max(0, min(row.derived_available, item.total_qty or 0)) and _ledger(reason='reconcile', user=actor, note=note) with delta_available = new - prev; always sets row.status='resolved', resolved_by_user_id, resolved_at=utcnow, resolution_note[:300]; audit 'resolve_stock_discrepancy'; commit. No guard against resolving an already-resolved row. Uses the stored derived_available (last computed by reconcile), not a fresh computation.
- Print request lifecycle: PRINT_REQUEST_STATUSES = ('submitted','approved','printing','completed','rejected'); PRINT_REQUEST_PRINTERS = ('P1S_1','P1S_2','P1S_3','P1S_4','H2S'). submit_print_request always writes status='submitted'. update_print_request accepts any target status in the tuple with no transition matrix; only side effects are: previous != 'printing' and new == 'printing' -> insert PrintRun(request_id, printer_type, status='printing', started_by_user_id=actor.id, started_at=utcnow); new in {'completed','rejected'} -> latest PrintRun (by id desc) with status 'printing' gets status 'done' (completed) or 'failed' (rejected) and completed_at. 'approved' creates no run. PrintRun.error is never written. reviewed_by_user_id/reviewed_at are overwritten on every update. pending_count() counts status in (submitted, approved, printing).
- Print uploads: stored under <instance_path>/gcode_uploads as f'printreq_{UTC %Y%m%d_%H%M%S}_{uuid4().hex[:8]}_{secure_filename}' (portal requests) or f'{ts}_{uuid8}_{name}' (legacy jobs); allowed extensions ALLOWED_GCODE_EXTENSIONS = {'gcode','gco','3mf'}; size limit Settings.print_max_upload_bytes = ASME_PRINT_MAX_UPLOAD_MB (default 50) * 1MiB, enforced in _store_upload by seeking the stream; Flask MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH = max(print_max_upload_bytes, 64 MiB). Only file_path is stored (no original filename column on PrintRequest).
- Scheduling slot algorithm (compute_available_slots(duration_minutes, day_count, preferred_room='') -> (slots, error)): provider = get_provider() (app.extensions['asme_calendar_provider'], built once from Settings.calendar_provider 'google'|'outlook'); day_count clamped 1..30, duration clamped 30..180; tz = provider.tzinfo() if provider has callable tzinfo (GoogleCalendarProvider -> ZoneInfo(google_calendar_timezone, default America/Chicago); Outlook has none -> naive); busy = provider.free_busy(range_start, range_end) -> on CalendarError returns ([], str(exc)); iterate days x rooms (preferred_room if valid else both MEETING_ROOMS = ('Robotics Room','Fluids Lab')), cursor from work_hours() start to end stepping 30 min, slot must end by work end, start >= now+2min, not overlap provider busy, not overlap local Event conflict (find_event_room_conflict on naive times). Slot dict: {'room','start','end','token': f'{room}|{start:%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M}|{duration}'}. work_hours() reads CALENDAR_WORK_HOURS_START/END (defaults 08:00/22:00; end<=start -> 22:00).
- Room conflict rule (find_event_room_conflict(room, start, end, exclude_event_id=None)): events where lower(coalesce(location,'')) == lower(room) AND status IN ('requested','scheduled') AND start_time < end AND end_time > start; first by start_time. 'cancelled' events do not block. Applies to book_slot (pre-insert and again post-flush excluding the new id -> Conflict 'slot_taken'), create_event and update_event (Conflict 'room_conflict', only when the target status is requested/scheduled).
- book_slot(user, team_name, slot_token, notes='', sync=True) -> Event writes Event(title=f'ASME - {team_name or Team} Meeting'[:220], description=notes[:4000] or None, kind='meeting', location=room, status='scheduled', start_time, end_time, requested_by_user_id=user.id, created_by_user_id=user.id); audit 'schedule_meeting' details f'room={room} start={iso} by={user.email}'; commit; emit booking.created. parse_slot_token raises Validation(field='slot_token') 'Select a valid available slot.' on any parse failure and 'Select a valid room.' if the room segment is not a MEETING_ROOMS name (case-insensitive). team_name is not persisted anywhere except inside title.
- Admin events: create_event(form, actor) requires title, a valid MEETING_ROOMS location, parse_datetime_local start/end (formats %Y-%m-%dT%H:%M, %Y-%m-%d %H:%M, %Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S) with end > start, else Validation('Enter valid title, room, start, and end times.'); status defaults 'scheduled' if not in EVENT_STATUSES=('requested','scheduled','cancelled'); kind defaults 'meeting' and is NOT validated against EVENT_KINDS; audit 'create_event'. update_event(event, form, actor) updates only title, location, start_time, end_time, status (not description/kind); audit 'update_event_status'. Neither creates CalendarSync or an outbox job.
- Attendance event resolution: candidate_events(now=None) = Event.status=='scheduled' AND end_time >= now AND start_time <= now + Settings.checkin_soon_window_minutes (ASME_CHECKIN_SOON_WINDOW_MINUTES, default 20), ordered by start_time; uses datetime.now() (local naive). ensure_open_shop_event(day=None, created_by=None) finds Event(kind='open_shop', start_time == day 00:00) else adds Event(title='Open shop - YYYY-MM-DD', kind='open_shop', location=None, status='scheduled', start 00:00, end 23:59) and flushes only (caller commits). /api/v1/checkins: explicit event_id, else exactly one candidate, else 409 {'ok':false,'code':'choose_event','events':[...]} when >1, else open-shop event; kiosk /checkin does the same with a session-backed select page.
- check_user_into_event(user, event, method='shared_tag', tag_uid=None) -> (AttendanceRecord, created): existing = any AttendanceRecord with event_id and user_id (ANY checkin_method, including 'rsvp') -> returns (existing, False) with no write and no event; else inserts AttendanceRecord(event_id, user_id, member_id=member_for_user(user).id|None, tag_uid=clean or None, checkin_method=method[:40], checkin_time=utcnow), commits, emits attendance.recorded. admin_checkin(event, *, tag_uid=None, user_id=None, requested_method=None, actor=None) -> (row, user): tag -> identity.resolve_user_from_tag_uid (users.nfc_uid or active nfc_tags, active users only) else NotFound('Tag UID not assigned to an active user.'); method = requested_method if in {'kiosk','nfc'} else 'nfc'; user_id -> NotFound('Selected user not found.'), method 'manual'; neither -> Validation('Provide tag UID or select a user.'); existing record -> Conflict code 'already_checked_in'; audit 'attendance_checkin'; commit; emit. rsvp(user, event) -> (row, created) is idempotent on (event, user, checkin_method='rsvp'), commits, emits nothing.
- legacy_scan(uid) -> (Member, first_today): Validation on empty; Member.query.filter_by(nfc_tag=uid) (exact, case-sensitive match, unlike every other tag lookup which uses func.lower) else NotFound; inserts AttendanceScan(member_id, scanned_uid, attendance_date=today) and commits; first_today = count of that member's scans today == 1; if user_for_member(member) and first_today: ensure_open_shop_event(), commit, check_user_into_event(user, event, method='nfc', tag_uid=uid) (always the open-shop event, never a running meeting).
- legacy_transact(*, member, item, action, qty, notes=None, due_date=None, auth_user=None): 'checkout' -> checkout(source='kiosk'); non-'return' -> Validation('Invalid inventory action.'); 'return' with get_open_loan(item.id, member=member) whose qty == qty -> return_loan(condition='manual-return', source='kiosk'); otherwise (no open loan OR qty differs) performs a manual restock: capped UPDATE of available_qty, inserts Transaction(action='return', qty, status='RETURNED', return_condition='manual-return', timestamp=return_time=now, member_id, user_id=auth_user.id|None), ledger reason='return' note='source=kiosk manual-return', commit, NO event. Pinned by tests/test_inventory.py::test_legacy_transact_return_without_open_loan_restocks.
- adjust_counts(item, total_qty, available_qty, note, actor) inserts a zero-qty Transaction(action='return', qty=0, status='RETURNED', return_condition='admin-correction', notes/return_notes=note[:300], member_id=member_for_user(actor), user_id=actor.id) as an activity-feed marker plus ledger reason='adjust' linked to it; audit 'adjust_inventory'; commit; no event. These rows are visible to any Loan query filtering status='RETURNED'.
- save_item(form, actor) -> Item: creates Item(name, total_qty=0, available_qty=0) + flush when item_id missing/unknown; Conflict codes 'nfc_taken' (validate_item_tag_uid checks members.nfc_tag, users.nfc_uid, active nfc_tags.tag_uid, other items.nfc_tag, other item_tags.tag_value), 'item_code_taken', 'tracker_taken' (each after db.session.rollback()); item_type not in ITEM_TYPES=('tool','consumable') -> 'tool'; is_consumable = item_type=='consumable'; active parsed from form 'active' (default '1'); total_qty default previous; available_qty default min(prev_available, total) then clamped to [0,total]; ensure_item_tracking_ids assigns unique item_code 'ASME-INV-%05d' and treasury_tracker_id 'ASME-TRK-<code suffix>' with '-2','-3' de-dup suffixes; set_item_primary_nfc sets items.nfc_tag (None when the form field is empty) and upserts item_tags(source='inventory_admin'); ledger reason='edit' only if total or available changed; audit 'save_inventory_item'; commit; emit chapter.changed reason='item_saved'.

**Extension points**

- Add a stock mutation: call inventory._ledger(item, delta_available=..., delta_total=..., reason='<new reason>', loan=..., user=..., note=...) in the same session before db.session.commit(); refresh(item) first if you used a bulk UPDATE. reason is free text VARCHAR(40); update the StockLedger docstring list. Any new path that changes items.available_qty outside inventory.py will be flagged by reconcile_stock unless it also changes loans consistently.
- Add a domain event: define a constant in asme/events/__init__.py, append it to ALL_EVENTS (tests assert on it), emit after commit, and (if the onboarding engine should react) add it to USER_EVENTS or CHAPTER_EVENTS in asme/services/onboarding/__init__.py. BOOKING_CREATED currently has no subscriber and is the natural hook for booking notifications.
- Add background work: decorate a function with @asme.jobs.outbox.handler('<kind>') in asme/jobs/handlers.py (module is imported by asme.jobs at app creation), and call asme.jobs.outbox.enqueue('<kind>', payload) inside the service before its commit so the job lands transactionally. Handler exceptions trigger retry with exponential backoff up to max_attempts. calendar.delete_event already exists but has no producer - wiring it into scheduling.update_event when status becomes 'cancelled' (payload {'sync_id': sync.id}) is the intended shape.
- Recurring jobs: outbox.ensure_recurring(kind, timedelta, payload) in asme/jobs/outbox._loop (currently only 'stock.reconcile' every 24h).
- Calendar providers: subclass asme.integrations.calendar.base.CalendarProvider (name, status, free_busy, create_event, delete_event, embed_url, optional tzinfo()), register in build_provider() keyed by Settings.calendar_provider, and add the config key to Settings.from_env. Tests swap providers via app.extensions['asme_calendar_provider'].
- Print lifecycle: PRINT_REQUEST_STATUSES / PRINT_REQUEST_PRINTERS / PRINTER_TYPES in asme/constants.py drive validation; add transition rules inside fabrication.update_print_request (currently none). PrintRun.error is available but unused for failure detail.
- Attendance methods: checkin_method is a free VARCHAR(40); onboarding rules treat 'rsvp' specially (excluded from attendance counts). New check-in surfaces should call attendance.check_user_into_event (idempotent, emits) rather than writing AttendanceRecord directly.
- Event resolution window for taps: Settings.checkin_soon_window_minutes (ASME_CHECKIN_SOON_WINDOW_MINUTES); open-shop auto events via attendance.ensure_open_shop_event.
- Slot generation knobs: work_hours() (CALENDAR_WORK_HOURS_START/END), 30-minute step and 2-minute lead time are hardcoded in compute_available_slots; MEETING_ROOMS tuple in constants defines rooms everywhere (provider room_ids maps, conflict checks, legacy form).
- Route error translation: reuse @on_service_error(default_endpoint) for HTML routes and the blueprint-level errorhandler(ServiceError) for JSON; raise ServiceError subclasses with a stable `code` and put machine-readable extras in **extra (they are merged into the JSON body, e.g. available=, expected=, errors=[]).
- Entitlement gating: require_entitlement(<key from constants ALL_ENTITLEMENTS>) on new routes; shadow mode is the default (ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE=0).
- Serializers for new fields: asme/services/serializers.py serialize_item / serialize_loan / serialize_print_request / serialize_event / serialize_attendance_record are the API shapes; the /api/v1/items ETag is a sha1 of the serialized body so adding fields changes ETags.
- Tag resolution order (payload prefix -> item_tags -> items.nfc_tag) is centralized in inventory.find_item_by_tag; TAG_PREFIXES in constants.
- Bulk import column aliases are the *aliases tuples in inventory.bulk_import; matching precedence item_code -> nfc -> name.

**Risks / known defects**

- Unauthenticated write endpoints reach these services: legacy_ops POST /transact, /attendance/scan, /print/submit, /print/job/<id>/complete|fail|delete and GET /print/job/<id>/download|open, and api_legacy POST /api/inventory/transact, /api/attendance/scan, /api/print/submit, /api/print/job/<id>/complete|fail|delete, GET /api/bootstrap have neither a login gate nor the ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS _gate(). Both blueprints are always registered (asme/blueprints/__init__.py) and there is no blueprint before_request. /transact and /api/inventory/transact accept member_id/item_id and perform checkouts/returns/restocks (with ledger rows) as any member; /api/bootstrap leaks the full member roster (names, emails, nfc_tag).
- Idempotency replay is unscoped: inventory.checkout returns any existing Loan whose idempotency_key matches, regardless of item, borrower, qty or whether it is already RETURNED; keys are client-chosen (max 120 chars) and global across users. Two concurrent first-time requests with the same key both pass the SELECT, both decrement stock, and the second INSERT fails on uq_transactions_idempotency_key as an untranslated IntegrityError (HTTP 500) - the stock decrement is rolled back only because the session is discarded at teardown.
- return_loan is not concurrency-safe: the status=='OUT' guard is a plain read, and the CASE-capped UPDATE is unconditional on loan state, so two simultaneous returns of the same loan can both increment available_qty (bounded only by total_qty) and write two ledger rows. Likewise the ledger delta_available for returns is always +qty even when the CASE capped the change, so sum(delta_available) can drift from available_after.
- legacy_transact 'return' with an open loan whose qty differs from the submitted qty silently falls through to a manual restock (new RETURNED row with no checkout) and leaves the real loan OUT - double counting stock and creating a discrepancy that reconcile will flag against the item.
- adjust_counts writes qty=0 status='RETURNED' Transaction rows attributed to the admin's user_id; onboarding rules count Loan rows by user_id and status (asme/services/onboarding/rules.py lines 140-145, 199-201), so admin corrections and legacy manual-restock rows count as 'returned loans' for whoever performed them, and /api/v1/checkouts?state=returned lists them.
- book_slot trusts the slot token: parse_slot_token only checks format and room name, so any client with room_booking can book any time (past, outside work hours, or during a provider-busy window) - only the local events-table conflict check applies; external calendar busy windows are never re-verified at booking time. The post-flush conflict re-check is not a serialization guarantee on databases with READ COMMITTED isolation (fine on SQLite where writes serialize).
- Cancelling or rescheduling a booked event (scheduling.update_event) does not touch CalendarSync or enqueue calendar.delete_event, so the external calendar keeps the original entry; the 'calendar.delete_event' handler is dead code. Admin create_event never mirrors to the external calendar at all. Legacy meetings (meetings table) and portal events (events table) are conflict-checked against different tables, so the two surfaces can double-book a room.
- Attendance idempotency treats an RSVP as a check-in: check_user_into_event returns (existing, False) if ANY AttendanceRecord exists for (event, user), including checkin_method='rsvp', so a member who RSVP'd and then taps in gets no 'nfc'/'shared_tag' record and no attendance.recorded event, and onboarding rules (which exclude rsvp) never credit the attendance.
- Open-shop auto events are status='scheduled' all day, so once one exists for today it is always a candidate; whenever a real meeting is also running, candidate_events() returns 2+ and every tap hits the 409 choose_event / select-page path. legacy_scan always attaches to the open-shop event even when a meeting is running.
- Clock mixing: Event.start_time/end_time, candidate_events, upcoming_events, overdue_loans (date.today) and today_count use local naive time, while AttendanceRecord.checkin_time, Loan timestamps and CalendarSync use UTC. today_count/history-by-date filters on UTC checkin_time with local-day bounds; Loan.is_overdue uses utc date while overdue_loans uses local date. On a UTC-hosted server (Elastic Beanstalk) 'local' is UTC, so behavior differs between developer machines and production.
- compute_available_slots with the Google provider produces tz-aware slot datetimes but stores naive local Event times parsed from the token; find_event_room_conflict compares naive values. If google_calendar_timezone differs from the server's local zone, 'now' filtering and stored times disagree. The Outlook provider returns busy windows re-tagged with the requested tzinfo without conversion (it assumes the mailbox timezone equals outlook_calendar_tz).
- reconcile_stock and resolve_discrepancy race with live loans: derived_available is a snapshot; resolve_discrepancy(apply_derived=True) writes that stale snapshot into available_qty even if loans changed since the last reconcile, and re-resolving an already resolved row is allowed and overwrites resolver fields. reconcile also clamps derived to >= 0, so an over-issued item (open loans > total_qty) is reported as derived 0 rather than negative.
- Free-form status fields without transition validation: PrintRequest.status can move from completed back to submitted; a request set to 'printing' twice in a row with a different status in between creates multiple PrintRun rows and only the latest is closed; PrintRun.error is never populated; PrintRequest.user_id is the event payload's user_id on PRINT_UPDATED so the requester (not the reviewer) is re-evaluated.
- Legacy print queue executes shell commands: launch_print_command runs subprocess.run(cmd_template.format(file=..., filename=..., job_id=...), shell=True) synchronously inside the request; the command template comes from env/instance file and the filename passes through secure_filename, but any operator template that does not quote {file}/{filename} is injectable and a slow printer command blocks the worker/request.
- save_item with an empty nfc_tag form field sets items.nfc_tag = NULL (clears the primary tag) while leaving item_tags rows in place; validate_item_tag_uid/ItemTag lookups are case-insensitive but the DB UNIQUE constraints on item_tags.tag_value, items.nfc_tag, members.nfc_tag, users.nfc_uid are case-sensitive, so the same UID in different case can exist in two tables and still resolve ambiguously.

## onboarding (Launchpad engine)

Launchpad is a derived-progress onboarding engine. Static definitions live in three tables (onboarding_tracks -> onboarding_phases -> onboarding_tasks) upserted by key from Python dict specs in seeds.py (MEMBER_TRACK key=member_onboarding audience=user with 4 phases/15 tasks; CHAPTER_TRACK key=chapter_setup audience=chapter with 2 phases/10 tasks; 3 TrainingModule rows). Per-subject state lives in onboarding_task_states / onboarding_phase_states keyed on (subject_type in {user,chapter}, subject_id str) so the same engine drives per-user and per-academic-year tracks. engine.evaluate(subject) walks every active track for the subject's audience, calls rules.evaluate_rule(task, subject, state) for each task (12 registered rule_type handlers in rules.RULES, each querying domain tables and returning RuleResult(current,target,complete,evidence)), writes task status pending/in_progress/complete (human rules manual/signoff keep stored status and only refresh counters), then advances phase status locked->active->complete when all is_required tasks are complete, regresses complete->active (and revokes source=phase entitlements for that phase) when a required task stops being complete, locks every phase after the first incomplete one, commits, and returns progress(subject). Phase completion applies phase.grants: keys prefixed "role:" promote user.role via normalize_role/role_allows (never demote) and audit "promote_role"; any other key becomes an Entitlement row via entitlements.grant(source="phase", source_ref=f"{track.key}:{phase.key}"). entitlements is a separate axis from role: has_entitlement(user,key) is True for admins always, else requires an Entitlement row with revoked_at NULL and expires_at NULL or in future. Admin overrides are Entitlement rows with source="override" source_ref="admin" (optional expires_at); the engine only revokes source="phase" rows, so overrides survive re-evaluation. Route gating is auth.session.require_entitlement(key): passes if has_entitlement; otherwise if Settings.onboarding_enforce (env ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE, default False) returns entitlement_denied_response (JSON 403 code=entitlement_required with entitlement/phase/phase_name, or flash+redirect to portal.member_launchpad), else SHADOW MODE: appends key to flask.g.shadow_entitlement_blocks, logs "shadow-block entitlement=... user_id=... path=..." on logger asme.auth, and lets the request through; logging_setup emits shadow_blocks in the per-request structured log. Re-evaluation is triggered by asme.events (in-process bus; emit(name, **payload) after commit; handler exceptions are logged and swallowed): services/onboarding/__init__.register_event_handlers() (called from create_app) subscribes _on_user_event (needs payload user_id; loads User; engine.evaluate_user) to 12 USER_EVENTS and _on_chapter_event (engine.evaluate_chapter()) to CHAPTER_EVENTS = chapter.changed, user.created, nfc.assigned. Also re-evaluated synchronously on GET /portal/member/launchpad, GET /api/v1/onboarding, launchpad_admin_context (evaluate_chapter + member_overview), the outbox jobs onboarding.evaluate / onboarding.evaluate_all, flask CLI evaluate-launchpad, and summary_for_user for subjects with no PhaseState yet.

**Key facts**

- MODULE LAYOUT: asme/services/onboarding/__init__.py exports engine, entitlements, rules, seeds, register_event_handlers; logger name 'asme.onboarding'.
- EVENT WIRING (__init__.py): USER_EVENTS = (loan.opened, loan.returned, attendance.recorded, print.submitted, print.updated, training.completed, user.updated, user.created, nfc.assigned, team.joined, hours.logged, task.signed_off). CHAPTER_EVENTS = (chapter.changed, user.created, nfc.assigned). _on_user_event(name, **payload) returns silently if payload lacks 'user_id' or User not found, else engine.evaluate_user(user). _on_chapter_event ignores payload, calls engine.evaluate_chapter(). booking.created is defined in asme.events but NOT subscribed by onboarding. task.signed_off is subscribed but grep shows NO emitter anywhere in asme/.
- EVENT BUS (asme/events/__init__.py): subscribe(name, handler) dedupes; emit(name, **payload) -> int handlers run; calls named subscribers then '*' subscribers; wraps each handler in try/except logging via 'asme.events' (onboarding failures never propagate). ALL_EVENTS tuple of 14 names; docstring says tests assert on it. Handlers are called as handler(name, **payload).
- register_event_handlers() is invoked in create_app (asme/__init__.py line 81) after register_blueprints; subscriptions are module-global (process-wide), events.clear() exists for tests.
- SUBJECT: rules.Subject dataclass(type: str, id: str, user: User|None) with is_user / is_chapter properties. engine.subject_for_user(user) -> Subject('user', str(user.id), user). engine.subject_for_chapter(year_key=None) -> Subject('chapter', year_key or academic_year_key(), None). academic_year_key() = f'{start}-{(start+1)%100:02d}' where start=year if month>=8 else year-1 (e.g. '2026-27'). Constants SUBJECT_USER='user', SUBJECT_CHAPTER='chapter' in asme/constants.py.
- tracks_for(subject) = Track where audience==subject.type AND is_active IS TRUE ordered by id asc. progress() and evaluate() both use tracks_payload[0] for top-level percent/steps_left, i.e. the FIRST active track per audience is the headline track.
- TASK STATE MACHINE (engine._apply_result): current=int(result.current); target=max(int(result.target),1); if result.complete: status='complete' and completed_at set only on transition; else status='in_progress' if current>0 else 'pending', completed_at=None; evidence=result.evidence (json, default=str). For rule_type in HUMAN_RULES={'manual','signoff'} evaluate() does NOT call _apply_result; it only refreshes current/target and leaves status/completed_at/completed_by/note untouched.
- PHASE STATE MACHINE (engine.evaluate): per track, previous_complete starts True. For each phase in Phase.order: get/create PhaseState (default 'locked'); compute all_required_done over tasks with is_required. Transitions: locked & previous_complete -> 'active' (started_at set once). complete & !all_required_done -> 'active', completed_at=None, _revoke_grants, log 'phase regressed'. active & all_required_done -> 'complete', completed_at=now, _apply_grants, log 'phase completed'. complete & all_required_done -> _apply_grants again (idempotent re-assert). Second pass: after the first non-complete phase, every later non-complete phase is forced to 'locked'. Note: a locked phase whose predecessor is incomplete is never activated even if all its tasks are already complete; a phase that is active cannot go back to locked unless a predecessor becomes incomplete (second pass).
- evaluate(subject, commit=True) commits db.session then returns progress(subject). evaluate_user(user, commit=True) and evaluate_chapter(year_key=None, commit=True) are thin wrappers.
- GRANTS (engine._apply_grants): no-op when subject.user is None (chapter track grants are [] anyway). source_ref = f'{track.key}:{phase.key}' (e.g. 'member_onboarding:shop_ready'; note model comment says 'member:shop_ready' but code uses track.key). For each key in phase.grants: if key startswith 'role:' -> wanted=normalize_role(suffix); if not role_allows(user.role, wanted): user.role=wanted and audit.record('promote_role', 'user_id=.. role=.. via=<source_ref>') (never demotes; not reverted on regression). Else ent.grant(user, key, source='phase', source_ref=source_ref, granted_by=None). _revoke_grants -> ent.revoke_phase_grants(user, source_ref) which only touches source='phase' rows with that exact source_ref; role promotions are NOT undone.
- ENTITLEMENT KEYS (asme/constants.py): portal_access, event_checkin, shop_access, print_submit, room_booking, extended_loan_14d (ENT_EXTENDED_LOAN), print_approve, checkout_signoff; ALL_ENTITLEMENTS tuple of those 8. Admin grant/revoke routes validate key in ALL_ENTITLEMENTS. user_entitlements() adds ALL_ENTITLEMENTS for admins. Seeded grants: signed_up -> [portal_access, event_checkin]; shop_ready -> [shop_access, print_submit]; contributor -> [extended_loan_14d, room_booking]; team_lead -> ['role:team_leader', print_approve, checkout_signoff]. Nothing in the read code consumes extended_loan_14d or checkout_signoff via require_entitlement (grep of require_entitlement shows only ENT_PORTAL_ACCESS, ENT_SHOP_ACCESS, ENT_PRINT_SUBMIT, ENT_PRINT_APPROVE, ENT_EVENT_CHECKIN, ENT_ROOM_BOOKING).
- ENTITLEMENTS API (entitlements.py): active_rows(user) -> Entitlement rows with revoked_at NULL filtered in Python by row.is_active (also checks expires_at). user_entitlements(user)->set[str] (empty set for None; union ALL_ENTITLEMENTS if role_allows(role,'admin')). has_entitlement(user,key)->bool (False for None; True for admin). grant(user, key, *, source='phase', source_ref=None, granted_by=None, expires_at=None, reason=None, commit=False) -> (row, created: bool); idempotent on active (user,key,source) — returns existing and updates its source_ref if different; new row audits 'grant_entitlement'. revoke(user, key, *, source=None, revoked_by=None, reason=None, commit=False) -> int count; sets revoked_at/revoked_by_user_id (and reason if given) on all unrevoked rows for key (optionally filtered by source); audits 'revoke_entitlement' when count>0. revoke_phase_grants(user, source_ref, commit=False) -> int; sets revoked_at and reason='phase requirements no longer met' on source='phase' rows with source_ref; no audit. phase_that_grants(key) -> first Phase (order asc, id asc, ANY track) whose grants contain key, or None.
- OVERRIDE MECHANISM: admin POST /portal/admin/launchpad/users/<user_id>/entitlements/grant -> entitlements.grant(target, key, source='override', source_ref='admin', granted_by=admin, expires_at=utcnow+expires_days if form expires_days>0 else None, reason=form reason, commit=True). Revoke route -> entitlements.revoke(target, key, revoked_by=admin, reason, commit=True) with source=None, so it revokes BOTH phase and override rows; a phase row revoked this way is re-granted on the next evaluate because the phase is still complete (engine re-asserts grants, comment 'idempotent re-assert (e.g. after admin revoke expiry)'). Override rows are never revoked by the engine (test_override_grant_survives_reevaluation).
- SHADOW vs ENFORCE: Settings.onboarding_enforce: bool = False; from_env reads _bool('ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE', default=False) (truthy set {1,true,yes,on,y}); tests pass onboarding_enforce=True via create_app(**overrides). require_entitlement(key) decorator (asme/auth/session.py): no user -> _deny_login (JSON {'ok':False,'code':'login_required','error':'Login required.'} 401 when request path starts with /api/ or Accept prefers JSON; else flash + redirect auth.login_page). has_entitlement -> proceed. Not entitled and enforce -> entitlement_denied_response(key,user): payload {'ok':False,'code':'entitlement_required','entitlement':key,'phase':phase.key|None,'phase_name':phase.name|None,'error':"You need '<key>' to do that. Complete the Launchpad phase that unlocks it."} -> JSON 403, or flash 'Finish the "<phase.name>" phase in Launchpad to unlock that.' (or generic) + redirect portal.member_launchpad. Not entitled and NOT enforce -> append key to g.shadow_entitlement_blocks (list), log.info('shadow-block entitlement=%s user_id=%s path=%s') on 'asme.auth', proceed. logging_setup._log_request includes 'shadow_blocks': g.shadow_entitlement_blocks in the structured request log. cfg.onboarding_enforce is exposed to templates as 'onboarding_enforced' (context processor + admin settings/launchpad contexts + member_dashboard_context).
- require_entitlement is always stacked AFTER require_login/require_role in existing routes (role first, then entitlement). Admins bypass entitlements entirely (has_entitlement short-circuit).
- RULE CONTRACT (rules.py): handler signature fn(subject: Subject, config: dict, state: TaskState|None) -> RuleResult; RuleResult dataclass(current:int=0, target:int=1, complete:bool=False, evidence:dict={}). evaluate_rule(task, subject, state): RULES.get(task.rule_type) else RuleResult(evidence={'error': f'unknown rule_type {task.rule_type}'}) (unknown rule = never completes, no exception). HUMAN_RULES={'manual','signoff'}. Adding a rule type = add function + register in RULES dict.
- RULES registered (12): manual, signoff (both -> _stored(state): complete iff state.status=='complete', evidence completed_by); existence (config fields[list] and/or field[str]; special names 'nfc' (user.nfc_uid truthy OR active NFCTag row) and 'team' (ProjectMembership with left_at NULL); others getattr(user,name) not in (None,'',0); current=total-missing, target=max(len(fields),1), evidence missing[]); email_domain (config domains[]; matches exact or subdomain; evidence domain); count_threshold (config model in USER_COUNT_MODELS {'AttendanceRecord','Loan','Transaction'->Loan,'PrintRequest','ProjectMembership'}, min (>=1), window, where.status, exclude_rsvp default True for AttendanceRecord; evidence count); hours_threshold (sum WorkLog.hours, config min, window, approved_only; current=int(min(hours,min)); evidence hours); clean_streak (config min, window; on_time = count of RETURNED loans with returned_on_time; overdue_open = OUT loans with due_date < today; complete iff on_time>=min and overdue_open==0); loan_signed_off (count Loan where signed_off_by_user_id NOT NULL >= min); training (config module key, min_score default module.min_score; newest-first TrainingCompletion rows; first row with is_valid (expires_at NULL or > utcnow) and score>=min_score (if min_score not None; score None fails) completes; module missing -> evidence error 'module not found'); chapter_count (config model in {'User' (is_active, where.role_in), 'Item' (where.active default True), 'ItemLocation' (distinct lower(location) non-empty), 'Project' (where.joinable), 'Event' (status=='scheduled', where.future default True -> start_time >= datetime.now(), where.kind), 'NFCTag' (active)}, min; unknown model -> 0); config_set (config setting in {'calendar' (scheduling.provider_status().enabled or scheduling.calendar_embed_url()), 'smtp' (cfg.smtp_user and cfg.smtp_pass), 'printers' (cfg.h2s_print_cmd and cfg.p1s_print_cmd)}; evidence detail); member_phase_ratio (config phase key, min_ratio default 0.5; members = active users with role in [member, team_leader]; done = distinct subject_id of PhaseState status complete for any Phase with that key and subject_type 'user'; target=int(round(min_ratio*100)); current=min(round(ratio*100), target)). All user rules return RuleResult() (incomplete) when subject.user is None.
- WINDOW CONFIG (_window_start): config['window'] '' -> None; 'semester' -> utils.semester_start() (Aug 1 if month>=8; Jan 1 if month 1-5; Jun 1 if month 6-7); 'days:N' -> today - N days; anything else -> None. Applied to AttendanceRecord.checkin_time, Loan.timestamp, PrintRequest.created_at (datetime.combine(since, min.time())) and WorkLog.logged_for (date).
- PROGRESS PAYLOAD (engine.progress(subject) -> dict): {'subject': {'type','id'}, 'tracks': [ {key,name,audience,description,percent,required_total,required_done,steps_left,current_phase,complete,phases:[ {key,name,tagline,description,order,status,est_minutes,grants,tasks:[ {key,name,description,required,status,current,target,est_minutes,rule_type,rule_config,cta_route,cta_label,help_url,evidence,completed_at(iso|None),note,human} ],required_total,required_done,steps_left,started_at,completed_at} ] } ], 'percent': tracks[0].percent or 0, 'steps_left': tracks[0].steps_left or 0, 'entitlements': sorted(user_entitlements) or []}. percent = round(required_done/required_total*100) counting only is_required tasks across all phases (member track: 13 required of 15). current_phase = first phase with status 'active'; track.steps_left = current_phase.steps_left. track.complete = all phases complete (and at least one phase). Missing TaskState -> status 'pending', current 0, target 1, evidence {}. Missing PhaseState -> 'locked'. progress() does NOT evaluate; it reads stored state.
- summary_for_user(user) -> {'percent','steps_left','current_phase','complete','entitlements'}; calls progress() if any PhaseState exists for the subject, else evaluate() (first-touch evaluation for legacy accounts). Used by member_dashboard_context ('launchpad' key), GET /api/v1/me ('launchpad' + serialize_user(entitlements=...)), and member_overview().
- HUMAN TASKS: engine.find_task(task_key, audience) -> first Task (id asc) joined Phase->Track where Task.key==task_key AND Track.audience==audience AND Track.is_active. complete_task(task_key, subject, actor, note=None) -> TaskState: NotFound('Task not found.', code='task_not_found') if missing; Validation('That task is completed automatically when its requirement is met.', code='task_not_manual', rule_type=...) if rule_type not in HUMAN_RULES; signoff: by_role = rule_config.by_role or 'team_leader'; actor must role_allows(by_role) else Forbidden(code='signoff_role'); actor cannot be subject.user unless admin -> Forbidden(code='self_signoff'); manual: user subject requires actor is owner or role_allows team_leader else Forbidden(code='not_owner'); chapter subject requires admin else Forbidden(code='admin_only'); rule_config.note_required and blank note -> Validation('A note is required to complete this task.', field='note'). On success: status='complete', current=1, target=1, completed_at=utcnow, completed_by_user_id=actor.id, note=note[:300] or None, audit 'complete_onboarding_task', commit, then evaluate(subject). reopen_task(task_key, subject, actor, note=None): admin only (Forbidden code='admin_only'); resets status='pending', current=0, completed_at/completed_by None, note; audit 'reopen_onboarding_task'; commit; evaluate.
- TRAINING: engine.record_training(user, module_key, score=None, actor=None, note=None) -> TrainingCompletion: NotFound(code='module_not_found'); actor != user and not team_leader -> Forbidden(code='signoff_role'); actor == user and module.min_score truthy and not admin -> Forbidden(code='signoff_role') (scored modules cannot be self-recorded); score coerced int, must be 0..100 else Validation(field='score'); expires_at = utcnow + module.validity_days days if set; recorded_by_user_id=actor.id; audit 'record_training'; commit; emits events.TRAINING_COMPLETED(user_id, module, score) which triggers evaluate_user via the bus. Seeded modules: shop_safety (min_score 80, validity 365d, required), machine_basics (no min_score, 730d, optional), lead_essentials (no min_score, never expires, required).
- member_overview() -> list of {'user': User, **summary_for_user(user)} for active users with role in ['member','team_leader'] ordered by name, id. launchpad_admin_context additionally computes pending_signoffs (human tasks in each user's current_phase that are not complete) and unsigned_loans (Loan status 'OUT' with signed_off_by_user_id NULL, latest 50).
- SEEDS (seeds.py): seed_default_tracks(commit=True) = seed_training_modules() + _upsert_track(MEMBER_TRACK) + _upsert_track(CHAPTER_TRACK). Upsert matches Track by key, Phase by (track_id,key), Task by (phase_id,key); always overwrites name/description/order/est_minutes/grants/rule_type/rule_config/cta_*/help_url/is_required (default True) and sets track.is_active=True. It NEVER deletes phases/tasks removed from the spec (seen_phase_ids/seen_task_ids are collected but unused) and never touches TaskState/PhaseState/Entitlement rows. Called from bootstrap.seed_defaults(commit) (auto_migrate startup path when not testing, CLI 'seed' and 'upgrade-db') and directly in tests/conftest.
- SEEDED MEMBER TRACK task keys by phase: signed_up(order1): verify_email(email_domain uiowa.edu), complete_profile(existence major,graduation_year), link_nfc(existence nfc), join_team(existence team). shop_ready(2): shop_safety(training shop_safety min_score 80), first_meeting(count_threshold AttendanceRecord min1), supervised_checkout(loan_signed_off min1), machine_training(training machine_basics, OPTIONAL). contributor(3): three_meetings(count_threshold AttendanceRecord min3 window semester), clean_returns(clean_streak min3), ten_hours(hours_threshold min10 window semester), first_print(count_threshold PrintRequest min1 where status completed, OPTIONAL). team_lead(4): nominated(signoff by_role admin), lead_training(training lead_essentials), build_cycle(signoff by_role team_leader).
