**Extension points**

- NEW RULE TYPE: add `def rule_<name>(subject, config, state) -> RuleResult` in asme/services/onboarding/rules.py and register it in RULES. Return RuleResult() (incomplete) when subject.user is None for user rules. Never store derived state; query domain tables. If the task must be human-marked, add its rule_type to HUMAN_RULES so evaluate() preserves stored status and complete_task accepts it.
- NEW COUNTABLE MODEL for count_threshold: add a branch in rules._count_query (filter by user, optional where.status, optional window) and a name in USER_COUNT_MODELS. For chapter_count add a branch in rules._chapter_count.
- NEW config_set setting: add an `elif which == '<name>'` branch in rules.rule_config_set reading from asme.config.settings().
- NEW TASK / PHASE / TRACK: edit MEMBER_TRACK / CHAPTER_TRACK / TRAINING_MODULES dicts in seeds.py (or build a new spec dict and call seeds._upsert_track(spec)); seeding is by key and idempotent. Add phase 'grants' as entitlement keys (must be added to constants.ALL_ENTITLEMENTS to be admin-manageable and auto-granted to admins) or 'role:<role>' strings. If you add a new track for audience 'user' it will NOT be the headline track unless its id is lower than member_onboarding (tracks_for orders by id asc and progress uses tracks[0]). Removing a task from the spec does not delete it from the DB — you must delete rows manually or set Track.is_active=False.
- NEW ENTITLEMENT KEY: add ENT_* constant and append to ALL_ENTITLEMENTS in asme/constants.py; add it to a phase's grants in seeds.py; gate routes with @require_entitlement(ENT_X) placed after @require_login/@require_role. Import entitlements lazily if the module is imported by asme.auth.session (circular import).
- NEW DOMAIN EVENT that should re-evaluate a user: define the constant in asme/events/__init__.py, append to ALL_EVENTS, add it to USER_EVENTS (or CHAPTER_EVENTS) in asme/services/onboarding/__init__.py, and emit it from the service AFTER db.session.commit() with keyword payload including user_id=<int>. Handler ignores events with no user_id or unknown user.
- NEW MODULE that writes domain data a rule reads: emit an existing event (user.updated with user_id is the generic catch-all for per-user changes; chapter.changed with reason= for chapter-wide changes) after commit, or enqueue outbox job 'onboarding.evaluate' with {'user_id': id} / {'chapter': True} for deferred evaluation.
- PROGRESS CONSUMERS: engine.progress(subject) for full payload without re-evaluating; engine.summary_for_user(user) for cheap dashboard reads; engine.evaluate_user(user) when freshness matters (each call runs every rule query and commits).
- HUMAN TASK FLOWS: call engine.complete_task(task_key, subject, actor, note) / engine.reopen_task(...) rather than mutating TaskState directly; they audit, commit, and re-evaluate. Wrap HTML routes with @on_service_error(endpoint); API routes rely on the api_v1 ServiceError errorhandler.
- OVERRIDE / TEMPORARY ACCESS: entitlements.grant(user, key, source='override', source_ref=<who/why>, granted_by=actor, expires_at=..., reason=..., commit=True). Use a source other than 'phase' so the engine's regression revoke does not touch it. entitlements.revoke(..., source='override') to remove only overrides.
- ROLE PROMOTION via phase: use grant string 'role:<role>' in phase.grants; engine promotes with normalize_role/role_allows and audits 'promote_role'. Demotion is never automatic.
- BACKGROUND RE-EVALUATION: outbox.enqueue('onboarding.evaluate_all', {}) or ('onboarding.evaluate', {'user_id':..., 'chapter': True}); remember to db.session.commit() after enqueue.
- TESTING: tests/conftest.make_app(onboarding_enforce=True) fixture 'enforced_app' for enforce-mode tests; captured_events fixture subscribes '*' to assert emitted events; seed_default_tracks() is called per test app.

**Risks / known defects**

- Shadow mode is the default (ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE unset -> False). Flipping to enforce will start returning 403 code=entitlement_required on every @require_entitlement route for non-admin users lacking grants; existing member accounts that predate Launchpad only receive grants when something triggers evaluate (summary_for_user, dashboard load, event, or CLI evaluate-launchpad). Run `flask evaluate-launchpad` before enabling enforcement.
- engine.evaluate() is synchronous and runs inside event emit on the request thread for every subscribed domain event (checkout, return, check-in, print submit/update, profile save, NFC assign, team join, hours log). It issues one query per task rule plus per-state lookups (member track: 15 tasks, ~40+ queries) and commits. It also commits the session mid-request, which will flush any unrelated pending changes the emitting code left uncommitted. Handler exceptions are swallowed and logged by the bus, so a broken rule silently stops progress updates.
- evaluate() commits unconditionally (commit=True default) even when called from GET routes (/portal/member/launchpad, /api/v1/onboarding, admin launchpad page via evaluate_chapter). Read paths have write side effects and create TaskState/PhaseState rows on first sight.
- member_overview() calls summary_for_user per active member (N+1 across users, each reading all task/phase states, and evaluating for users without PhaseState). launchpad_admin_context also runs evaluate_chapter(), whose member_phase_ratio and chapter_count rules scan users/items/events. Admin and leader Launchpad pages get slower linearly with roster size.
- chapter subject id is academic_year_key() computed at call time. On 1 August a new chapter subject appears with all phases locked/active from scratch; the old year's state rows remain but are never re-read. Any manual chapter tasks (open_print_queue, set_loan_periods) must be re-completed each year.
- Role promotion via 'role:team_leader' is one-way: regression of the team_lead phase revokes print_approve/checkout_signoff entitlements but leaves user.role='team_leader'. Since admins bypass entitlements and role checks come first, a demoted-by-requirements lead keeps role-gated pages but loses entitlement-gated actions only when enforce=1.
- entitlements.revoke with source=None (used by the admin revoke route) revokes phase-sourced rows too; the next evaluate() re-grants them because the phase is still complete (engine re-asserts grants on complete phases). Admin revoke of a phase-earned entitlement is therefore not durable; only override rows stay revoked.
- entitlements.grant idempotency is per (user, key, source) ignoring source_ref: if two phases (or two tracks) grant the same key, only one active phase row exists and its source_ref is overwritten to the most recent phase. Regression of either phase then calls revoke_phase_grants with its own source_ref; if the ref was overwritten by the other phase, the revoke silently misses (returns 0) and the entitlement persists; conversely regression of the phase whose ref won revokes a grant the other phase still legitimately earns until the next evaluate re-asserts it.
- Phase.grants and Task.rule_config are JSON in Text columns with lenient parsing (invalid JSON -> [] / {}); a malformed seed silently disables grants or rule config rather than failing.
- Task.key is unique only per phase; engine.find_task picks the lowest Task.id matching (key, audience). Reusing a task key in a second phase or second track of the same audience makes the later one unreachable through complete_task/reopen_task.
- rules.rule_existence uses getattr(user, name) and treats 0 as missing; a graduation_year of 0 or a boolean False field would be reported missing. Field names in rule_config come from seeds/DB and are not validated against User columns (typo -> always missing -> task can never complete).
- count_threshold on 'Loan'/'Transaction' filters by Loan.user_id; loans created through legacy kiosk paths with user=None (member only) do not count, and inventory.checkout emits loan.opened with user_id=None which the onboarding handler ignores.
- rule_training reads config.get('min_score', module.min_score): an explicit rule_config {'min_score': null} disables the score check even when the module has min_score set. Self-recorded unscored modules are allowed for the actor themselves (only scored modules require a lead).
- rule_chapter_count for Event uses datetime.now() (local time) while everything else uses datetime.utcnow(); Event.start_time comparisons are timezone-naive.
- seeds._upsert_track never deletes phases/tasks removed from the spec (seen_*_ids collected but unused) and always re-activates the track (is_active=True). Deactivating a seeded track by setting is_active=False is undone on next seed/startup with auto_migrate.

## jobs-events-integrations

Three loosely coupled pieces. (1) asme/events: a synchronous, in-process, module-global pub/sub bus (subscribe/unsubscribe/clear/emit) with 14 string event names; services call events.emit(NAME, **payload) strictly AFTER db.session.commit(); the only production subscriber is the Launchpad onboarding engine (asme/services/onboarding/__init__.py.register_event_handlers, called from create_app), which re-evaluates the user (payload user_id) or the chapter and commits. Subscriber exceptions are logged on logger 'asme.events' and swallowed. (2) asme/jobs: a transactional outbox on table outbox_jobs (model OutboxJob). enqueue() only session.add()s the row (caller commits with the domain write); process_pending() releases stale 'running' rows (>10 min), claims 'pending' due rows with a conditional UPDATE, runs the registered handler (dict registry filled by the @handler(kind) decorator in asme/jobs/handlers.py), marks done, or on any exception rolls back, increments attempts, sets last_error, and reschedules with backoff min(2**attempts*15s, 3600s) until attempts >= max_attempts -> 'failed'. A daemon thread 'asme-outbox' (start_worker) is started lazily on the first HTTP request per process when ASME_OUTBOX_WORKER=1 and not testing; it loops ensure_recurring('stock.reconcile', 24h) + process_pending() every ASME_OUTBOX_POLL_SECONDS (15). Alternative runners: `flask run-jobs --loop`, `python manage.py worker`. Six handlers: calendar.create_event, calendar.delete_event, mail.send, stock.reconcile, onboarding.evaluate, onboarding.evaluate_all. No idempotency key; handlers must be idempotent. (3) asme/integrations: mail.py (single send_email(cfg,to,subject,body) STARTTLS plain-text SMTP; MailNotConfigured), http.py (urllib JSON client with retry used only by Outlook), calendar/ (CalendarProvider interface: status/free_busy/create_event/delete_event/embed_url; GoogleCalendarProvider via service account; OutlookCalendarProvider via Graph client-credentials; build_provider(cfg) picks by Settings.calendar_provider; get_provider() reads current_app.extensions['asme_calendar_provider']), assistant.py (Anthropic tool-use loop run_assistant() with 14 read-only SQLAlchemy analytics tools; exposed at POST /api/ask admin-only). logging_setup.py installs one JSON-lines stdout handler on the root logger and logs one 'request' record per HTTP request on logger 'asme.request' with request_id/method/path/status/duration_ms/user_id/endpoint/shadow_blocks, echoing X-Request-Id.

**Key facts**

- EVENT BUS API (asme/events/__init__.py): module-global `_subscribers: dict[str, list[Callable[..., None]]] = defaultdict(list)`; `subscribe(name: str, handler: Callable[..., None]) -> None` (dedupes by identity); `unsubscribe(name, handler) -> None`; `clear() -> None`; `emit(name: str, **payload) -> int` calls `handler(name, **payload)` for every subscriber of `name` and then every subscriber of the wildcard `"*"`, catches Exception per handler and logs `log.exception("event handler failed: event=%s handler=%r")` on logger `asme.events`, returns count of handlers that completed without raising. Synchronous, same thread, no persistence, no cross-process delivery, no return values, no priority/ordering beyond registration order.
- EVENT NAMES (constants -> string): LOAN_OPENED='loan.opened', LOAN_RETURNED='loan.returned', ATTENDANCE_RECORDED='attendance.recorded', PRINT_SUBMITTED='print.submitted', PRINT_UPDATED='print.updated', TRAINING_COMPLETED='training.completed', USER_UPDATED='user.updated', USER_CREATED='user.created', NFC_ASSIGNED='nfc.assigned', TEAM_JOINED='team.joined', HOURS_LOGGED='hours.logged', TASK_SIGNED_OFF='task.signed_off', BOOKING_CREATED='booking.created', CHAPTER_CHANGED='chapter.changed'; `ALL_EVENTS` tuple lists all 14. Docstring says tests assert on ALL_EVENTS but no test references it (grep).
- EVENT EMITTERS + PAYLOAD KEYS (all emitted immediately after db.session.commit()): LOAN_OPENED asme/services/inventory.py:343 {loan_id, user_id|None, member_id|None, item_id, signed_off: bool} and :395 sign_off_loan {loan_id,user_id,member_id,item_id,signed_off=True}; LOAN_RETURNED inventory.py:380 {loan_id,user_id,member_id,item_id,on_time}; ATTENDANCE_RECORDED asme/services/attendance.py:71,:115 {user_id,event_id,method}; PRINT_SUBMITTED asme/services/fabrication.py:95 {request_id,user_id}; PRINT_UPDATED fabrication.py:125 {request_id,user_id,status}; TRAINING_COMPLETED asme/services/onboarding/engine.py:361 {user_id,module(key str),score}; USER_CREATED asme/services/identity.py:281,:489,:544 and asme/services/roster.py:192 {user_id}; USER_UPDATED identity.py:365,:805 and asme/services/content.py:97 {user_id}, identity.py:626 {user_id, role_changed: bool}; NFC_ASSIGNED identity.py:628,:791 {user_id,tag_uid}; TEAM_JOINED content.py:87 {user_id,project_id}; HOURS_LOGGED content.py:113,:121 {user_id,project_id|None,hours}; BOOKING_CREATED asme/services/scheduling.py:158 {event_id,user_id}; CHAPTER_CHANGED {reason} with reasons 'project_saved' (content.py:57), 'item_saved' (inventory.py:508), 'inventory_reset' (:598), 'inventory_import' (:771), 'roster_import' (roster.py:193), 'event_created' (scheduling.py:204), 'event_updated' (:235). TASK_SIGNED_OFF is defined and subscribed but NEVER emitted anywhere under asme/.
- EVENT SUBSCRIBERS: only asme/services/onboarding/__init__.py. `USER_EVENTS` = all names except BOOKING_CREATED and CHAPTER_CHANGED (12 names) -> `_on_user_event(name, **payload)`: reads payload.get('user_id'), `db.session.get(User, int(user_id))`, calls `engine.evaluate_user(user)` (which commits: `evaluate_user(user, commit=True)`). `CHAPTER_EVENTS` = (CHAPTER_CHANGED, USER_CREATED, NFC_ASSIGNED) -> `_on_chapter_event(name, **payload)` -> `engine.evaluate_chapter()` (commits). `register_event_handlers()` is called once in create_app (asme/__init__.py:81) after register_blueprints. BOOKING_CREATED currently has no subscriber. tests/conftest.py fixture `captured_events` subscribes a '*' wildcard handler and unsubscribes on teardown.
- OUTBOX TABLE `outbox_jobs` (model OutboxJob, asme/models/audit.py:19; DDL migrations/versions/0002_*): id Integer PK; kind String(60) NOT NULL, index ix_outbox_jobs_kind; payload_json Text NOT NULL, ORM default '{}' (no server default); status String(20) NOT NULL, ORM default 'pending', index ix_outbox_jobs_status, values pending|running|done|failed; attempts Integer NOT NULL, default 0; max_attempts Integer NOT NULL, default 5; run_at DateTime NOT NULL, default datetime.utcnow, index ix_outbox_jobs_run_at; locked_at DateTime NULL; last_error String(1000) NULL; created_at DateTime NOT NULL, default utcnow; completed_at DateTime NULL. No unique constraints, no FKs, no idempotency/dedupe column. All timestamps naive UTC.
- OUTBOX ENQUEUE API: `enqueue(kind: str, payload: dict | None = None, run_at: datetime | None = None, max_attempts: int = 5) -> OutboxJob` (asme/jobs/outbox.py:43) builds OutboxJob(kind, payload_json=json.dumps(payload or {}, default=str), status='pending', run_at=run_at or utcnow, max_attempts) and ONLY calls db.session.add(job) - no flush/commit; the caller's db.session.commit() lands it in the same transaction as the domain write. Payload must be JSON-serializable (non-JSON values are str()'d silently, e.g. datetimes). Re-exported from asme.jobs (`from asme.jobs import enqueue, process_pending, start_worker, stop_worker`).
- OUTBOX HANDLER REGISTRY: `_handlers: dict[str, Callable[[dict], None]]` module dict in asme/jobs/outbox.py; decorator `handler(kind: str)` stores fn under kind (silently overwrites an existing registration) and returns fn unchanged; `registered_kinds() -> list[str]` sorted. Handler signature is `fn(payload: dict) -> None`; return value ignored; any exception = failure/retry. Registration happens by importing asme/jobs/handlers.py, which asme/jobs/__init__.py imports at line 1 and create_app imports via `import asme.jobs` (asme/__init__.py:77). Importing only `asme.jobs.outbox` still triggers the package __init__, so handlers are always registered when outbox is imported.
- OUTBOX WORKER LOOP `process_pending(limit: int = 20) -> int` (outbox.py:94): step 1 bulk UPDATE rows with status='running' AND locked_at < now-10min back to status='pending', locked_at=NULL, then commit (crashed-worker recovery; also re-runs any legitimately long job >10 min). Step 2 SELECT status='pending' AND run_at <= now ORDER BY run_at ASC, id ASC LIMIT limit. Step 3 per job `_claim(job)`: conditional UPDATE WHERE id=job.id AND status='pending' SET status='running', locked_at=now, synchronize_session=False, commit; proceeds only if rowcount==1 (optimistic multi-process safety). Step 4 `_run_one(job)`: fn=_handlers.get(kind); payload=json.loads(payload_json or '{}'); if fn is None raises RuntimeError("no handler registered for job kind '<kind>'") which is treated like any handler failure; success path: db.session.refresh(job); status='done'; completed_at=now; last_error=None; commit; return True. Failure path: db.session.rollback() (discards any uncommitted handler work), job = db.session.get(OutboxJob, id); attempts=(attempts or 0)+1; last_error=str(exc)[:1000]; locked_at=None; if attempts >= (max_attempts or 1): status='failed' + log.error('outbox job failed permanently id=%s kind=%s error=%s'); else status='pending', run_at = now + timedelta(seconds=min(2**attempts*15, 3600)) (30s, 60s, 120s, 240s, ... capped 1h) + log.warning('outbox job retry ...'); commit; return False. Returns number of successes. Logger name 'asme.jobs'.
- OUTBOX RECURRING/ADMIN HELPERS: `ensure_recurring(kind: str, every: timedelta, payload: dict | None = None) -> bool` returns False if any job of kind exists with status in ('pending','running') OR status='done' with completed_at >= now-every; otherwise enqueue(kind, payload or {}, run_at=now, max_attempts=3) + commit, returns True. NOTE 'failed' rows are not considered, so a permanently failed recurring job is re-enqueued on the very next poll. `stats() -> dict[str,int]` counts by status; `recent_failures(limit=25) -> list[OutboxJob]` status='failed' ORDER BY id DESC; `retry(job)` sets status='pending', attempts=0, run_at=now, last_error=None, commit.
- OUTBOX THREAD: `_loop(app, stop_event)` enters `app.app_context()` once and loops until stop_event: try `ensure_recurring('stock.reconcile', timedelta(hours=24))` then `process_pending()`; on Exception log.exception('outbox worker iteration failed') + rollback; finally `db.session.remove()`; then `stop_event.wait(cfg.outbox_poll_seconds)`. `start_worker(app) -> threading.Thread | None` keyed by id(app) in `_worker_threads`/`_stop_events`, daemon thread named 'asme-outbox', idempotent while alive. `stop_worker(app)` sets the event and join(timeout=2). Started lazily by before_request hook `_start_worker_once` (asme/__init__.py:105-111) when `cfg.outbox_worker_enabled and not cfg.is_testing and not app.extensions.get('asme_worker_started')`; the flag app.extensions['asme_worker_started']=True is per process. With Procfile `gunicorn --workers 2` there will be two independent worker threads (one per process) competing via _claim. 'stock.reconcile' is the ONLY scheduled recurring job and it is scheduled from inside _loop only.
- OUTBOX CONFIG: Settings.outbox_worker_enabled <- env ASME_OUTBOX_WORKER (bool, default True); Settings.outbox_poll_seconds <- ASME_OUTBOX_POLL_SECONDS (int, default 15, minimum 1). tests/conftest.py make_app() passes outbox_worker_enabled=False and env='testing'. manage.py creates the app with outbox_worker_enabled=(command == 'serve'); `python manage.py worker` loops process_pending() with time.sleep(poll) in the foreground (no ensure_recurring call - the recurring stock.reconcile is NOT scheduled by this runner). Flask CLI `run-jobs [--loop/--once]` (asme/__init__.py:181) likewise only calls process_pending(). Admin POST /portal/admin/launchpad/reevaluate calls outbox.process_pending() inline when the worker flag is off.
- OUTBOX HANDLERS (asme/jobs/handlers.py): 'calendar.create_event' payload {event_id:int, organizer?:str} - loads Event (silently returns if missing); finds latest CalendarSync(subject_type='event', subject_id=event.id) ORDER BY id DESC or creates one (provider=get_provider().name, status='pending'); idempotency guard: return if sync.status=='synced' and sync.external_id; description = 'Organizer: {organizer or event.requested_by.name}' + event.description; provider.create_event(event.location or '', event.title, description, event.start_time, event.end_time); on CalendarError sets sync.status='failed', sync.last_error=str(exc)[:500], commits, re-raises (so outbox retries); on success sets sync.provider/external_id/calendar_id/link/status='synced'/last_error=None/last_synced_at, event.calendar_event_link=link, and if provider.name=='google' also event.google_event_id/event.google_calendar_id (legacy columns); commit. Producer: services/scheduling.book_slot (line 155) which adds CalendarSync(pending) + enqueue only when provider.status().enabled. 'calendar.delete_event' payload {sync_id:int} - loads CalendarSync; returns if missing or no external_id; get_provider().delete_event(sync.calendar_id, sync.external_id); status='deleted', last_synced_at; commit. NO producer anywhere in asme/ (grep). 'mail.send' payload {to:str, subject?:str, body?:str} -> mail.send_email(settings(), to, subject or '(no subject)', body or ''). Producer: services/notifications.queue_email(to, subject, body) which itself has no callers. 'stock.reconcile' ignores payload; lazy-imports asme.services.inventory and calls reconcile_stock() (signature `reconcile_stock(actor=None) -> list[StockDiscrepancy]`). 'onboarding.evaluate' payload {user_id?:int, chapter?:bool} -> engine.evaluate_user / engine.evaluate_chapter; no producer today. 'onboarding.evaluate_all' ignores payload; evaluates every User where is_active is True then the chapter; producer admin.launchpad_reevaluate.
- HANDLER TRANSACTION CONTRACT: handlers run inside the worker's app context on the scoped db.session; they are expected to db.session.commit() their own work (all built-ins do). After fn returns, _run_one does db.session.refresh(job) then commits the status flip separately, so a handler that commits and then raises leaves its committed side effects in place while the job is retried (calendar_create_event relies on this to persist sync.status='failed'). A handler that raises before committing loses everything via rollback. process_pending() must be invoked on a clean session because _claim() commits whatever is pending on the session.
- MAIL ADAPTER (asme/integrations/mail.py): `class MailNotConfigured(RuntimeError)`; `is_configured(cfg: Settings) -> bool` = bool(cfg.smtp_user and cfg.smtp_pass); `send_email(cfg: Settings, to: str, subject: str, body: str) -> None` raises MailNotConfigured('SMTP is not configured. Set ASME_SMTP_USER and ASME_SMTP_PASS.') when not configured, ValueError('Recipient is required.') when `to` is blank; builds email.message.EmailMessage with From=cfg.smtp_user, To=to.strip(), Subject, set_content(body) (text/plain only); `smtplib.SMTP(cfg.smtp_host, cfg.smtp_port, timeout=30)` -> starttls() -> login(cfg.smtp_user, cfg.smtp_pass) -> send_message(). No HTML, attachments, CC/BCC, Reply-To, multiple recipients, or implicit-TLS(465) support; smtplib exceptions propagate. Settings: smtp_host <- ASME_SMTP_HOST (default 'smtp.office365.com'), smtp_port <- ASME_SMTP_PORT (587), smtp_user <- ASME_SMTP_USER, smtp_pass <- ASME_SMTP_PASS, cancel_notify_to <- ASME_CANCEL_NOTIFY_TO. Settings.validate() adds problem 'ASME_SMTP_USER and ASME_SMTP_PASS must be set together.' (blocks startup in production). Callers: jobs/handlers.mail_send; services/notifications.send_meeting_cancel_confirmation_email(meeting, confirm_url, reject_url) -> str|None (legacy synchronous, returns error string); services/notifications.queue_email(to, subject, body) -> OutboxJob (durable, uncommitted).
- HTTP CLIENT (asme/integrations/http.py): `http_json_request(method, url, headers=None, form_data=None, json_data=None, timeout=30, retries=0, retry_backoff=0.8) -> tuple[status:int|None, payload:dict, error:str|None]`; urllib only. form_data -> urlencoded body + 'Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded'; json_data -> JSON body + 'application/json'. Success returns (resp.status, parsed json or {}, None). HTTPError with code in (429,500,502,503,504) and attempts remaining -> sleep retry_backoff*2**attempt and retry; otherwise returns (code, parsed error body or {'raw': body}, error_body). URLError / any other Exception -> retry if attempts remain else (None, {}, str(exc)). Used only by OutlookCalendarProvider.
- CALENDAR INTERFACE (asme/integrations/calendar/base.py): `@dataclass ProviderStatus(provider: str, enabled: bool, errors: list[str]=[], warnings: list[str]=[], room_ids: dict[str,str]={})`; `@dataclass CreatedEvent(external_id: str|None, calendar_id: str|None, link: str|None)`; `class CalendarError(RuntimeError)` (the only exception type callers catch); `class CalendarProvider` with class attr `name = 'none'` and methods `status(self) -> ProviderStatus`, `free_busy(self, time_min: datetime, time_max: datetime) -> dict[str, list[tuple[datetime, datetime]]]` (busy windows keyed by room name from constants.MEETING_ROOMS = ('Robotics Room', 'Fluids Lab')), `create_event(self, room: str, summary: str, description: str, start_local: datetime, end_local: datetime) -> CreatedEvent`, `delete_event(self, calendar_id: str|None, external_id: str) -> None`, `embed_url(self) -> str` (default ''). `NullProvider(reason='No calendar provider is configured.')` name 'none': status() -> enabled=False, errors=[reason]; other ops raise CalendarError(reason). NullProvider is exported but build_provider never returns it.
- CALENDAR FACTORY (asme/integrations/calendar/__init__.py): `build_provider(cfg: Settings) -> CalendarProvider` returns OutlookCalendarProvider(cfg) if cfg.calendar_provider == 'outlook' else GoogleCalendarProvider(cfg). `get_provider() -> CalendarProvider` returns current_app.extensions['asme_calendar_provider'], lazily building and caching it if absent. create_app sets app.extensions['asme_calendar_provider'] = build_provider(cfg) (asme/__init__.py:71). Tests swap in a fake via app.extensions['asme_calendar_provider'] = Fake() (tests/test_outbox.py:64). Settings.calendar_provider <- ASME_CALENDAR_PROVIDER lower-cased, default 'google', any value outside {'google','outlook'} coerced to 'google'.
- GOOGLE PROVIDER (asme/integrations/calendar/google.py) name='google', ctor (cfg: Settings); optional deps google.oauth2.service_account + googleapiclient.discovery.build (set to None on ImportError; requirements pin google-api-python-client>=2.160,<3.0, google-auth>=2.35,<3.0). room_ids() -> {'Robotics Room': normalize_google_calendar_id(cfg.google_calendar_id_robotics), 'Fluids Lab': normalize(cfg.google_calendar_id_fluids)}; `normalize_google_calendar_id(raw)` URL-decodes up to twice and accepts raw id, 'src=<id>' or a full embed URL (extracts ?src=). tzinfo() -> ZoneInfo(cfg.google_calendar_timezone) else UTC. status() errors: 'ASME_CALENDAR_PROVIDER is not set to google.', 'GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ID_ROBOTICS is not configured.', 'GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ID_FLUIDS is not configured.', 'GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON is not configured.', 'google-api-python-client is not installed.'; enabled = not errors. service() lazily builds and caches build('calendar','v3', credentials=service_account.Credentials.from_service_account_info(info, scopes=['https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar']), cache_discovery=False), where cfg.google_service_account_json may be a file path or inline JSON; ANY failure returns None silently. free_busy() -> freebusy().query(body={timeMin, timeMax (isoformat), timeZone, items:[{id}]}) -> per-room list of (start,end) converted .astimezone(tzinfo); raises CalendarError('Google Calendar integration is not configured.') or CalendarError('Google freebusy request failed: ...'). create_event() -> events().insert(calendarId=room id, body={summary[:220], description[:4000], location=room, start/end {dateTime: isoformat, timeZone}}) -> CreatedEvent(id, calendar_id, htmlLink); CalendarError('No Google calendar ID configured for <room>.') or 'Google event create failed: ...'. delete_event(): no-op when calendar_id or external_id falsy; events().delete; CalendarError on failure. embed_url(): cfg.google_calendar_embed_url or 'https://calendar.google.com/calendar/embed?ctz=<tz>&mode=WEEK&showTitle=0&showPrint=0&src=<id>&src=<id>'. Config keys: GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON, GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ID_ROBOTICS, GOOGLE_CALENDAR_ID_FLUIDS, GOOGLE_CALENDAR_TIMEZONE (default 'America/Chicago'), ASME_GOOGLE_CALENDAR_EMBED_URL.
- OUTLOOK PROVIDER (asme/integrations/calendar/outlook.py) name='outlook', GRAPH='https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0', ctor (cfg) with in-memory `_token_cache` {access_token, expires_at, tenant_id, client_id}. room_ids(): robotics -> cfg.outlook_calendar_robotics_id or cfg.outlook_calendar_id; fluids -> cfg.outlook_calendar_fluids_id or cfg.outlook_calendar_id; calendar_id_for_room(room). `validate_outlook_sync_config(cfg) -> (errors, warnings)`: requires ASME_OUTLOOK_CALENDAR_USER, ASME_OUTLOOK_TENANT_ID (rejects common/organizations/consumers), ASME_OUTLOOK_CLIENT_ID, ASME_OUTLOOK_CLIENT_SECRET; error if mailbox domain in constants.KNOWN_NON_MS_MAIL_DOMAINS, warning if in POSSIBLY_CONSUMER_MS_DOMAINS or not email-shaped. `has_any_outlook_sync_inputs(cfg) -> bool`. access_token(): client-credentials POST https://login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant}/oauth2/v2.0/token scope 'https://graph.microsoft.com/.default' via http_json_request(retries=2); caches until now + max(60, expires_in-120) (900s if no expires_in); CalendarError('Outlook token request failed: ...'[:250]) / 'Outlook token response missing access_token.'. free_busy(): per room GET {GRAPH}/users/{user}/calendars/{id}/calendarView (or /users/{user}/calendarView when no id) ?startDateTime&endDateTime&$select=start,end&$top=200 with Bearer header, retries=2; non-200 -> CalendarError('Outlook calendarView failed for <room>: ...'); parses start/end dateTime[:19] as naive and stamps time_min.tzinfo; no @odata.nextLink paging; no Prefer: outlook.timezone header. create_event(): POST .../calendars/{id}/events or .../events with {subject[:250], body {contentType:'Text', content: description or 'Created from ASME website scheduler.'}, start/end {dateTime: '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S', timeZone: cfg.outlook_calendar_tz}, location {displayName: room}}; expects 200/201 and 'id'; CreatedEvent(id, calendar_id or None, webLink). delete_event(): DELETE /users/{user}/events/{id}; 200/202/204/404 succeed; else CalendarError. embed_url(): normalize_outlook_embed_url(cfg.outlook_calendar_embed_url) rewrites https://outlook.(live|office|office365).com/owa/calendar/<owner>/<publish>/<cid>/.../index.html -> /calendar/0/published/<owner>/<publish>/<cid>/calendar.html/. Config keys: ASME_OUTLOOK_TENANT_ID, ASME_OUTLOOK_CLIENT_ID, ASME_OUTLOOK_CLIENT_SECRET, ASME_OUTLOOK_CALENDAR_USER, ASME_OUTLOOK_CALENDAR_ID, ASME_OUTLOOK_CALENDAR_ROBOTICS_ID, ASME_OUTLOOK_CALENDAR_FLUIDS_ID, ASME_OUTLOOK_CALENDAR_TZ (default 'Central Standard Time'), ASME_OUTLOOK_CALENDAR_EMBED_URL. Settings.validate() blocks production startup when the 4 core Outlook inputs are only partially set. Legacy services/scheduling.legacy_book_meeting/legacy_confirm_cancel instantiate OutlookCalendarProvider(cfg) directly (bypassing get_provider) for the legacy `meetings` table.
- CALENDARSYNC TABLE `calendar_syncs` (asme/models/events.py:45): id Integer PK; subject_type String(40) NOT NULL default 'event'; subject_id Integer NOT NULL index ix_calendar_syncs_subject_id (no FK); provider String(20) NOT NULL ('google'|'outlook'); calendar_id String(260) NULL; external_id String(260) NULL; link String(500) NULL; status String(20) NOT NULL default 'pending' (pending|synced|failed|deleted); last_synced_at DateTime NULL; last_error String(500) NULL; created_at DateTime NOT NULL default utcnow. No unique constraint on (subject_type, subject_id): handlers pick the newest by id DESC. services/scheduling.sync_status_for_event(event_id) does the same lookup. Related Event columns: calendar_event_link String(500) NULL, google_event_id String(220) NULL, google_calendar_id String(260) NULL (legacy inline ids still written for google).
- ASSISTANT (asme/integrations/assistant.py): lazy `_db()`/`_models()` accessors to avoid circular imports; `_setting(name, env_name)` reads current_app.config['SETTINGS'].<name> falling back to os.environ[env_name] (the only config read of os.environ outside asme/config.py). `TOOLS` = 14 Anthropic tool specs {name, description, input_schema}: count_active_members(), get_pending_prints(), get_print_queue_stats(), get_overdue_checkouts(), get_active_checkouts(), search_inventory(query), most_checked_out_items(limit=10), get_recent_signups(days=30), get_upcoming_events(limit=10), get_attendance_stats(event_id?), get_inactive_members(days=30), get_unread_contact_messages(), search_members(query), get_dashboard_summary(). `TOOL_MAP` name -> `tool_<name>(**kwargs) -> dict`; all read-only SQLAlchemy queries (several use the legacy Transaction/Member tables with status 'OUT' / action 'checkout', not Loan). `SYSTEM_PROMPT` ('ASKme Analytics'). `run_assistant(user_message: str, history: list | None = None, max_steps: int = 6) -> dict`: returns {'reply': 'The anthropic Python package is not installed...', 'tool_calls': []} on ImportError, {'reply': 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is not set...', 'tool_calls': []} when key missing; else Anthropic(api_key).messages.create(model=_setting('anthropic_model','ANTHROPIC_MODEL') or 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001', max_tokens=1024, system=SYSTEM_PROMPT, tools=TOOLS, messages=history+[user]) in a loop up to max_steps; on stop_reason=='tool_use' executes each tool_use block via TOOL_MAP (unknown -> {'error': 'Unknown tool <name>'}, exceptions -> {'error': 'ExcType: msg'}), appends {'type':'tool_result','tool_use_id','content': json.dumps(result, default=str)[:4000]} as a user message and continues; on any other stop returns {'reply': joined text blocks or '(no response)', 'tool_calls': [{'name','args'}...], 'history': messages}; after max_steps returns {'reply': 'Stopped after too many tool steps.', 'tool_calls': [...]} (no history key). Settings: anthropic_api_key <- ANTHROPIC_API_KEY; anthropic_model <- ASME_ASSISTANT_MODEL (default 'claude-sonnet-5'), so the in-code haiku fallback and the ANTHROPIC_MODEL env fallback are effectively dead unless the setting is empty. requirements: anthropic>=0.40,<1.0. services/ai_assistant.py at repo root is a re-export shim.
- ASSISTANT ROUTE: POST /api/ask (asme/blueprints/api_legacy.py:84, blueprint 'api_legacy' url_prefix '/api', endpoint api_legacy.api_ask): inline gate `current_auth_user()` and normalize_role(user.role)=='admin' else 403 {'error':'admin only'}; JSON body {message: str (required, max 2000 chars), history: list (client-supplied, unvalidated)}; 400 {'error':'empty message'} / {'error':'message too long'}; 200 {'reply': str, 'tool_calls': [...]} (updated history is NOT returned). Runs synchronously in the request (gunicorn --timeout 120).
- LOGGING (asme/logging_setup.py): `JsonFormatter.format(record)` emits one JSON object per line: {'ts': formatTime(record, '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') (local time, no tz/millis), 'level': levelname, 'logger': name, 'msg': getMessage()} merged with `record.asme` when it is a dict (pass via `extra={'asme': {...}}`), plus 'exc': formatted traceback when exc_info; json.dumps(default=str). `configure_logging(app)`: adds a single logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout) with JsonFormatter to the ROOT logger, tagged `handler._asme_json = True` so repeated create_app() calls do not duplicate it; root and logger 'asme' level = DEBUG if app.debug else INFO; logger 'werkzeug' forced to WARNING (no default access log). Registers @app.before_request `_start_timer`: g._req_started = time.perf_counter(); g.request_id = (request.headers.get('X-Request-Id') or uuid4().hex[:12]).strip()[:64]. Registers @app.after_request `_log_request`: for paths starting '/static/' or equal '/healthz' only sets response header X-Request-Id; otherwise logs INFO on logger 'asme.request' msg 'request' with asme={request_id, method, path, status: response.status_code, duration_ms (round 1dp), user_id: session.get('auth_user_id'), endpoint: request.endpoint, shadow_blocks: g.shadow_entitlement_blocks (list of entitlement keys that would have blocked in enforce mode; populated by asme/auth/session.require_entitlement when ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE=0)} and sets X-Request-Id on the response. configure_logging is called in create_app BEFORE _register_request_hooks, so its before_request runs first and its after_request runs last. Existing logger names: 'asme', 'asme.request', 'asme.events', 'asme.jobs', 'asme.auth', 'asme.bootstrap', 'asme.onboarding'. Worker-thread log lines share the same JSON handler but carry no request_id.
- APP WIRING ORDER (asme/__init__.py create_app): Settings.from_env(**overrides) -> validate() (RuntimeError in production on problems, warnings otherwise) -> db.init_app, migrate.init_app -> app.extensions['asme_calendar_provider']=build_provider(cfg) -> configure_logging(app) -> register_blueprints(app) -> register_event_handlers() -> _register_request_hooks (worker lazy-start, auth guardrails, no-cache headers, teardown db.session.remove()) -> _register_cli -> optional bootstrap.sync_schema()+seed_defaults() when auto_migrate and not testing. Deployment: Procfile `release: python manage.py upgrade`, `web: gunicorn --bind :8000 --workers 2 --timeout 120 app:app`; no dedicated worker process is declared, so background work relies on the in-process thread(s).
- TEST PATTERN FOR OUTBOX (tests/test_outbox.py): register ad-hoc handlers with `@outbox.handler('test.echo')` inside the test, `outbox.enqueue(kind, payload, max_attempts=N)`, `db.session.commit()`, then assert on `outbox.process_pending()` return value and OutboxJob.status/attempts/last_error; force a retry to run by setting job.run_at to the past; swap the calendar provider via app.extensions['asme_calendar_provider']. conftest make_app(): env='testing', database_url='sqlite://', auto_migrate=False, outbox_worker_enabled=False, onboarding_enforce=False, calendar_provider='google'.

**Extension points**

- ADD A JOB HANDLER: in asme/jobs/handlers.py add `@handler("<domain>.<verb>")/ndef <name>(payload: dict) -> None:` (kind must fit String(60)). Contract: payload is the JSON-decoded dict (datetimes arrive as strings because enqueue uses default=str); load rows by id from payload, never pass ORM objects; be idempotent (jobs can run twice: stale-lock release after 10 min, admin retry, duplicate enqueues); do your own `db.session.commit()`; raise any exception to trigger retry/backoff (message[:1000] stored in last_error), return normally to mark done; lazy-import services inside the function to avoid import cycles (pattern used by stock.reconcile/onboarding.*). If you put handlers in a new module, import it from asme/jobs/__init__.py or they will never register (unknown kinds fail at run time, not enqueue time). Producer side: `from asme.jobs.outbox import enqueue` (or `from asme.jobs import enqueue`) and call `enqueue(kind, payload, run_at=None, max_attempts=5)` BEFORE the service's `db.session.commit()` so the row shares the transaction. Test: `from asme.jobs import outbox`; enqueue; db.session.commit(); assert outbox.process_pending() == 1; inspect OutboxJob rows (see tests/test_outbox.py). Update the handler list in README.md 'Background work'.
- ADD A RECURRING JOB: the only scheduler is the `ensure_recurring("stock.reconcile", timedelta(hours=24))` call inside `_loop` in asme/jobs/outbox.py:160; add another `ensure_recurring(kind, every, payload)` line there (max_attempts is fixed at 3 by ensure_recurring). Note `flask run-jobs` and `manage.py worker` do not call ensure_recurring, so a dedicated worker process will not schedule recurring jobs unless you also add the calls there (or refactor a shared `tick()`).
- ADD A DOMAIN EVENT: in asme/events/__init__.py add `NEW_NAME = "domain.verb"` and append it to ALL_EVENTS. Emit from the service with `from asme import events; events.emit(events.NEW_NAME, key=value, ...)` placed AFTER `db.session.commit()` (subscribers commit themselves; emitting before commit would let a subscriber commit your half-finished transaction). Include `user_id` in the payload if the onboarding engine should re-evaluate that user. Subscribe by adding the constant to USER_EVENTS or CHAPTER_EVENTS in asme/services/onboarding/__init__.py, or by writing `def on_x(name: str, **payload) -> None` and calling `events.subscribe(events.NEW_NAME, on_x)` from a register function invoked in create_app (asme/__init__.py wiring section). Subscribers run synchronously inside the request thread and their exceptions are swallowed/logged - for anything slow or external, have the subscriber `enqueue(...)` an outbox job and `db.session.commit()` instead of doing the work inline. Tests can capture emissions with the `captured_events` fixture ('*' subscriber).
- ADD A CALENDAR PROVIDER: create asme/integrations/calendar/<name>.py subclassing CalendarProvider with a unique class attr `name`, implementing status() -> ProviderStatus (enabled must be False with human-readable errors when unconfigured), free_busy(time_min, time_max) -> {room: [(start, end)]} for every room in constants.MEETING_ROOMS, create_event(room, summary, description, start_local, end_local) -> CreatedEvent, delete_event(calendar_id, external_id), embed_url(); raise only CalendarError for surfaced failures. Add a branch in build_provider() (asme/integrations/calendar/__init__.py), add the name to the allowed set in Settings.from_env (asme/config.py:228 `{"google","outlook"}`), add typed config fields + env parsing to Settings, and consider Settings.validate()/warnings(). calendar.create_event in handlers.py has a google-only branch writing legacy Event.google_* columns; CalendarSync.provider is String(20).
- ADD AN ASSISTANT TOOL: in asme/integrations/assistant.py append a spec to TOOLS ({name, description, input_schema with JSON-schema properties/required}), implement `tool_<name>(**kwargs) -> dict` using `_db()`/`_models()` (read-only queries; keep output small - serialized result is truncated to 4000 chars), and register it in TOOL_MAP under the same name. Add any new model to `_models()`.
- ADD A LOG FIELD / STRUCTURED LOG: use `logging.getLogger("asme.<sub>")` (inherits the level set on 'asme') and pass `extra={"asme": {...}}` to merge keys into the JSON line; `g.request_id` is available inside a request for correlation. To add request-level fields edit the `asme` dict in `_log_request` (asme/logging_setup.py:58-69). Background/worker logs have no request_id - add job id/kind in the extra dict if needed.
- SEND MAIL: durable path `asme.services.notifications.queue_email(to, subject, body)` (returns the uncommitted OutboxJob; caller commits) -> 'mail.send' handler -> mail.send_email; synchronous path `mail.send_email(settings(), to, subject, body)` wrapped in try/except. To support HTML/attachments/multiple recipients extend `send_email` in asme/integrations/mail.py (single choke point) and the 'mail.send' payload shape together.
- SWAP THE WORKER BACKEND (e.g. RQ/Celery): per the outbox docstring the seam is `_handlers` + `enqueue`/`process_pending` in asme/jobs/outbox.py; producers only import `enqueue`, consumers only register via `handler`. Keep `process_pending()` callable synchronously because tests and admin.launchpad_reevaluate depend on it.
- OVERRIDE INTEGRATIONS IN TESTS: calendar via `app.extensions["asme_calendar_provider"] = FakeProvider()`; outbox handlers via `@outbox.handler("test.x")` inside the test; settings via `make_app(**overrides)` / `create_app(**overrides)` (any Settings field name); events via `events.subscribe("*", fn)` / `events.clear()`.

**Risks / known defects**

- Duplicate execution is by design, not prevented: no idempotency key on outbox_jobs, the 10-minute stale-lock release re-queues any job still legitimately running, and admin retry resets attempts. Every handler MUST tolerate re-runs; only calendar.create_event has an explicit guard (sync.status=='synced' and external_id).
- ensure_recurring() ignores status='failed', so once stock.reconcile fails max_attempts(3) times it is re-enqueued on every poll (default every 15s) forever, filling outbox_jobs and the log with failures.
- The worker thread starts only on the first HTTP request per gunicorn process and is a daemon thread: on an idle deployment nothing runs; on process exit an in-flight job stays 'running' until the 10-min release. Procfile declares no dedicated worker process, and with `--workers 2` two threads poll the same table (safe via conditional UPDATE, but on SQLite concurrent writers can raise 'database is locked' which surfaces as retries/attempt burn).
- Unknown job kinds (typos, handler module not imported) are only detected at run time: each attempt fails with RuntimeError and burns the backoff schedule (30s..1h) before ending 'failed'.
- process_pending()/_claim() call db.session.commit() on the shared scoped session; calling process_pending() from request code with uncommitted changes (admin.launchpad_reevaluate commits first, correctly) would commit them as a side effect. On handler failure the whole session is rolled back, so a handler that raised after creating rows without committing loses them (calendar.create_event creates the CalendarSync row in-handler when none exists; a non-CalendarError failure discards it).
- Event bus subscribers run synchronously in the request and commit the session (engine.evaluate commit=True). Any future emit placed before the service's own commit would let the onboarding engine commit a partial transaction. Subscriber exceptions are swallowed (logged only), so onboarding regressions are silent from the caller's perspective.
- TASK_SIGNED_OFF is defined and subscribed but never emitted; BOOKING_CREATED is emitted but has no subscriber; the ALL_EVENTS docstring claims a test asserts on it and none does - drift between the constant list, emitters and subscribers is unchecked.
- 'calendar.delete_event' has no producer: cancelling/updating/deleting an Event via admin or API never removes or updates the mirrored Google/Outlook event (only the legacy Meeting path deletes via OutlookCalendarProvider directly). 'onboarding.evaluate' and notifications.queue_email are likewise unused.
- mail.send has no pre-check: when SMTP is unconfigured every queued email spends all 5 attempts (~8 minutes of backoff) raising MailNotConfigured before failing. send_email is STARTTLS-only, plain text, single recipient, From always equals smtp_user.
- Outlook free_busy reads calendarView with $top=200 and no @odata.nextLink paging (busy windows beyond 200 events in the window are silently dropped -> double booking) and it truncates returned dateTime strings to 19 chars then stamps them with time_min.tzinfo without sending a Prefer: outlook.timezone header (Graph returns UTC by default), so busy windows may be offset from local time. Verify against Graph behaviour before relying on it.
- GoogleCalendarProvider.service() swallows every exception and returns None, so bad credentials/JSON surface only as the generic 'Google Calendar integration is not configured.' CalendarError; Google status() also reports disabled whenever cfg.calendar_provider != 'google' even if fully configured.
- Assistant/: runs up to 6 synchronous Anthropic calls inside the request with no client timeout (gunicorn --timeout 120 is the only bound); client-supplied `history` is trusted verbatim (cost/prompt-injection surface); tool results ship member names/emails to a third-party API; `_setting` reads os.environ (ANTHROPIC_API_KEY/ANTHROPIC_MODEL) contradicting the config.py 'nothing else reads os.environ' rule, and the env var name ANTHROPIC_MODEL differs from the documented ASME_ASSISTANT_MODEL; the in-code fallback model id 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001' and Settings default 'claude-sonnet-5' were not verified against the current API here. api_ask uses an inline admin check instead of the shared require_role decorator. Several tools query the legacy Transaction/Member tables rather than Loan, so answers may diverge from portal data.
- Logging: `ts` is local time without timezone or milliseconds; configure_logging sets the ROOT logger level (affects all libraries) and installs the JSON handler on root, so every third-party log line becomes JSON; werkzeug access logging is suppressed entirely, making the 'asme.request' record the only per-request log. The handler dedupe flag is process-global, so calling create_app twice with different debug flags keeps the first level semantics only for the handler but re-applies levels.
- handler() decorator overwrites an existing kind silently; a second module registering the same kind (or a test) replaces production behaviour with no warning.
- The `outbox_jobs` payload is free-form JSON with default=str; there is no schema validation, so producer/handler payload drift (renamed keys) fails only at run time via KeyError -> retries -> failed.

## blueprints-web

Six HTML blueprints (public, auth, kiosk, portal, admin, legacy_ops) plus two JSON blueprints (api_v1, api_legacy) are registered in a fixed tuple in asme/blueprints/__init__.py:register_blueprints(app): (public, auth, kiosk, portal, admin, api_v1, api_legacy, legacy_ops). url_prefixes: portal="/portal", admin="/portal/admin", api_v1="/api/v1", api_legacy="/api"; public/auth/kiosk/legacy_ops have none. There are no URL collisions among the un-prefixed blueprints. Auth gating is done exclusively by three decorators in asme/auth/session.py: require_login, require_role(min_role) (ROLE_ORDER guest=0<member=1<team_leader=2<admin=3; unknown roles normalize to "member"), and require_entitlement(key) (admins always pass; shadow mode unless ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE=1). Denials redirect (HTML) to auth.login_page?next=<path>, portal.portal_router, or portal.member_launchpad, or return JSON 401/403 when _wants_json() (path starts with /api/, request.is_json, or Accept has application/json without text/html). Service failures surface as ServiceError(message, code, status, **extra); HTML routes translate via _helpers.flash_error / @on_service_error(default_endpoint) (flash + redirect_to_next), while a number of POST routes have no handler and would 500. Template context is built by read-only builders in _context.py (public_site_context, member_dashboard_context, admin_dashboard_context, legacy_dashboard_context/render_ops_page, frontend_portal_context, build_executive_cards); the only context processor is app-level _inject_globals -> {app_version:"2.0.0", onboarding_enforced}. No HTML error handlers exist anywhere (app or blueprint); only api_v1/api_legacy register bp-level ServiceError (and api_v1 404) handlers. Legacy ops pages gate on settings().legacy_ops_enabled (ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS, default False) by redirecting to a named portal endpoint, but most legacy POST/file routes and GET /export are NOT gated and have NO auth; kiosk /pair/member and /pair/item are also unauthenticated direct-DB writes. Several admin endpoints are redirect stubs (nfc, announcements, content, exports, exports/download.zip) whose endpoint names are still referenced by redirect_to_next in POST handlers and by hardcoded nav links in templates/portal/base.html.

**Key facts**

- Registration order (asme/blueprints/__init__.py:10): public, auth, kiosk, portal, admin, api_v1, api_legacy, legacy_ops. Each module exposes `bp`. Docstring says order does not matter because rules do not overlap (verified: no duplicate path+method among un-prefixed blueprints).
- Blueprint names/prefixes: public (None), auth (None), kiosk (None), portal ('/portal'), admin ('/portal/admin'), api_v1 ('/api/v1'), api_legacy ('/api'), legacy_ops (None).
- Decorator semantics (asme/auth/session.py): require_login -> _deny_login() if current_auth_user() is None. require_role(min_role) -> _deny_login() if anonymous, _deny_role() if role_allows(user.role, min_role) is False. require_entitlement(key) -> _deny_login() if anonymous; passes if entitlements.has_entitlement(user,key) (admins always True); if settings().onboarding_enforce -> entitlement_denied_response(key,user); else appends key to g.shadow_entitlement_blocks, logs 'shadow-block', and proceeds.
- Denial responses: _deny_login HTML = flash('Please log in to continue.','error') + 302 to url_for('auth.login_page', next=request.path) (query string dropped); JSON = 401 {"ok":false,"code":"login_required","error":"Login required."}. _deny_role HTML = flash + 302 to portal.portal_router; JSON = 403 {"ok":false,"code":"forbidden","error":"You do not have permission to do that."}. entitlement_denied_response HTML = flash + 302 to portal.member_launchpad; JSON = 403 {"ok":false,"code":"entitlement_required","entitlement":key,"phase":<phase.key|null>,"phase_name":<phase.name|null>,"error":...}.
- current_auth_user() (session.py:57) is memoized on g per request; it signs the user out (session.clear()) when: no auth_user_id; session['auth_boot_token'] != settings().app_boot_token; user missing/inactive; or idle timeout exceeded (admin_session_idle_minutes=30 default via ASME_ADMIN_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES for role>=admin, member_session_idle_minutes=240 via ASME_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES otherwise; 0 disables). It refreshes session['auth_last_seen_ts'] on every call. app_boot_token defaults to uuid4().hex per process unless ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN is set.
- Session keys used by web blueprints: auth_user_id, auth_user_role, auth_boot_token, auth_login_ts, auth_last_seen_ts (set by sign_in_user, which also session.clear()s and sets session.permanent=False), active_member_id (legacy kiosk 'active member'; set by sign_in_user when a Member links, and by legacy_ops POST /session/member), checkin_candidate_ids (kiosk multi-event check-in), latest_roster_credentials_file (READ ONLY in admin.members_page; never written anywhere in repo).
- App-level request hooks (asme/__init__.py:_register_request_hooks): before_request _start_worker_once (starts outbox worker thread on first request if cfg.outbox_worker_enabled and not testing; per-process); before_request _enforce_auth_session_guardrails (calls current_auth_user() on every request except paths starting with '/static/' or '/arm-sim' and exactly '/healthz'); after_request _no_cache_for_authenticated (if session.auth_user_id: Cache-Control 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, max-age=0, private', Pragma no-cache, Expires 0, Vary += Cookie; always setdefault X-Content-Type-Options=nosniff, X-Frame-Options=SAMEORIGIN, Referrer-Policy=strict-origin-when-cross-origin); teardown_appcontext db.session.remove(). logging_setup adds before_request _start_timer (g.request_id from X-Request-Id header or uuid4 hex[:12]) and after_request _log_request (JSON log line with request_id, method, path, status, duration_ms, user_id, endpoint, shadow_blocks; sets X-Request-Id response header; skips logging for /static/ and /healthz).
- Template context processor: exactly one, app-level `_inject_globals` -> {'app_version': '2.0.0', 'onboarding_enforced': cfg.onboarding_enforce}. No blueprint-level context processors, template filters, or template globals are registered. No app- or blueprint-level HTML errorhandlers (Flask defaults for 404/405/500). api_v1 has @bp.errorhandler(ServiceError) and @bp.errorhandler(404); api_legacy has @bp.errorhandler(ServiceError) (out of scope).
- Flask config set in create_app: SETTINGS=<Settings>, SECRET_KEY=cfg.secret_key (ASME_SECRET_KEY, default 'asme-dev-secret'; production refuses defaults), SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URI=cfg.database_url (ASME_DATABASE_URL default sqlite:///inventory.db), SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE (ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE default Lax), SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY=True, SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE (ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE default bool(RENDER env)), SESSION_PERMANENT=False, TEMPLATES_AUTO_RELOAD, TESTING, MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH=max(ASME_PRINT_MAX_UPLOAD_MB*1MB (default 50MB), 64MB). template_folder=<repo>/templates, static_folder=<repo>/static, instance_path=<repo>/instance.
- Settings keys read by these blueprints (asme/config.py Settings dataclass -> env var): legacy_ops_enabled<-ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS (False); onboarding_enforce<-ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE (False); outbox_worker_enabled<-ASME_OUTBOX_WORKER (True); calendar_provider<-ASME_CALENDAR_PROVIDER ('google'|'outlook', default google); calendar_scheduling_days<-CALENDAR_SCHEDULING_DAYS (14); google_calendar_embed_url<-ASME_GOOGLE_CALENDAR_EMBED_URL; outlook_calendar_embed_url<-ASME_OUTLOOK_CALENDAR_EMBED_URL; google_service_account_json<-GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_JSON; outlook_tenant_id/client_id/client_secret/calendar_user/calendar_id/calendar_robotics_id/calendar_fluids_id/calendar_tz<-ASME_OUTLOOK_*; smtp_user/smtp_pass<-ASME_SMTP_USER/PASS; cancel_notify_to<-ASME_CANCEL_NOTIFY_TO; h2s_print_cmd/p1s_print_cmd<-ASME_H2S_PRINT_CMD/ASME_P1S_PRINT_CMD (also loadable from instance/print_commands.env); default_admin_email<-ASME_DEFAULT_ADMIN_EMAIL; shared_admin_email/password<-ASME_DEFAULT_ADMIN_EMAIL|ASME_SHARED_ADMIN_EMAIL|ASME_ADMIN_EMAIL (and *_PASSWORD); admin_emails<-ASME_ADMIN_EMAILS (comma list, lowercased frozenset).
- ServiceError (asme/services/errors.py): ServiceError(message, code='bad_request', status=400, **extra) with .message/.code/.status/.extra and to_dict() -> {ok:false, code, error, **extra}. Subclasses: NotFound(404,'not_found'), Conflict(409,'conflict'), Forbidden(403,'forbidden'), Validation(400,'validation', field=). _helpers.flash_error(exc) flashes exc.message as 'error' and each entry of exc.extra['errors'] as 'info'. _helpers.on_service_error(default_endpoint, **values) wraps a view: ServiceError -> flash_error + redirect_to_next(default_endpoint, **values).
- redirect_to_next(default_endpoint, **values) (asme/utils/http.py:30): uses safe_next_url(request.form.get('next') or request.args.get('next')); safe_next_url accepts only strings starting with '/' and not '//' (relative in-app paths), else falls back to url_for(default_endpoint). legacy_ops.redirect_home(page) uses the same rule but only reads request.form['next'] and maps page -> PAGE_ENDPOINTS (dashboard, attendance, inventory, prints, activity, calendar, scan, my_items, admin_nfc; unknown -> legacy_ops.dashboard_page).
- Flash categories used: 'error', 'success', 'info' (only these three).
- Context builders (asme/blueprints/_context.py) are read-only by design (docstring), except public_site_context -> current_auth_user() may clear the session, and current_user_member() may auto-link and COMMIT user.member_id by email match. public_site_context(page_title) keys: page_title, current_user, projects (all Project ordered created_at desc), project_filters, announcements (content.public_announcements(limit=5)), executive_cards (build_executive_cards()). member_dashboard_context() keys: current_user, member_profile, items (active Items), open_checkouts (inventory.open_loans_for(user=,member=)), print_requests (PrintRequest by user_id), portal_print_printers (PRINT_REQUEST_PRINTERS), upcoming_events (scheduling.upcoming_events(limit=25)), calendar_embed_url, announcements (content.member_announcements(limit=8)), my_help_messages (ContactMessage kind='help' by user_id, limit 40), launchpad (onboarding engine.summary_for_user(user)), onboarding_enforced. admin_dashboard_context() keys: current_user, members, users, tags (NFCTag), items, item_primary_tags, transactions (120), print_requests (120), events (120), attendance_records (200), projects, contact_messages (60), audit_logs (audit.recent(150)), roles ['member','team_leader','admin'], print_request_statuses, portal_print_printers, item_types, active_members_count, checked_out_now_count (sum Transaction.qty where status='OUT'), attendance_today_count, overdue_items_count, low_stock_items_count, upcoming_meetings_count, pending_print_count, calendar_provider, google_embed_set, outlook_embed_set.
- legacy_dashboard_context(transaction_limit=15) keys: members, items, recent_transactions, today_attendance (attendance.today_unique_scans()), attendance_count, queues (fabrication.queue_snapshot()), default_due, today, low_stock_count (available_qty<=2), active_prints_count, h2s_waiting_count, p1s_waiting_count. render_ops_page(template_name, active_page, page_title, page_subtitle, transaction_limit=15) adds active_page, page_title, page_subtitle, active_member (get_active_member()), active_member_is_admin, h2s/p1s_print_cmd_configured/_value, print_commands_env_file (<instance>/print_commands.env). frontend_portal_context() keys: active_member, active_member_is_admin, members, item_count, available_total, my_open_count, club_mission, club_highlights, project_showcase.
- is_admin_member(member) (legacy kiosk admin check): True if member.email in settings().admin_emails, or member.member_class contains any of 'admin','officer','lead','president','chair'. get_active_member() reads session['active_member_id']. Legacy ops routes use ONLY this check (no require_* decorators).
- portal.portal_router (GET /portal, require_login): admin -> 302 admin.dashboard; otherwise 302 portal.member_dashboard. auth.login_page POST success -> next or portal.portal_router; auth.admin_login_page success -> next or admin.dashboard.
- active_page values expected by templates/portal/base.html nav: admin_dashboard, admin_launchpad, admin_members, admin_attendance, admin_inventory, admin_prints, admin_calendar, admin_nfc, admin_exports, admin_announcements, admin_settings, member_dashboard, member_launchpad, member_launchpad_lead, member_inventory, member_prints, member_calendar, member_profile; base.html switches admin/member theme on `current_user.role == 'admin' and not active_page.startswith('member_')`. Nav hrefs are hardcoded literal paths (not url_for). templates/ops/base.html likewise hardcodes /dashboard, /attendance, /inventory, /scan, /my-items, /prints, /activity, /calendar, /admin/nfc, /settings.
- Templates rendered by web blueprints: site/{landing,events,gallery,who_we_are,executive_team,projects,project_detail,contact,join,sponsors,kiosk,checkin,checkin_select,checkin_success,signup,login,admin_login,forgot_password,reset_password}.html; portal/{member_dashboard_home,member_inventory,member_item_detail,member_checkouts,member_prints,member_print_detail,member_calendar,member_schedule,member_help,member_profile,member_launchpad,admin_launchpad,admin_dashboard_home,admin_members,admin_attendance,admin_inventory,admin_prints,admin_calendar,admin_settings}.html; ops/{dashboard,attendance,inventory,prints,activity,settings,scan,my_items,admin_nfc,calendar}.html; front/portal.html; pair_member.html and pair_item.html (templates root). Templates on disk with NO rendering route: templates/index.html, templates/site/home.html, templates/portal/admin_announcements.html, templates/portal/admin_exports.html, templates/portal/admin_nfc.html.
- GET / renders site/landing.html with NO context (no public_site_context); landing.html does not extend site/base.html and never references current_user.
- Static passthrough routes in public.py use @bp.route with default methods (GET, HEAD, OPTIONS): /arm-sim, /arm-sim/<path:subpath> (static/arm-sim), /stl/<path:subpath> (static/arm-sim/stl), /hdri/<path:subpath> (static/arm-sim/hdri). Only '/arm-sim' prefix (and /static/, /healthz) skips the auth-guardrail before_request; /stl and /hdri do not.
- Kiosk flow: GET /kiosk (anonymous) renders site/kiosk.html with inventory_next=url_for(portal.member_inventory) and admin_next=url_for(admin.dashboard); logged-in users are redirected to portal.member_inventory. GET /checkin (require_login): attendance.candidate_events(); 0 -> site/checkin.html active_event=None; 1 -> attendance.check_user_into_event(user, event, method='shared_tag') then 302 kiosk.checkin_success?event_id=&status=new|existing; >1 -> session['checkin_candidate_ids'] then 302 kiosk.checkin_select.
- Legacy gate: legacy_ops._gate(portal_endpoint) returns redirect(url_for(portal_endpoint)) when settings().legacy_ops_enabled is False, else None. Gated GET pages map: /dashboard->portal.portal_router, /attendance->admin.attendance_page, /inventory->portal.member_inventory, /prints->portal.member_prints, /activity->admin.inventory_page, /settings->admin.settings_page, /my-items->portal.member_checkouts, /admin/nfc and POST /admin/nfc/register->admin.nfc_page (itself a redirect to admin.dashboard), POST /admin/inventory/correct->admin.inventory_page, /calendar and its 4 sub-routes->portal.member_calendar. /app and /scan redirect to literal '/kiosk' when disabled. NOT gated at all (run regardless of flag, no auth): POST /session/member, POST /session/member/clear, POST /attendance/scan, POST /transact, POST /print/submit, POST /print/job/<id>/complete|fail|delete, GET /print/job/<id>/download|open, GET /export.
- Legacy raw-HTML responses: GET /calendar/cancel/confirm/<token> returns 404 '<h3>Cancellation link is invalid or already used.</h3>...' when token unknown, 500 '<h3>Cancellation could not be completed.</h3><p>{error}</p>...' when scheduling.legacy_confirm_cancel returns an error string, else 200 '<h3>Cancellation confirmed.</h3>...'. GET /calendar/cancel/reject/<token> returns 404 same style or 200 '<h3>Cancellation request rejected.</h3>...'. These are the only non-template, non-redirect HTML bodies in the web blueprints.
- File/CSV/PDF responses: admin.import_roster (POST) -> text/csv attachment member_credentials_<UTC %Y%m%d_%H%M%S>.csv columns name,member_id,username,password; admin.export_fresh_credentials (POST) -> same CSV shape; admin.export_fresh_credentials_pdf (POST) -> application/pdf member_credentials_name_last_nfc_user_pass_<ts>.pdf via roster.credentials_pdf_bytes (reportlab; failure -> flash + redirect); admin.download_roster_credentials (GET) -> send_file from roster.roster_credentials_dir()/Path(filename).name as text/csv; admin.attendance_export -> attendance_records.csv (attendance.EXPORT_HEADERS = record_id,event_id,event_title,user_id,user_name,tag_uid,method,checkin_time); admin.inventory_item_nfc_pdf -> inventory_item_nfc_<date>.pdf; admin.inventory_treasury_report -> inventory_treasury_report.csv (inventory.TREASURY_REPORT_HEADERS); legacy_ops.export_excel -> instance/inventory_export.xlsx via pandas/openpyxl with sheets Members, Items, Transactions, ItemTags, Attendance, PrintJobs, Meetings; legacy print job download/open -> send_file(job.file_path).

| Method | Path | Endpoint | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/` | `public.public_home -> render site/landing.html (NO context builder; no current_user in context)` | none |
| GET | `/healthz` | `public.health_check -> 200 JSON {"ok": true, "service": "asme-web"}; skipped by auth-guardrail hook and request log` | none |
| GET | `/events` | `public.public_events -> render site/events.html [public_site_context('Events')]` | none |
| GET | `/gallery` | `public.public_gallery -> render site/gallery.html [public_site_context('Gallery') + gallery_images, makeathon_images scanned from static/images/{gallery,makeathon} with optional _thumbs/<stem>.jpg; exts jpg/jpeg/png/webp/gif]` | none |
| GET,HEAD,OPTIONS | `/arm-sim/<path:subpath>` | `public.arm_sim_assets -> send_from_directory(static/arm-sim, subpath)` | none (path prefix /arm-sim also skips auth-guardrail before_request) |
| GET,HEAD,OPTIONS | `/arm-sim` | `public.arm_sim_index -> send_from_directory(static/arm-sim, 'index.html')` | none |
| GET,HEAD,OPTIONS | `/stl/<path:subpath>` | `public.arm_sim_stl -> send_from_directory(static/arm-sim/stl, subpath)` | none |
| GET,HEAD,OPTIONS | `/hdri/<path:subpath>` | `public.arm_sim_hdri -> send_from_directory(static/arm-sim/hdri, subpath)` | none |
| GET | `/who-we-are` | `public.public_who_we_are -> render site/who_we_are.html [public_site_context + mission=FRONT_CLUB_MISSION, highlights=FRONT_CLUB_HIGHLIGHTS, asme_facts=FRONT_ABOUT_ASME_FACTS, uiowa_projects=FRONT_UIOWA_CURRENT_PROJECTS]` | none |
| GET | `/about` | `public.public_about_alias -> 302 public.public_who_we_are` | none |
| GET | `/executive-team` | `public.public_executive_team -> render site/executive_team.html [public_site_context('Executive Team')]` | none |
| GET | `/exec` | `public.public_exec_alias -> 302 public.public_executive_team` | none |
| GET | `/projects` | `public.public_projects -> render site/projects.html [public_site_context('Projects')]` | none |
| GET | `/projects/<slug>` | `public.public_project_detail -> Project.query.filter_by(slug=slug.strip().lower()).first(); missing -> flash('Project not found.','error') + 302 public.public_projects; render site/project_detail.html [+ project, project_gallery=parse_json_list(project.gallery_json), project_timeline=parse_json_list(project.timeline), project_model_url=static/models/projects/<slug>.glb if exists else static/models/hero/humanoid-soldering.glb]` | none |
| GET,POST | `/contact` | `public.public_contact -> _inbox_form: render site/contact.html; POST content.create_contact_message(name, email, message=form.message or '', kind='contact', target=None, subject=form.subject.strip() or None); ServiceError -> flash_error + re-render; success flash 'Message sent. Our admin team will follow up.' + 302 public.public_contact` | none |
| GET | `/socials` | `public.public_socials -> 302 public.public_contact` | none |
| GET,POST | `/join` | `public.public_join -> render site/join.html; POST requires form.interest (else flash + re-render); content.create_contact_message(name, email, message=form.message or 'Interested in: <interest>', kind='join', target='membership', subject='Join Interest: <interest[:120]>'); ServiceError -> generic flash 'Name, email, and interest area are required.' (service message discarded) + re-render; success 302 public.public_join` | none |
| GET,POST | `/sponsors` | `public.public_sponsors -> _inbox_form: render site/sponsors.html; POST content.create_contact_message(kind='sponsor', target='sponsorship', subject='Sponsorship Inquiry', message=form.message or ''); success 302 public.public_sponsors` | none |
| GET,POST | `/signup` | `auth.signup_page -> render site/signup.html; POST identity.signup(name, email, password, role=form.role or 'member'); success 302 auth.login_page  [auth.py, outside listed set]` | none |
| GET,POST | `/login` | `auth.login_page -> render site/login.html [next_url=safe_next_url(args.next or form.next)]; POST rate-limited (LoginRateLimiter by ip+identifier), identity.authenticate(identifier, password), sign_in_user; 302 next_url or portal.portal_router  [auth.py]` | none |
| GET,POST | `/admin-login` | `auth.admin_login_page -> render site/admin_login.html; already-admin -> 302 admin.dashboard; POST accepts shared env admin creds (identity.ensure_shared_admin_user) or identity.authenticate with role>=admin; 302 next_url or admin.dashboard  [auth.py]` | none |
| GET | `/admin` | `auth.admin_home -> 302 admin.dashboard if admin else 302 auth.admin_login_page  [auth.py]` | none |
| GET,POST | `/logout` | `auth.logout_page -> sign_out_user(); flash; 302 public.public_home with no-store headers  [auth.py]` | none |
| GET,POST | `/forgot-password` | `auth.forgot_password_page -> render site/forgot_password.html; POST identity.create_password_reset(user) and FLASHES the external reset link; 302 self  [auth.py]` | none |
| GET,POST | `/reset-password/<token>` | `auth.reset_password_page -> identity.find_valid_reset(token) (invalid -> 302 auth.forgot_password_page); render site/reset_password.html; POST identity.consume_password_reset(row, password, confirm_password); success 302 auth.login_page  [auth.py]` | none |
| GET | `/kiosk` | `kiosk.kiosk_entry -> logged in: 302 portal.member_inventory; else render site/kiosk.html [public_site_context('Kiosk Login') + inventory_next=url_for(portal.member_inventory), admin_next=url_for(admin.dashboard)]` | none |
| GET | `/checkin` | `kiosk.checkin_entry -> attendance.candidate_events(); none -> render site/checkin.html [active_event=None]; one -> attendance.check_user_into_event(user, event, method='shared_tag') then 302 kiosk.checkin_success?event_id=<id>&status=new|existing; many -> session['checkin_candidate_ids']=[ids] then 302 kiosk.checkin_select` | require_login |
| GET,POST | `/checkin/select` | `kiosk.checkin_select -> reads session['checkin_candidate_ids'] (empty -> flash info + 302 kiosk.checkin_entry); GET render site/checkin_select.html [candidate_events]; POST form.event_id must be in candidates -> attendance.check_user_into_event(..., method='shared_tag'), pop session key, 302 kiosk.checkin_success` | require_login |
| GET | `/checkin/success` | `kiosk.checkin_success -> args event_id, status (default 'new'); missing event -> flash + 302 kiosk.checkin_entry; render site/checkin_success.html [event, status]` | require_login |
| GET,POST | `/pair/member` | `kiosk.pair_member -> GET render pair_member.html [members, paired (Member.nfc_tag IS NOT NULL)]; POST form member_id, tag: uniqueness checks vs Member.nfc_tag, ItemTag.tag_value (lower), Item.nfc_tag (lower), NFCTag.tag_uid active (lower); then DIRECT WRITE member.nfc_tag=tag; db.session.commit(); 302 self` | NONE (unauthenticated write; not legacy-flag gated) |
| GET,POST | `/pair/item` | `kiosk.pair_item -> GET render pair_item.html [items, paired]; POST form item_id, tag: checks Item.nfc_tag, NFCTag.tag_uid (any, lower), ItemTag mapping; DIRECT WRITE item.nfc_tag=tag and insert ItemTag(item_id, tag_value=tag, source='pair_item_page') if unmapped; commit; 302 self` | NONE (unauthenticated write; not legacy-flag gated) |
| GET | `/portal` | `portal.portal_router -> admin: 302 admin.dashboard; else 302 portal.member_dashboard` | require_login |
| GET | `/portal/member` | `portal.member_dashboard -> render portal/member_dashboard_home.html [member_dashboard_context + page_title='Member Dashboard', active_page='member_dashboard', tile_counts{inventory,my_items,prints,upcoming_events,announcements}]` | require_role('member') |
| GET | `/portal/member/inventory` | `portal.member_inventory -> render portal/member_inventory.html [active_page='member_inventory'; ?q= filters items in Python over name/category/location/description/notes; context.query]` | require_role('member') |
| GET | `/portal/member/items/<int:item_id>` | `portal.member_item_detail -> db.session.get(Item); missing -> flash + 302 portal.member_inventory; inventory.get_open_loan(item.id, user=, member=); render portal/member_item_detail.html [item, open_tx; active_page='member_inventory']` | require_role('member') |
| GET | `/portal/member/inventory/<int:item_id>` | `portal.member_item_detail (second rule, same endpoint)` | require_role('member') |
| GET | `/portal/member/checkouts` | `portal.member_checkouts -> render portal/member_checkouts.html ['My Items', active_page='member_my_items']` | require_role('member') |
| GET | `/portal/member/my-items` | `portal.member_checkouts (second rule, same endpoint)` | require_role('member') |
| GET | `/portal/member/prints` | `portal.member_prints -> render portal/member_prints.html ['3D Printing', active_page='member_prints']` | require_role('member') |
| GET | `/portal/member/prints/<int:request_id>` | `portal.member_print_detail -> PrintRequest must exist AND row.user_id == current user id else flash 'Print request not found.' + 302 portal.member_prints; render portal/member_print_detail.html [print_request]` | require_role('member') + ownership check |
| GET | `/portal/member/calendar` | `portal.member_calendar -> render portal/member_calendar.html [team_mode=role_allows(user.role,'team_leader'), meeting_rooms=MEETING_ROOMS, calendar_provider=settings().calendar_provider; active_page='member_calendar']` | require_role('member') |
| GET,POST | `/portal/member/schedule` | `portal.member_schedule -> render portal/member_schedule.html [meeting_rooms, calendar_provider, google_schedule_enabled=scheduling.provider_status().enabled, google_schedule_errors, duration_options [30,60,90], range_options [7,14], form_values{team_name,duration,days,location,notes}, slots]; POST form action='preview'(default)|'book', team_name required, duration in {30,60,90} else 60, days in {7,14} else 14, location via scheduling.normalize_meeting_room; preview -> scheduling.compute_available_slots(duration, days, location); book -> requires provider enabled, scheduling.book_slot(current_user, team_name, form.slot_token, notes=) ; ServiceError -> flash_error + recompute slots + re-render; success flash + 302 portal.member_calendar` | require_role('team_leader') + require_entitlement('room_booking') [shadow unless |
| GET | `/portal/leader/schedule` | `portal.leader_schedule_alias -> 302 portal.member_schedule` | require_role('team_leader') |
