| POST | `/calendar/book` | `legacy_ops.book_meeting -> scheduling.legacy_book_meeting(request.form) -> (meeting, calendar_error, sync_configured, config_has_inputs); ServiceError -> flash_error; flash variant by sync outcome; redirect_home('calendar')` | no auth; disabled -> 302 portal.member_calendar |
| POST | `/calendar/meeting/<int:meeting_id>/cancel` | `legacy_ops.request_meeting_cancel -> scheduling.legacy_request_cancel(meeting) -> token (ServiceError -> flash info); notifications.send_meeting_cancel_confirmation_email(meeting, confirm_url=url_for(legacy_ops.confirm_meeting_cancel,_external), reject_url=...) -> error|None; on error scheduling.legacy_clear_cancel(meeting) + flash; redirect_home('calendar')` | no auth; disabled -> 302 portal.member_calendar |
| GET | `/calendar/cancel/confirm/<token>` | `legacy_ops.confirm_meeting_cancel -> scheduling.find_meeting_by_cancel_token(token); unknown -> raw HTML 404; scheduling.legacy_confirm_cancel(meeting) error -> raw HTML 500; else raw HTML 200 'Cancellation confirmed.'` | no auth (token-bearer); disabled -> 302 portal.member_calendar |
| GET | `/calendar/cancel/reject/<token>` | `legacy_ops.reject_meeting_cancel -> unknown token -> raw HTML 404; scheduling.legacy_clear_cancel(meeting); raw HTML 200 'Cancellation request rejected.'` | no auth (token-bearer); disabled -> 302 portal.member_calendar |
| POST | `/attendance/scan` | `legacy_ops.attendance_scan -> attendance.legacy_scan(form.uid) -> (member, first_today); ServiceError -> flash(message,'error'); redirect_home('attendance')` | NONE (no auth, NOT legacy-flag gated) |
| POST | `/transact` | `legacy_ops.transact -> identity.resolve_member(form.member_tag, form.member_id), inventory.resolve_item(form.item_tag, form.item_id), form.action in {checkout, return}, qty=parse_int(form.qty,1); inventory.legacy_transact(member=, item=, action=, qty=, notes=, due_date=parse_due_date(form.due_date), auth_user=current_auth_user()); ServiceError -> flash_error; redirect_home('inventory')` | NONE (no auth, NOT legacy-flag gated) |
| POST | `/print/submit` | `legacy_ops.submit_print_job -> identity.resolve_member(...); fabrication.submit_print_job(member, form.printer_type, files.gcode_file, notes=) -> (job, started); ServiceError -> flash_error; redirect_home('prints')` | NONE (no auth, NOT legacy-flag gated) |
| POST | `/print/job/<int:job_id>/complete` | `legacy_ops.complete_print_job -> fabrication.finish_print_job(job, 'done'); redirect_home('prints')` | NONE (no auth, NOT legacy-flag gated) |
| POST | `/print/job/<int:job_id>/fail` | `legacy_ops.fail_print_job -> fabrication.finish_print_job(job, 'failed'); redirect_home('prints')` | NONE (no auth, NOT legacy-flag gated) |
| GET | `/print/job/<int:job_id>/download` | `legacy_ops.download_print_job -> send_file(job.file_path, as_attachment=True, download_name=job.file_name); missing -> flash + redirect_home('prints')` | NONE (no auth, NOT legacy-flag gated) |
| GET | `/print/job/<int:job_id>/open` | `legacy_ops.open_print_job -> send_file(job.file_path, inline, mimetype=guess_type(file_name) or application/octet-stream)` | NONE (no auth, NOT legacy-flag gated) |
| POST | `/print/job/<int:job_id>/delete` | `legacy_ops.delete_print_job -> fabrication.delete_print_job_with_file(job) -> {file_error, file_removed, file_name}; ServiceError -> flash_error; redirect_home('prints')` | NONE (no auth, NOT legacy-flag gated) |
| GET | `/export` | `legacy_ops.export_excel -> pandas/openpyxl (ImportError -> flash + 302 legacy_ops.dashboard_page); dumps ALL Members (id,name,email,class,nfc_tag,created_at), Items, Transactions, ItemTags, AttendanceScans, PrintJobs (incl. file_path), Meetings (incl. cancel_request_token) to <instance>/inventory_export.xlsx and send_file(as_attachment, 'inventory_export.xlsx')` | NONE (no auth, NOT legacy-flag gated) |

**Extension points**

- Add a blueprint: create asme/blueprints/<name>.py exposing `bp = Blueprint(...)` and append the module to the tuple in asme/blueprints/__init__.py:register_blueprints (line 10). Nothing else discovers blueprints.
- Add a portal/admin page: use portal._page(template, title, active_page, **extra) or admin._page(...) so the base template gets page_title/active_page plus the full dashboard context; add the new active_page key to the hardcoded nav in templates/portal/base.html (nav uses literal hrefs, not url_for).
- Add a public page: build context with _context.public_site_context(page_title) (provides current_user, projects, announcements, executive_cards used by site/base.html).
- Add a mutating HTML POST route: stack decorators in this order: @bp.post -> @require_role(...) [-> @require_entitlement(KEY)] -> @on_service_error('<default_endpoint>') and finish with redirect_to_next('<default_endpoint>'); forms may carry a hidden `next` (relative path) to override the redirect.
- Gate on a new entitlement: add ENT_* constant to asme/constants.py and include it in ALL_ENTITLEMENTS (whitelist for admin grant/revoke UI), then decorate with @require_entitlement(KEY); remember shadow mode semantics.
- Implement the admin stubs without breaking references: replace the redirect bodies of admin.nfc_page, admin.announcements_page, admin.content_page, admin.exports_page, admin.exports_zip while KEEPING the endpoint names (used by redirect_to_next defaults in announcement_update/delete, project_save, contact_status, by legacy _gate targets, and by templates/portal/base.html nav). Unused templates portal/admin_nfc.html, admin_announcements.html, admin_exports.html already exist on disk.
- Global template variables: extend the single app-level context processor _inject_globals in asme/__init__.py:_register_request_hooks (currently app_version, onboarding_enforced).
- Global HTML error pages: none exist; add @app.errorhandler(404/500) in create_app (HTML blueprints have no errorhandlers; api_v1/api_legacy own their JSON ServiceError handlers).
- Legacy compatibility: legacy_ops.PAGE_ENDPOINTS maps page keys to endpoints for redirect_home(); legacy_ops._gate(portal_endpoint) is the single place mapping each legacy page to its portal replacement when ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS is off.
- Public inbox forms: public._inbox_form(template, page_title, kind, target, subject_fn, success_message, endpoint) is the pattern for new contact-style forms; CONTACT_KINDS in constants.py = (contact, join, sponsor, help).
- Auth decision helpers for new JSON-or-HTML routes: session._wants_json() decides response shape; ServiceError.to_dict() gives the JSON error body {ok:false, code, error, **extra}.
- Executive team content: EXEC_PROFILE_OVERRIDES (keys 'email:<addr>' / 'name:<normalized name>') and MANUAL_EXECUTIVE_PROFILES in asme/content_data.py override/augment User.exec_title/exec_message/headshot_url without DB changes.

**Risks / known defects**

- Unauthenticated write endpoints reachable regardless of ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS: kiosk GET/POST /pair/member and /pair/item (direct writes to Member.nfc_tag, Item.nfc_tag, ItemTag), legacy_ops POST /session/member, /session/member/clear, /attendance/scan, /transact (checkout/return stock), /print/submit (file upload), /print/job/<id>/complete|fail|delete, and GET /print/job/<id>/download|open (serves arbitrary stored job files). None call require_login/require_role and none check the legacy flag.
- GET /export (legacy_ops.export_excel) is unauthenticated, not legacy-gated, and dumps every Member email + NFC UID, all Transactions, PrintJob file paths, and Meeting cancel_request_token values to an xlsx (also writes <instance>/inventory_export.xlsx on disk). Highest-priority exposure in this subsystem.
- Credential leakage via flash messages (rendered into HTML, persisted in the session cookie until displayed): admin.reset_user_password flashes the temporary password; admin.user_invite_link flashes a live reset link; auth.forgot_password_page flashes a reset link to ANY anonymous requester who supplies an active email (no email delivery, so effectively account takeover if the email is known).
- POST /portal/admin/members/export-credentials and /export-credentials-pdf are labelled exports but call identity.build_fresh_member_credentials(), which regenerates usernames, passwords and NFC ids for every active member/team_leader user; a stray click invalidates all member logins.
- Session invalidation on deploy/scale-out: current_auth_user() signs out any session whose auth_boot_token != settings().app_boot_token, and app_boot_token defaults to uuid4().hex per process when ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN is unset. With multiple gunicorn workers or a restart, users are logged out randomly/immediately. Must set ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN in production.
- GET with side effects: portal.member_launchpad calls engine.evaluate_user(user) with commit=True (writes task/phase state and grants/revokes entitlements) on every page view; current_user_member() commits a User.member_id auto-link during 'read' context building; kiosk GET /checkin performs a check-in when exactly one candidate event exists.
- Unhandled ServiceError -> 500 (no on_service_error, no try/except): portal.member_help (content.create_contact_message), admin.import_roster (roster.import_roster_entries), admin.inventory_adjust, admin.resolve_discrepancy, admin.announcement_update/delete, admin.contact_status, admin.unassign_nfc, admin.retry_job, portal.event_rsvp, legacy complete/fail print job. Any validation raised by those services surfaces as a Flask 500 page.
- admin.import_roster performs the import, then if no credentials were produced calls db.session.rollback() — but roster.import_roster_entries may have already committed internally (unverified); partial imports are possible. It also returns a CSV directly, so the `next` redirect convention is not honoured and a failed parse leaves the browser on the members page with a flash.
- Shadow-mode entitlements: ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE defaults to False, so require_entitlement on /portal/member/schedule, /portal/inventory/checkout, /portal/print/request currently always passes (only logs 'shadow-block'). Flipping the flag will start blocking members who have not completed Launchpad phases; admins are exempt.
- Stub endpoints break UX loops: admin POST handlers for announcements/projects/contact redirect_to_next('admin.announcements_page') which itself 302s to /portal/admin; templates/portal/base.html links to /portal/admin/nfc, /portal/admin/exports, /portal/admin/announcements which all bounce to the dashboard. legacy _gate for /admin/nfc targets admin.nfc_page -> double redirect.
- Hardcoded URLs in templates/portal/base.html and templates/ops/base.html (no url_for): changing any url_prefix or path breaks navigation silently; legacy_ops.app_frontend and scan_page also redirect to the literal string '/kiosk'.
- _deny_login redirects to auth.login_page with next=request.path, dropping the query string (e.g. ?tab=history, ?q=...). redirect_to_next/safe_next_url accept any relative path starting with '/', so a crafted form `next` can bounce a user to any in-app URL (not off-site).
- public_join swallows the service's validation message and always flashes 'Name, email, and interest area are required.'; public_sponsors/contact pass message='' when blank and rely on the service to reject — user-facing error text depends on content.create_contact_message internals.
- admin_dashboard_context() runs ~15 queries including full-table loads of members, users, items, projects and NFC tags on EVERY admin page (via admin._page and the pages that call it directly); admin.calendar_page adds 2 more per MEETING_ROOM. Will degrade with roster size.
- portal.member_inventory filters items in Python after loading all active Items; portal.member_print_detail is the only member route with an explicit ownership check — member_item_detail/get_open_loan are scoped to the user, but the launchpad task/team endpoints trust task_key/project_id from the form and rely on service-layer validation.

## blueprints-api

Two JSON surfaces on one Flask app (factory asme.create_app; Flask>=3.0). api_v1 (Blueprint "api_v1", url_prefix "/api/v1", asme/blueprints/api_v1.py) is the versioned surface: session-cookie auth only, gates are require_login / require_role(min_role) / require_entitlement(key) from asme/auth/session.py, success envelope is api_ok() -> {"ok": true, ["message"], ["payload"]}, error envelope is {"ok": false, "code", "error", ...extra} produced either by ServiceError.to_dict() via a blueprint-level @bp.errorhandler(ServiceError) or by api_error()/inline jsonify. Collections use opaque base64url(JSON) cursors ({"after": id} ascending for /items, {"before": id} descending for /checkouts and /print-requests) with limit clamped 1..200 (default 50) and next_cursor null at end; GET /items additionally returns a sha1 ETag over the sorted JSON body and answers If-None-Match with 304. POST /checkouts requires an Idempotency-Key (header, JSON field, or form field, max 120 chars) that is stored on transactions.idempotency_key (unique) and replayed by returning the existing loan. api_legacy (Blueprint "api_legacy", url_prefix "/api") is the kiosk surface: mostly ungated, keyed on session["active_member_id"], returns {"ok": true, "message", "payload": <full bootstrap snapshot>} on writes and has several inconsistent error shapes. There are NO app-level error handlers, so unmatched /api/v1 URLs, 405s, 413s and 500s come back as Flask HTML. Every response carries X-Request-Id; authenticated responses get Cache-Control no-store (overriding the /items private,max-age=0 header).

**Key facts**

- REGISTRATION: asme/blueprints/__init__.py register_blueprints(app) registers in order (public, auth, kiosk, portal, admin, api_v1, api_legacy, legacy_ops) via app.register_blueprint(module.bp). api_v1: bp = Blueprint("api_v1", __name__, url_prefix="/api/v1"). api_legacy: bp = Blueprint("api_legacy", __name__, url_prefix="/api"). legacy_ops and kiosk have no url_prefix. Nothing is conditionally registered; ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS only gates legacy_ops HTML pages via _gate(), NOT the /api/* JSON routes.
- SUCCESS ENVELOPE (asme/utils/http.py): def api_ok(payload=None, message=None, status=200): body = {"ok": True}; adds "message" only if message is not None; adds "payload" only if payload is not None; returns (jsonify(body), status). All v1 endpoints wrap their data under "payload" (e.g. {"ok": true, "payload": {"checkout": {...}}}). No v1 endpoint currently passes message.
- ERROR ENVELOPE (asme/utils/http.py): def api_error(message, status=400, code="bad_request", **extra): body = {"ok": False, "error": message, "code": code}; body.update(extra); returns (jsonify(body), status). Only api_legacy imports/uses api_error; api_v1 relies on raising ServiceError subclasses instead.
- SERVICE ERRORS (asme/services/errors.py): class ServiceError(Exception).__init__(self, message: str, code: str = "bad_request", status: int = 400, **extra) stores .message/.code/.status/.extra; to_dict() -> {"ok": False, "code": self.code, "error": self.message} merged with self.extra. Subclasses: NotFound(message="Not found.", code="not_found", **extra) -> 404; Conflict(message="Conflict.", code="conflict", **extra) -> 409; Forbidden(message="Not allowed.", code="forbidden", **extra) -> 403; Validation(message, code="validation", field=None, **extra) -> 400 and puts field into extra as "field" when given. Extras observed in the wild: "field", "available" (insufficient_stock), "expected" (qty_mismatch), "rule_type" (task_not_manual).
- ERROR HANDLER: both blueprints declare @bp.errorhandler(ServiceError) def _service_error(exc): return jsonify(exc.to_dict()), exc.status. api_v1 also declares @bp.errorhandler(404) -> ({"ok": False, "code": "not_found", "error": "Not found."}, 404) but blueprint-scoped handlers only fire for errors raised inside a matched view of that blueprint; a URL that matches no rule has request.blueprint None so this handler never runs for unknown /api/v1/* paths (and api_v1 never calls abort(404), it raises NotFound). grep confirms no app.errorhandler / register_error_handler anywhere in asme/, so 404-unmatched, 405, 413 (MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH), 500 return Flask default HTML.
- LOGIN GATE (asme/auth/session.py): require_login(view_func) -> if not current_auth_user(): _deny_login(). _deny_login(): if _wants_json(): return jsonify({"ok": False, "code": "login_required", "error": "Login required."}), 401 else flash + redirect to auth.login_page?next=path. _wants_json() is True when request.path.startswith("/api/") OR request.is_json OR ("application/json" in Accept and "text/html" not in Accept). Pinned by tests/test_auth_rbac.py::test_json_routes_get_json_denials (GET /api/v1/me -> 401, code login_required).
- ROLE GATE: require_role(min_role) -> not logged in: _deny_login(); role_allows(user.role, min_role) false: _deny_role() -> JSON: ({"ok": False, "code": "forbidden", "error": "You do not have permission to do that."}, 403). ROLE_ORDER = {"guest": 0, "member": 1, "team_leader": 2, "admin": 3} (asme/constants.py); normalize_role() lowercases/strips and maps unknown -> "member"; role_allows(user_role, required_role) compares ROLE_ORDER values >=.
- ENTITLEMENT GATE: require_entitlement(key) -> not logged in: _deny_login(); ent.has_entitlement(user, key) (admins always True; else any active Entitlement row with that key where revoked_at is NULL and expires_at is NULL or > utcnow) -> pass; else if settings().onboarding_enforce (env ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE, default False): entitlement_denied_response(key, user) -> JSON 403 {"ok": False, "code": "entitlement_required", "entitlement": key, "phase": phase.key|null, "phase_name": phase.name|null, "error": "You need '<key>' to do that. Complete the Launchpad phase that unlocks it."} where phase = entitlements.phase_that_grants(key) (first Phase by order asc, id asc whose grants list contains key); else SHADOW MODE: appends key to g.shadow_entitlement_blocks, logs "shadow-block entitlement=... user_id=... path=..." at INFO on logger asme.auth, and lets the request through (request log line includes shadow_blocks). Pinned by test_enforced_mode_blocks_checkout_with_phase_hint: body["entitlement"] == "shop_access", body["phase"] == "shop_ready".
- ENTITLEMENT KEYS (asme/constants.py): ENT_PORTAL_ACCESS="portal_access", ENT_EVENT_CHECKIN="event_checkin", ENT_SHOP_ACCESS="shop_access", ENT_PRINT_SUBMIT="print_submit", ENT_ROOM_BOOKING="room_booking", ENT_EXTENDED_LOAN="extended_loan_14d", ENT_PRINT_APPROVE="print_approve", ENT_CHECKOUT_SIGNOFF="checkout_signoff"; ALL_ENTITLEMENTS tuple of all eight. entitlements.user_entitlements(user) -> set of active keys, plus ALL_ENTITLEMENTS if role_allows(user.role, "admin").
- DECORATOR ORDER in api_v1: @bp.<method> outermost, then @require_login or @require_role(...), then @require_entitlement(...). So the login/role check runs before the entitlement check; require_entitlement also independently re-checks login.
- AUTH MODEL: session-cookie only (no bearer/API tokens anywhere). current_auth_user() reads session["auth_user_id"], validates session["auth_boot_token"] == settings().app_boot_token (env ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN, else random uuid4 per process -> all sessions invalidated on restart unless set), enforces idle timeout (admin 30 min ASME_ADMIN_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES, member 240 min ASME_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES), requires user.is_active, caches on flask.g. App before_request _enforce_auth_session_guardrails calls current_auth_user() on every request except /static/*, /arm-sim*, /healthz. Tests log in via POST /login data={identifier, password} (tests/conftest.py login()).
- BODY PARSING: api_v1._json() = request.get_json(silent=True) or {} (empty dict when not JSON). value_from_request(key, default=None) (asme/utils/http.py): if request.is_json -> (get_json(silent=True) or {}).get(key, default) else request.form.get(key, default). v1 handlers typically do body.get(k) or value_from_request(k), so JSON and form-encoded both work; note when request.is_json, value_from_request never consults request.form and vice versa. POST /print-requests uses form = request.form if request.form else _json() and reads request.files["print_file"]; POST /roster/import is multipart-only (request.files["roster_file"]).
- IDEMPOTENCY-KEY (asme/utils/http.py): def idempotency_key(): value = request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key").strip(); if empty and request.is_json -> str(json.get("idempotency_key") or "").strip(); if still empty -> request.form.get("idempotency_key").strip(); return value[:120] or None. POST /api/v1/checkouts: missing key -> raise Validation("Idempotency-Key header (or idempotency_key field) is required.", field="idempotency_key", code="idempotency_required") -> 400 {"ok":false,"code":"idempotency_required","error":...,"field":"idempotency_key"}. inventory.checkout(): if idempotency_key: existing = Loan.query.filter_by(idempotency_key=key).first(); if existing: return existing (BEFORE any validation of stock/duplicate; lookup is global, not scoped by user or item). Replay therefore returns 201 with the same serialized loan (test_checkout_and_return_roundtrip asserts same id). Stored in transactions.idempotency_key String(120) nullable unique. Legacy POST /api/checkout passes idempotency_key=str(value_from_request("idempotency_key","") or request.headers.get("Idempotency-Key") or "").strip() or None (optional; field takes precedence over header there).
- PAGINATION HELPERS (api_v1.py, module-private): _encode_cursor(value) = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(json.dumps(value).encode()).decode().rstrip("="); _decode_cursor(raw): None/empty -> None; else re-pad with "="*(-len%4), urlsafe_b64decode, json.loads; any exception -> raise Validation("Invalid cursor.", field="cursor", code="bad_cursor") -> 400 (test_bad_cursor_is_400 uses cursor=!!!). _limit(default=50, maximum=200) = max(1, min(parse_positive_int(request.args.get("limit"), default=default), maximum)); parse_positive_int returns default for empty/non-numeric/<=0 so limit=0 or limit=abc -> 50. Pattern: query.limit(limit+1), has_more = len(rows) > limit, rows = rows[:limit], next_cursor = _encode_cursor({...last id...}) if has_more and rows else None.
- CURSOR SHAPES: GET /items -> {"after": rows[-1].id}, query filter Item.id > int(cursor.get("after", 0)), order Item.id.asc(). GET /checkouts and GET /print-requests -> {"before": rows[-1].id}, filter <Model>.id < int(cursor.get("before", 0)), order id.desc(). Filters (q, state, status, mine) are NOT encoded in the cursor; clients must resend them. GET /events uses _limit() but has no cursor.
- ETAG (GET /api/v1/items only): payload = {"items": [...], "next_cursor": ...}; body = json.dumps({"ok": True, "payload": payload}, sort_keys=True, default=str); etag = hashlib.sha1(body.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() (bare 40-hex, no quotes, no W/); if request.headers.get("If-None-Match") == etag (exact string compare, no list parsing): return Response(status=304, headers={"ETag": etag}); else Response(body, status=200, mimetype="application/json", headers={"ETag": etag, "Cache-Control": "private, max-age=0"}). This endpoint bypasses api_ok/jsonify. App after_request _no_cache_for_authenticated then overwrites Cache-Control to "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, max-age=0, private" and sets Pragma: no-cache, Expires: 0, Vary: Cookie whenever session has auth_user_id (always true here since login is required). Pinned by test_items_etag_and_cursor.
- STATUS CODES USED BY v1: 200 default; 201 for POST /checkouts, POST /print-requests, POST /onboarding/training, POST /bookings, POST /roster/import, POST /checkins when a new record was created (200 on repeat); 304 on /items If-None-Match hit; 400 Validation/bad_cursor/idempotency_required; 401 login_required; 403 forbidden / entitlement_required / Forbidden service errors; 404 NotFound service errors (codes not_found, tag_unknown, item_not_found, loan_not_found, task_not_found, module_not_found); 409 Conflict service errors (already_checked_out, insufficient_stock, loan_closed, qty_mismatch, slot_taken, already_checked_in) and inline choose_event; 503 inline calendar_unavailable.
- GET /api/v1/health -> api_v1.health, no gate. 200 {"ok": true, "payload": {"service": "asme-web", "api": "v1", "time": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() (naive, no tz suffix)}}. Separate unauthenticated GET /healthz (public.health_check) returns {"ok": true, "service": "asme-web"} and is excluded from request logging/auth hook.
- GET /api/v1/me -> require_login. payload = {"user": serialize_user(user, entitlements=summary["entitlements"]), "launchpad": engine.summary_for_user(user)} where summary = {"percent": int, "steps_left": int, "current_phase": <phase payload>|null, "complete": bool, "entitlements": sorted list}. summary_for_user runs a full evaluate() (writes) if the user has no PhaseState rows yet.
- GET /api/v1/onboarding -> require_login. Query: user_id (int via parse_positive_int, default 0): if set and != self, requires role_allows(user.role, "team_leader") else raise Forbidden("Only leads can view another member's Launchpad.") -> 403 code forbidden; unknown id -> NotFound("User not found.") 404. include_chapter=<any truthy string> honoured only when role_allows(user.role, "admin") -> payload["chapter"] = engine.evaluate_chapter(). payload = engine.evaluate_user(target) = engine.evaluate(subject) which mutates TaskState/PhaseState, grants/revokes entitlements, commits, and returns progress(subject) = {"subject": {"type": "user", "id": "<str user id>"}, "tracks": [{"key","name","audience","description","percent","required_total","required_done","steps_left","current_phase","complete","phases": [{"key","name","tagline","description","order","status","est_minutes","grants","tasks": [{"key","name","description","required","status","current","target","est_minutes","rule_type","rule_config","cta_route","cta_label","help_url","evidence","completed_at","note","human"}],"required_total","required_done","steps_left","started_at","completed_at"}]}], "percent": tracks[0].percent or 0, "steps_left": tracks[0].steps_left or 0, "entitlements": sorted list}. Note: a GET performs writes (evaluate commits).
- POST /api/v1/onboarding/tasks/<task_key>/complete -> require_login. Body: subject_type ("user" default | "chapter"), note, user_id (optional; other user requires nothing at the view level beyond existence -> NotFound("User not found."); engine.complete_task enforces: manual task on another user requires team_leader (Forbidden code not_owner), chapter subject requires admin (code admin_only), signoff tasks require rule_config.by_role (default team_leader) (code signoff_role) and not self unless admin (code self_signoff), note_required -> Validation field note, non-HUMAN_RULES task -> Validation code task_not_manual with extra rule_type (400, pinned by test), unknown -> NotFound code task_not_found). int(other_id) is unguarded (non-numeric -> 500). Response 200 {"ok": true, "payload": {"task": task_key, "status": state.status, "completed_at": iso|null}}.
- POST /api/v1/onboarding/training -> require_role("team_leader"). Body: user_id (required; int(...) unguarded; 0/missing -> NotFound("User not found.")), module (TrainingModule.key; unknown -> NotFound code module_not_found), score (optional int 0..100 else Validation field score; int(score) unguarded), note (JSON-only via body.get). engine.record_training may raise Forbidden code signoff_role. Response 201 {"ok": true, "payload": {"id", "module": row.module.key, "score", "expires_at": iso|null}}. Emits events.TRAINING_COMPLETED.
- GET /api/v1/entitlements -> require_login. 200 {"ok": true, "payload": {"entitlements": sorted(list)}}.
- GET /api/v1/items -> require_login + require_entitlement(portal_access). Query: limit, cursor, q (lowercased LIKE %q% across lower(Item.name), lower(coalesce(Item.category,'')), lower(coalesce(Item.location,''))). Only Item.active IS TRUE. payload {"items": [serialize_item], "next_cursor": str|null}. ETag semantics above.
- GET /api/v1/items/by-tag/<path:uid> -> require_login + require_entitlement(portal_access). inventory.find_item_by_tag(clean_tag_value(uid)) resolution order: tag prefixed "item_id:"/"item:" + int -> (item, "payload_item_id"); ItemTag.tag_value case-insensitive -> "item_tags"; Item.nfc_tag case-insensitive -> "legacy_item_nfc_tag"; else (None, "not_found") ("empty" for blank). Not found -> NotFound("Tag not registered to an inventory item.", code="tag_unknown") 404. payload {"item": serialize_item, "resolved_via": str, "open_loan": serialize_loan|null} (open loan for the caller only, via inventory.get_open_loan(item.id, user=user, member=current_user_member(user))).

**Extension points**

- Add new /api/v1 routes directly in asme/blueprints/api_v1.py on the existing bp (Blueprint "api_v1", url_prefix "/api/v1") so they inherit the @bp.errorhandler(ServiceError) and the module-private _json/_limit/_encode_cursor/_decode_cursor helpers. If instead a separate blueprint (e.g. url_prefix "/api/v1/ops") is created, it must be added to the tuple in asme/blueprints/__init__.py register_blueprints and must re-declare its own @bp.errorhandler(ServiceError) (blueprint handlers are not inherited); the helper functions would need to be imported from api_v1 or moved to asme/utils/http.py.
- Response building: use api_ok(payload, message=None, status=200) from asme/utils/http.py for success and raise asme.services.errors.{Validation,NotFound,Conflict,Forbidden,ServiceError} for failures; add extra kwargs (e.g. field=, available=) to enrich the error body. For non-exception errors (like choose_event / calendar_unavailable) the precedent is inline jsonify({"ok": False, "code": ..., "error": ..., ...}), status.
- Gating: stack @bp.<method>, then @require_login or @require_role("team_leader"|"admin"), then @require_entitlement(ENT_*) (asme/auth/session.py, asme/constants.py). New entitlement keys must be added to constants.ALL_ENTITLEMENTS and to a Phase's grants (seeds in asme/services/onboarding/seeds.py) so phase_that_grants() can name the unlocking phase in the 403 body.
- Pagination: copy the list_checkouts pattern (decode cursor, filter id < before / id > after, order by id, limit+1, next_cursor via _encode_cursor). Keep filters out of the cursor and document that clients resend them. limit is clamped 1..200 with default 50 via _limit().
- Conditional GET: copy list_items for ETag support (json.dumps(sort_keys=True, default=str) -> sha1 hexdigest -> compare to If-None-Match -> 304 with ETag; 200 as raw Response with mimetype application/json). Remember the app after_request rewrites Cache-Control for authenticated sessions.
- Idempotent writes: read the key with idempotency_key() and raise Validation(..., field="idempotency_key", code="idempotency_required") when absent; persist it on a UNIQUE nullable String(120) column of the new table and return the existing row on replay (inventory.checkout precedent). Scope the lookup per actor if cross-user replays must not leak.
- Serializers: add serialize_<thing>(row) functions to asme/services/serializers.py using asme.utils.iso_or_none for date/datetime fields; all existing serializers return flat dicts with snake_case keys and ISO-8601 strings.
- Side-effects plumbing available to new endpoints: asme.services.audit.record(action, details, actor) (adds AuditLog, caller commits), asme.events.emit(name, **payload) (in-process bus; onboarding engine subscribes), asme.jobs.outbox.enqueue(kind, payload, run_at=None, max_attempts=5) (durable background work, caller commits), settings() from asme.config for typed config (add new env vars to Settings + from_env).
- Domain services to call rather than touching db.session directly (services/__init__.py docstring: "Blueprints call these; nothing else touches db.session"): asme.services.inventory (checkout, return_loan, find_item_by_tag, get_open_loan, open_loans_for, resolve_item, reconcile_stock), attendance (candidate_events, ensure_open_shop_event, check_user_into_event, admin_checkin, rsvp), scheduling (compute_available_slots, book_slot, upcoming_events, sync_status_for_event, create_event, update_event), fabrication (submit_print_request, update_print_request, queue_snapshot), identity (resolve_user_from_tag_uid, resolve_member, member_for_user, user_for_member), roster (parse_roster_pdf_entries, import_roster_entries, emit_import_events), onboarding.engine (evaluate_user, evaluate_chapter, summary_for_user, complete_task, reopen_task, record_training, member_overview), onboarding.entitlements (user_entitlements, has_entitlement, grant, revoke).
- Tests: extend tests/test_api_v1.py using conftest fixtures client, users (admin/lead/member), login_as, item, project, db, enforced_app, captured_events; smoke test test_route_count_is_complete asserts specific rules exist in app.url_map. Update the README.md "API v1" table (lines 104-123) when adding routes.
- Legacy /api surface (api_legacy.py) is frozen in shape by design (docstring: kept byte-compatible); new clients should target /api/v1 only.

**Risks / known defects**

- No app-level error handlers exist (grep confirms only the two blueprint-level ServiceError handlers and one blueprint-level 404): requests to unknown /api/v1/* paths, wrong methods (405), oversized bodies (413 from MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH) and unhandled exceptions (500) return Flask's HTML pages, not the JSON envelope. The api_v1 @bp.errorhandler(404) is effectively dead code for routing misses.
- Unguarded int() conversions in api_v1 produce 500s on bad input instead of a 400 Validation: complete_task (user_id), record_training (user_id, score), create_checkout (item_id, signed_off_by_user_id), create_checkin (event_id), return_checkout/return_loan (qty), and _decode_cursor consumers (cursor.get on a non-dict payload, int() on non-numeric after/before). A cursor that is valid base64 JSON but not an object crashes.
- Idempotency-Key replay in inventory.checkout is a global Loan.query.filter_by(idempotency_key=key) lookup with no user/item scoping and runs before validation: two different users (or two clients) sending the same key get the same loan back; a key collision silently returns someone else's checkout with 201.
- GET /api/v1/onboarding and GET /api/v1/me are not read-only: engine.evaluate() creates TaskState/PhaseState rows, grants/revokes Entitlement rows and commits on every call (summary_for_user only on first sight). Adding caching or retry semantics around these must account for writes.
- GET /api/v1/items sets Cache-Control: private, max-age=0 but the app after_request unconditionally overwrites it (and adds Pragma/Expires/Vary: Cookie) for any authenticated session, so the header the client actually sees is no-store; only the ETag/304 path survives.
- Legacy /api endpoints are largely unauthenticated: GET /api/bootstrap returns every member (name, email, nfc_tag) and every item to anyone; POST /api/attendance/scan, /api/inventory/transact, /api/print/submit and /api/print/job/<id>/{complete,fail,delete} (which deletes files from disk) have no auth gate at all; ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS does not gate them.
- Entitlement gates default to shadow mode (ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE unset -> False): in production require_entitlement only logs and lets requests through unless the flag is set; tests for enforcement use the enforced_app fixture.
- PATCH /api/v1/print-requests/<id> always overwrites admin_notes (missing field -> NULL) and always stamps reviewed_by/reviewed_at, so a PATCH intended to change only printer_type wipes existing admin notes.
- POST /api/v1/roster/import returns generated plaintext passwords in the JSON response (payload.credentials[].password) and resets existing users' passwords by default (reset_existing_passwords=True); it also depends on the optional pypdf import (RuntimeError -> HTML 500 if missing).
- Legacy error envelopes are inconsistent: _require_active_member 401 and several 400/404/409 bodies omit "code"; api_attendance_scan and api_inventory_transact pass ServiceErrors through api_error() so exc.code is replaced by "bad_request" (and transact forces status 400); /api/ask uses {"error": ...}. New v1 ops endpoints should not copy these.
- Cursor pagination on /checkouts and /print-requests does not encode the state/status/mine filters; a client that changes filters between pages gets inconsistent results. /events is capped by limit with no cursor. Admin GET /checkouts without ?mine returns every loan in the system.
- Time handling is mixed: Event start/end, candidate_events and upcoming_events use naive local datetime.now(); AttendanceRecord.checkin_time, health.time, loans use datetime.utcnow(); serialized ISO strings carry no timezone suffix. Ops endpoints that compare or display times must pick one convention explicitly.
- Session/boot-token coupling: app_boot_token defaults to a fresh uuid4 per process when ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN is unset, so every restart (or multi-worker gunicorn without a shared token) invalidates all API sessions with a 401 login_required.
- serialize_print_job calls url_for("legacy_ops.open_print_job"/"download_print_job"); it will raise BuildError (500) if the legacy_ops blueprint is ever unregistered, and it is invoked on every legacy api_success() via build_bootstrap_payload.
- build_bootstrap_payload() (returned by every legacy write) loads all members, all items, today's scans, 15 transactions and print queues on each call; performance degrades linearly with catalog/roster size.

## frontend-templates

Server-rendered Jinja2 frontend with NO bundler in the Flask app: hand-authored CSS in static/css (design-system.css = spacing/type/radius/motion tokens + shared components; portal.css and site.css each define their OWN identical-ish light color palette on :root; admin-ai.css / faq-bot.css are hard-coded hex widgets). Two shells: templates/portal/base.html (240px sticky sidebar + main, light theme, blue accent for members, gold accent via body.portal-theme-admin for admins, single `content` block, flash-stack from get_flashed_messages, inline sidebar JS, conditional admin-ai widget) and templates/site/base.html (fixed blurred white header, 8-link nav, blocks head_extras/content/page_scripts, CDN Spline+Lenis+GSAP, [data-reveal] IntersectionObserver reveals, faq-bot widget). The public site's visual identity is split: `/` renders the standalone, pure-black, full-viewport templates/site/landing.html (192-frame canvas rover scrub, Inter 200/300 + JetBrains Mono, Spline humanoid handoff, no header/portal link), while inner pages are light chrome wrapping dark cinematic full-bleed chapters (#060912/#0c1322 backgrounds, Space Grotesk display type, Iowa gold #FFCD00 buttons, scroll-scrub sections). Portal pages are rendered by blueprints portal (/portal, member+leader) and admin (/portal/admin) via a `_page(template,title,active_page,**extra)` helper on top of member_dashboard_context()/admin_dashboard_context(); `current_user`, `page_title`, `active_page` arrive via the context dict, not a context processor (only app_version/onboarding_enforced are globals). There is no CSRF protection anywhere (SameSite=Lax cookie is the only mitigation); all mutations are plain POST forms with a hidden `next` consumed by redirect_to_next(). Flash categories: success/error/info (warning styled only in design-system.css). Fonts: Inter + Space Grotesk via Google Fonts @import. Breakpoints: portal 1024/768; site 1024/880/640; widgets 480. Untracked apps/ops-web/ (created today) is a Vite+React 19 npm scaffold with runtime deps only (no vite/tsc devDependencies, no src). Three orphaned portal templates, an orphaned site/home.html, dead legacy ops templates referencing deleted CSS, and an unwired static/js/landing WebGL engine (with node:test files) also exist.

**Key facts**

- APP FACTORY (asme/__init__.py): Flask(template_folder=ROOT/'templates', static_folder=ROOT/'static'); config SECRET_KEY=cfg.secret_key (env ASME_SECRET_KEY, default 'asme-dev-secret'; production refuses defaults), SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE=cfg.session_cookie_samesite (env ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE default 'Lax'), SESSION_COOKIE_HTTPONLY=True, SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=cfg.session_cookie_secure (env ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE, default True only when RENDER env set), SESSION_PERMANENT=False, TEMPLATES_AUTO_RELOAD=cfg.templates_auto_reload (env ASME_TEMPLATES_AUTO_RELOAD, default on in development), MAX_CONTENT_LENGTH=max(print_max_upload_bytes, 64MiB). jinja_env.auto_reload mirrors it.
- CONTEXT PROCESSOR: only `app_version` ('2.0.0') and `onboarding_enforced` are injected globally. `current_user`, `page_title`, `active_page`, `page_subtitle` are NOT globals; every HTML route must supply them via public_site_context()/member_dashboard_context()/admin_dashboard_context() or explicitly. `site/landing.html` is rendered with render_template('site/landing.html') and NO context.
- REQUEST HOOKS: before_request calls current_auth_user() on every non-/static/, non-/arm-sim, non-/healthz path (enforces boot token + idle timeout: admin 30 min, member 240 min by default). after_request: when session has auth_user_id -> Cache-Control 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, max-age=0, private', Pragma no-cache, Expires 0, Vary +Cookie; always setdefault X-Content-Type-Options nosniff, X-Frame-Options SAMEORIGIN, Referrer-Policy strict-origin-when-cross-origin.
- CSRF: zero matches for 'csrf' in any .py/.html/.js; no Flask-WTF/SeaSurf/Talisman in requirements.txt (Flask, Flask-SQLAlchemy, Flask-Migrate, Werkzeug, pandas, openpyxl, gunicorn, google-api-python-client, google-auth, pypdf, reportlab, psycopg2-binary, anthropic, python-dotenv). All portal/admin/site mutations are plain <form method=post> with no token; logout is POST /logout (GET also allowed). Only mitigation is SameSite=Lax session cookie.
- BUNDLER: none for the Flask app. No package.json/vite.config/tailwind.config/postcss.config/tsconfig at repo root or under static/. CSS/JS are hand-authored and linked via url_for('static', ...). Vendored libs: static/vendor/model-viewer.min.js, static/vendor/gsap-3.15.0/{gsap,ScrollTrigger}.min.js, static/vendor/three-0.185.1/{three.core,three.module}.min.js. CDN in site/base.html: unpkg @splinetool/viewer@1.9.82 (type=module), jsdelivr lenis@1.1.20 (defer), cdnjs gsap 3.12.5 + ScrollTrigger 3.12.5 (defer). landing.html also loads unpkg spline-viewer@1.9.82. .gitignore/.ebignore ignore node_modules/.
- PREBUILT VITE OUTPUT: static/arm-sim/ is a committed Vite build (index.html with <script type=module crossorigin src=/arm-sim/assets/index-DZCKzyhH.js>, /arm-sim/assets/index-DAQfu-Nh.css, <div id=root>; plus stl/, hdri/, favicon.svg, icons.svg). Served by public.py: GET /arm-sim (index.html), /arm-sim/<path:subpath>, /stl/<path:subpath>, /hdri/<path:subpath> via send_from_directory. Its source is not in the repo. static/flora-preview/ is a standalone static prototype (index.html/styles.css/script.js) not routed anywhere.
- UNTRACKED NEW SCAFFOLD: apps/ops-web/ (git status '?? apps/', mtime today 15:36) contains only package.json, package-lock.json, node_modules (16 pkgs). package.json: name asme-ops-web, private, version 0.1.0, type module; scripts dev=vite, build='tsc -b && vite build', preview=vite preview, typecheck='tsc -b --noEmit', lint='eslint .', test='vitest run', test:e2e='playwright test'; dependencies: @hookform/resolvers ^5.9.1, @tanstack/react-query ^5.102.8, @tanstack/react-table ^9.2.4, clsx ^2.1.1, date-fns ^4.4.0, lucide-react ^1.43.0, react ^19.2.8, react-dom ^19.2.8, react-hook-form ^7.87.0, react-router-dom ^7.18.3, zod ^4.5.4. NO devDependencies (vite, typescript, eslint, vitest, playwright all absent), no src/, no index.html, no vite.config, no tsconfig.
- PORTAL SHELL (templates/portal/base.html, 118 lines): <title>{{ page_title }} | ASME Portal</title>; links css/design-system.css then css/portal.css (NO cache-busting v= param, unlike site/base.html), then css/admin-ai.css only when current_user.role|lower=='admin' AND NOT (active_page startswith 'member_'); favicon static/asme_logo.png. body class = 'portal-theme-admin' under the same admin condition else 'portal-theme-member'. Jinja locals: is_admin, in_member_view, show_admin_nav = is_admin and not in_member_view. Only one block: {% block content %}. No head/scripts block for child pages; children put <style>/<script> inline inside content.
- PORTAL NAV (hardcoded hrefs, not url_for). Admin nav (show_admin_nav): Dashboard /portal/admin [admin_dashboard], Launchpad /portal/admin/launchpad [admin_launchpad], Members /portal/admin/members [admin_members], Attendance /portal/admin/attendance [admin_attendance], Inventory /portal/admin/inventory [admin_inventory], Prints /portal/admin/prints [admin_prints], Calendar /portal/admin/calendar [admin_calendar], NFC Tags /portal/admin/nfc [admin_nfc], Exports /portal/admin/exports [admin_exports], Announcements /portal/admin/announcements [admin_announcements], Settings /portal/admin/settings [admin_settings]. Member nav: Dashboard /portal/member [member_dashboard], Launchpad /portal/member/launchpad [member_launchpad or member_launchpad_lead], Inventory /portal/member/inventory [member_inventory], Prints /portal/member/prints [member_prints], Calendar /portal/member/calendar [member_calendar], Profile /portal/member/profile [member_profile]. Below member nav: admin sees 'Switch to Admin' -> /portal/admin; team_leader sees 'Team Launchpad' -> /portal/leader/launchpad. Footer block: '← Website' -> / and <form action=/logout method=post><button>Logout</button></form>. active link class = 'active'.
- PORTAL SHELL header/flash: <main class=portal-main><header class=portal-head><h2>{{ page_title }}</h2>{% if page_subtitle %}<p>..</p>{% endif %}</header>, then {% with messages = get_flashed_messages(with_categories=True) %} -> <section class=flash-stack><div class='flash flash-{{ category }}'>{{ message }}</div>. Mobile: <button class=portal-mobile-toggle id=mobileToggle aria-label='Open navigation'>&#9776;</button> + <div class=portal-side-overlay id=sidebarOverlay>; inline JS toggles class 'open' on #portalSidebar and #sidebarOverlay, closes on any nav a click. Admin non-member view also gets <script src=js/admin-ai.js defer>. No portal route currently sets page_subtitle (only legacy render_ops_page does).
- SITE SHELL (templates/site/base.html, 188 lines): <title>{{ page_title }} | ASME @ UIowa</title>, meta description; css/design-system.css?v=20260416a, css/site.css?v=20260425o, css/faq-bot.css?v=20260416a; favicon asme_logo.png; scripts listed above; blocks: head_extras (in <head>), content (inside <main class=site-main> after flash-stack), page_scripts (after inline shell JS, before faq-bot.js). Header: <header class=site-header id=siteHeader> fixed, brand link '/', <nav class=site-nav id=siteNav> Home /, About /about, Team /exec, Projects /projects, Events /events, Gallery /gallery, Join /join, Contact /contact; <div class=site-actions>: if current_user -> 'Portal' /portal (.btn.btn-secondary.btn-sm) + POST /logout (.btn.btn-ghost.btn-sm) else Login /login + 'Join ASME' /join (.btn-primary); <button class=menu-toggle id=menuToggle> (3 spans). <div class=mobile-nav id=mobileNav> duplicates links + .mobile-nav-actions. <div class=site-header-spacer> (60px). <div class=site-bg> exists but site.css sets it display:none.
- SITE SHELL JS: header gets class 'scrolled' when scrollY>20; menu toggle toggles 'open' on #menuToggle/#mobileNav and sets body.style.overflow hidden; active nav = pathname startsWith(href) (exact for '/'). Inline <style>: [data-reveal] opacity 0 with .8s transitions; variants data-reveal=up(translateY 60px)/down/left(-80px)/right/scale(.88); .is-visible resets; prefers-reduced-motion disables. asmeInitScroll(): if reduced motion mark all visible; else creates window.__asmeLenis = new Lenis({duration:0.6, smoothWheel:true, wheelMultiplier:1.0}) once and hooks ScrollTrigger.update; IntersectionObserver threshold 0.12, rootMargin '0px 0px -8% 0px', honors data-reveal-delay (seconds); runs on DOMContentLoaded and window load. faq-bot.js fetches /static/js/faq-data.json (no server endpoint).
- DESIGN TOKENS — design-system.css :root (the ONLY shared token file; it does NOT define colors): --sp-1:4px --sp-2:8px --sp-3:12px --sp-4:16px --sp-5:20px --sp-6:24px --sp-7:28px --sp-8:32px --sp-10:40px --sp-12:48px --sp-16:64px --sp-20:80px; --text-xs:11px --text-sm:13px --text-base:15px --text-md:17px --text-lg:20px --text-xl:24px --text-2xl:30px --text-3xl:38px --text-4xl:48px --text-5xl:60px; --radius-sm:6px --radius-md:10px --radius-lg:14px --radius-xl:18px --radius-2xl:22px --radius-pill:999px; --max-w:1280px; --ease-out:cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1); --duration-fast:150ms; --duration-base:250ms. It also @imports Google Fonts 'Inter:wght@400;500;600;700;800' + 'Space+Grotesk:wght@500;600;700' and ships a reset, .btn/.btn-primary/.btn-secondary/.btn-ghost/.btn-sm/.btn-lg, .badge/.badge-default/-accent/-success/-warning/-danger, .flash/.flash-success/-error/-info/-warning, .flash-stack, .empty-state/-title/-desc, .section-label/.section-title/.section-desc, @keyframes fadeIn/slideUp, .animate-slide-up, .delay-1..5. Its component rules reference --accent/--accent-hover/--accent-glow/--accent-soft/--bg/--bg-hover/--line/--line-strong/--ink/--ink-secondary/--muted which must come from portal.css or site.css.
- DESIGN TOKENS — portal.css :root: --bg:#ffffff --bg-hover:#f8fafc --bg-subtle:#f8fafc --sidebar-bg:#f9fafb --line:#e2e8f0 --line-strong:#cbd5e1 --ink:#0f172a --ink-secondary:#334155 --muted:#64748b --placeholder:#94a3b8 --input-bg:#ffffff --accent:#2563eb --accent-hover:#1d4ed8 --accent-soft:#eff6ff --accent-glow:rgba(37,99,235,0.12) --good:#059669 --warn:#d97706 --bad:#dc2626 --shadow-sm:0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.05) --shadow-md:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.07). .portal-theme-admin overrides: --accent:#d97706 --accent-hover:#b45309 --accent-soft:#fffbeb --accent-glow:rgba(217,119,6,0.12). Unused anywhere: --sidebar-bg, --input-bg, --warn, --bg-card(site), --radius-2xl, --sp-20.
- DESIGN TOKENS — site.css :root (2028 lines, header comment 'Public Site (Light Theme)'): --bg:#ffffff --bg-hover:#f8fafc --bg-card:#ffffff --bg-subtle:#f8fafc --line:#e2e8f0 --line-strong:#cbd5e1 --ink:#0f172a --ink-secondary:#334155 --muted:#64748b --placeholder:#94a3b8 --accent:#2563eb --accent-hover:#1d4ed8 --accent-soft:#eff6ff --accent-glow:rgba(37,99,235,0.15) --good:#059669 --warn:#d97706 --bad:#dc2626 --shadow-sm:0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.06) --shadow-md:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.08) --shadow-lg:0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.1). Legacy root templates (templates/index.html, pair_item.html, pair_member.html) define their own --hawk-black:#000 --hawk-gold:#ffcd00 --hawk-white --ink:#101010 --ink-soft --line:#d9d9d9 --panel --panel-alt --danger:#b21f2d --ok:#176a3a with Barlow Condensed + IBM Plex Sans. No custom properties are defined inside any portal/*.html or site/*.html template. The only used-but-undefined token in the whole tree is var(--line-soft) in templates/site/login.html:27.
- FONTS: Inter (body, 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif) and Space Grotesk (display: .brand h1, .portal-head h2, .panel-head h3, .tile strong, .section-title, all .hh-*/.ww-* headings) loaded once via @import in design-system.css. landing.html separately @imports Inter 200;300;400;500;700 + JetBrains Mono 300;400 and uses weight 200 display type. admin-ai.css/faq-bot.css use 'SF Mono', monospace for code; launchpad templates use ui-monospace.
- BREAKPOINTS: portal.css @media (max-width:1024px) -> .portal-layout single column, .portal-side becomes position:fixed 260px off-canvas (translateX(-100%), .open -> 0, z-index 95, overlay z-index 94), .portal-mobile-toggle display:flex (fixed bottom/right var(--sp-5)=20px, 48px circle, z-index 90); @media (max-width:768px) -> .portal-main padding sp-3; .panel-grid.two, .split-fields, .tile-grid, .member-stat-grid, .admin-tiles, .detail-grid(.three-up) collapse to 1 column. admin_inventory.html adds @media (max-width:720px) .inv-kpis 2 cols. site.css: 1024px (hide .site-nav/.site-actions, show .menu-toggle, hero stacks), 640px (single-column grids, .site-main padding sp-4), 880px (scrollytelling .hh-*/.ww-* mobile fallbacks), plus prefers-reduced-motion blocks. admin-ai.css/faq-bot.css: 480px. landing.html: 720px.
- PORTAL LAYOUT CSS (portal.css): body 14px Inter line-height 1.55 background var(--bg-subtle); .portal-layout grid 240px 1fr min-height 100vh; .portal-side sticky top 0 height 100vh overflow-y auto bg --bg border-right --line padding sp-4 sp-3 flex column; .brand img 32px; .portal-side nav a padding 8px 12px radius-md text-sm 500 color --muted, ::before 5px dot, .active color --accent bg --accent-soft 600; .subtle-switch bordered pill-ish link text-xs; .portal-side form button full width transparent, hover red (#fecaca/#fef2f2/--bad); .portal-main padding sp-5 flex column gap sp-4 min-width 0; .portal-head bordered card, h2 Space Grotesk clamp(20px,2.5vw,28px). Components: .panel (border --line, radius-md, bg --bg, padding sp-5, hover border --line-strong), .panel-head (flex space-between baseline, h3 clamp(16px,1.8vw,20px), a text-xs accent), .panel-grid(.two minmax(260px,1fr)), .tile-grid auto-fill minmax(160px,1fr), .tile/.metric-card (p 10px uppercase muted, strong Space Grotesk 26px, span text-xs), .admin-tiles minmax(180px,1fr), .member-stat-grid repeat(4,1fr), .tab-nav a pill (active accent), .quick-links, .table-wrap overflow-x auto bordered, table separate borders text-sm, th 10px uppercase bg --bg-subtle, td border-bottom #f1f5f9, tbody tr:hover #fafbfc, .form-stack grid gap sp-3, .split-fields (3 cols; .two-up/.three-up), .inline-form, .badge(.bad/.good), .announcement-list/-item, .detail-grid(.three-up), .calendar-embed min-height 480px, .flash-stack/.flash(-success/-error/-info), .muted.
- PORTAL GLOBAL ELEMENT RULES (high blast radius): portal.css styles bare `label` (block, 10px, 600, uppercase, letter-spacing .04em, color --muted), bare `input, select, textarea` (width 100%, padding 9px 11px, border --line, radius-md, focus ring 0 0 0 3px --accent-glow), `textarea` min-height 70px, and bare `button` (accent background+border, white text, radius-pill, text-xs 600, padding 9px 18px, hover translateY(-1px)). design-system.css also styles `.btn`. Any new form element in the portal inherits these.
- FLASH CONTRACT: blueprints call flash(msg, category) with categories 'success', 'error', 'info' only (flash_error(exc) -> exc.message as 'error' then each exc.extra['errors'] as 'info'; attendance_checkin uses 'info' for ServiceError.status==409). Rendered as .flash.flash-<category>. design-system.css defines flash-success/error/info/warning; portal.css redefines flash-success/error/info (info uses var(--accent-soft) bg) and has no warning variant.
- ROLE GATES: asme/auth/session.py. ROLE_ORDER guest0 < member1 < team_leader2 < admin3 (asme/constants.py); normalize_role() lowercases and falls back to 'member'; role_allows(user_role, required). Decorators require_login, require_role(min_role), require_entitlement(key) (shadow mode unless ASME_ONBOARDING_ENFORCE=1; admins pass via has_entitlement). Denials: not logged in -> flash 'Please log in to continue.' error + 302 /login?next=<path> (JSON 401 {ok:false,code:'login_required'} when path starts /api/ or Accept prefers JSON); wrong role -> flash error + 302 /portal (JSON 403 code 'forbidden'). Templates test current_user.role|lower == 'admin' / 'team_leader' directly.
- CONTEXT BUILDERS (asme/blueprints/_context.py): public_site_context(page_title) -> {page_title, current_user, projects (all Project desc), project_filters, announcements=content.public_announcements(limit=5), executive_cards=build_executive_cards()}. member_dashboard_context() -> {current_user, member_profile, items (Item.active==True ordered name), open_checkouts, print_requests (user's), portal_print_printers=PRINT_REQUEST_PRINTERS, upcoming_events (limit 25), calendar_embed_url, announcements=content.member_announcements(limit=8), my_help_messages (40), launchpad=engine.summary_for_user, onboarding_enforced}. admin_dashboard_context() -> {current_user, members, users, tags, items (ALL incl inactive), item_primary_tags (dict item_id->tag), transactions (120), print_requests (120), events (120), attendance_records (200), projects, contact_messages (60), audit_logs (150), roles=['member','team_leader','admin'], print_request_statuses, portal_print_printers, item_types=ITEM_TYPES=('tool','consumable'), active_members_count, checked_out_now_count (sum Transaction.qty where status=='OUT'), attendance_today_count, overdue_items_count, low_stock_items_count, upcoming_meetings_count, pending_print_count, calendar_provider, google_embed_set, outlook_embed_set}. Both portal.py and admin.py define `_page(template, title, active_page, **extra)` that builds the respective context, sets page_title/active_page, merges extra, render_template.
- TEMPLATE -> ROUTE MAP. portal bp (url_prefix /portal, asme/blueprints/portal.py): member_dashboard_home.html (GET /member, active_page member_dashboard, adds tile_counts{inventory,my_items,prints,upcoming_events,announcements}), member_inventory.html (GET /member/inventory, query param q filtered in Python over name/category/location/description/notes, context.query), member_item_detail.html (GET /member/items/<id> and /member/inventory/<id>, active member_inventory, item, open_tx), member_checkouts.html (GET /member/checkouts and /member/my-items, active member_my_items — no nav item), member_prints.html (/member/prints), member_print_detail.html (/member/prints/<id>), member_calendar.html (/member/calendar; team_mode, meeting_rooms, calendar_provider), member_schedule.html (GET/POST /member/schedule, team_leader + room_booking), member_help.html (GET/POST /member/help, active member_help — no nav item), member_profile.html (GET/POST /member/profile), member_launchpad.html (/member/launchpad), admin_launchpad.html reused for leaders (GET /leader/launchpad, active member_launchpad_lead, is_admin_view=False). admin bp (url_prefix /portal/admin, admin.py): admin_dashboard_home.html (GET '', 'Admin Home', admin_dashboard), admin_members.html (/members), admin_attendance.html (/attendance, tab live|history), admin_inventory.html (/inventory: low_stock_items, overdue_transactions, stock_discrepancies), admin_prints.html (/prints), admin_calendar.html (/calendar), admin_settings.html (/settings, 'System Status'), admin_launchpad.html (/launchpad).
- ORPHANED / DEAD TEMPLATES: templates/portal/admin_announcements.html, admin_exports.html, admin_nfc.html exist but are never rendered — GET /portal/admin/announcements, /content, /exports, /exports/download.zip, /nfc all `redirect(url_for('admin.dashboard'))`, yet the sidebar still shows NFC Tags / Exports / Announcements links. templates/site/home.html (light shell + .hh-hero Spline scrollytelling, blocks content+page_scripts) is never rendered ('/' renders site/landing.html). templates/index.html is never rendered. templates/ops/*.html and templates/front/portal.html are gated behind ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS=1 (default 0) and link css/ops_pages.css and css/front_portal.css which no longer exist in static/css (only admin-ai.css, design-system.css, faq-bot.css, portal.css, site.css exist). static/js/landing/*.js + static/shaders/landing + static/vendor/three-0.185.1 + static/vendor/gsap-3.15.0 (Build Line WebGL engine, commit 9720e2c, docs/landing/CODEX_REPORT.md) are referenced by no template.
- FULL templates/ LISTING (59 files): root: index.html, pair_item.html, pair_member.html; front/portal.html; ops/{activity,admin_nfc,attendance,base,calendar,dashboard,inventory,my_items,prints,scan,settings}.html; portal/{admin_announcements,admin_attendance,admin_calendar,admin_dashboard_home,admin_exports,admin_inventory,admin_launchpad,admin_members,admin_nfc,admin_prints,admin_settings,base,member_calendar,member_checkouts,member_dashboard_home,member_help,member_inventory,member_item_detail,member_launchpad,member_print_detail,member_prints,member_profile,member_schedule}.html; site/{admin_login,base,checkin,checkin_select,checkin_success,contact,events,executive_team,forgot_password,gallery,home,join,kiosk,landing,login,project_detail,projects,reset_password,signup,sponsors,who_we_are}.html. All portal/* extend portal/base.html; all site/* except landing.html extend site/base.html; ops/* extend ops/base.html; pair_*.html, index.html, front/portal.html, site/landing.html are standalone full documents.

|---|---|---|---|

| GET | `/portal/member` | `portal.member_dashboard -> portal/member_dashboard_home.html` | require_role('member') |

| GET | `/portal/member/my-items and /portal/member/checkouts` | `portal.member_checkouts -> portal/member_checkouts.html` | require_role('member') |

| GET | `/portal/member/prints/<int:request_id>` | `portal.member_print_detail -> portal/member_print_detail.html` | require_role('member'); owner check else flash+302 |
| GET | `/portal/member/calendar` | `portal.member_calendar -> portal/member_calendar.html` | require_role('member') |
| GET/POST | `/portal/member/schedule` | `portal.member_schedule -> portal/member_schedule.html` | require_role('team_leader') + require_entitlement('room_booking') |
| GET/POST | `/portal/member/help` | `portal.member_help -> portal/member_help.html` | require_role('member') |
| GET/POST | `/portal/member/profile` | `portal.member_profile -> portal/member_profile.html (action=profile|password)` | require_role('member') |

| POST | `/portal/inventory/checkout` | `portal.checkout (form item_id, qty, notes, due_date, idempotency_key, next)` | require_role('member') + require_entitlement('shop_access') + on_service_error(' |
| POST | `/portal/inventory/return` | `portal.return_item` | require_role('member') + on_service_error('portal.member_checkouts') |

| GET | `/portal/admin` | `admin.dashboard -> portal/admin_dashboard_home.html` | require_role('admin') |
| GET | `/portal/admin/members` | `admin.members_page -> portal/admin_members.html (?show_inactive=)` | require_role('admin') |

| GET | `/portal/admin/calendar` | `admin.calendar_page -> portal/admin_calendar.html` | require_role('admin') |

| GET | `/portal/admin/launchpad` | `admin.launchpad_page -> portal/admin_launchpad.html` | require_role('admin') |
| GET | `/portal/admin/nfc, /portal/admin/exports, /portal/admin/exports/download.zip, /portal/admin/announcements (GET/POST), /portal/admin/content (GET/POST)` | `admin.nfc_page / exports_page / exports_zip / announcements_page / content_page -> 302 admin.dashboard (templates admin_nfc/admin_exports/admin_announcements orphaned)` | require_role('admin') |
| POST | `/portal/admin/inventory/item/save` | `admin.inventory_item_save -> inventory.save_item(request.form, actor)` | require_role('admin') + on_service_error('admin.inventory_page') |
| POST | `/portal/admin/inventory/adjust` | `admin.inventory_adjust (item_id, total_qty, available_qty, notes)` | require_role('admin') |

| GET | `/` | `public.public_home -> site/landing.html (no context)` | none |
| GET | `/events, /gallery, /who-we-are, /executive-team, /projects, /projects/<slug>` | `public.* -> site/events|gallery|who_we_are|executive_team|projects|project_detail.html` | none |
| GET/POST | `/contact, /join, /sponsors` | `public.public_contact/public_join/public_sponsors -> site/contact|join|sponsors.html` | none |
| GET | `/about, /exec, /socials` | `redirects to /who-we-are, /executive-team, /contact` | none |
| GET | `/arm-sim, /arm-sim/<path>, /stl/<path>, /hdri/<path>` | `public.arm_sim_* send_from_directory(static/arm-sim...)` | none |
| GET/POST | `/login, /signup, /admin-login, /forgot-password, /reset-password/<token>` | `auth.* -> site/login|signup|admin_login|forgot_password|reset_password.html` | none (login rate-limited per ip+identifier) |
| GET/POST | `/logout` | `auth.logout_page` | none |
| GET | `/kiosk, /checkin, /checkin/select (GET/POST), /checkin/success, /pair/member (GET/POST), /pair/item (GET/POST)` | `kiosk.* -> site/kiosk|checkin|checkin_select|checkin_success.html, pair_member.html, pair_item.html` | /checkin* require_login; others none |
| POST | `/api/ask` | `api_legacy.api_ask JSON {message, history} -> {reply, tool_calls}; 403 {error:'admin only'}, 400 {error:'empty message'|'message too long'}` | session admin (normalize_role=='admin') |
| GET | `/dashboard, /attendance, /inventory, /prints, /activity, /settings, /scan, /my-items, /admin/nfc, /calendar, /app` | `legacy_ops.* -> ops/*.html or front/portal.html only when ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS=1, else 302 into portal (/app -> /kiosk)` | settings().legacy_ops_enabled |

**Extension points**

- Add a portal page: create templates/portal/<name>.html with `{% extends "portal/base.html" %}{% block content %}...{% endblock %}` (only block available; put page CSS in an inline <style> and page JS in inline <script> inside the block, as admin_inventory.html/admin_members.html/launchpad templates do), add a route in asme/blueprints/portal.py or admin.py using `_page("portal/<name>.html", "Title", "<active_page_key>", **extra)`, and add a sidebar <a class="{{ 'active' if active_page == '<key>' else '' }}" href="/portal/..."> in templates/portal/base.html (member keys must start with 'member_' to keep the member theme/nav when an admin views them). Add the URL to tests/test_routes_smoke.py MEMBER/LEAD/ADMIN lists.
- Add a public page: extend site/base.html; override head_extras for extra <link>/<script>, content, and page_scripts (runs after Lenis/GSAP/ScrollTrigger defer scripts have been declared); render with `render_template("site/x.html", **public_site_context("Title"))` so current_user/announcements/executive_cards/projects are available; add link to both .site-nav and .mobile-nav in site/base.html and to .landing-links in site/landing.html; add to tests PUBLIC list. Use [data-reveal="up|down|left|right|scale"] + data-reveal-delay for scroll reveals.
- Theme/tokens: shared non-color tokens live in static/css/design-system.css :root; color tokens are duplicated in portal.css and site.css :root blocks — change both (or hoist to design-system.css) to keep parity. Admin accent is switched by body.portal-theme-admin (portal.css) overriding --accent/--accent-hover/--accent-soft/--accent-glow; add further per-role overrides there. Semantic colors available: --good/--warn/--bad; shadows --shadow-sm/--shadow-md (+ --shadow-lg site only).
- Mutating forms: POST to a blueprint route decorated with @require_role(...) (+ @require_entitlement(KEY) from asme.constants ENT_*) and @on_service_error('<endpoint>'); include <input type=hidden name=next value="/portal/..."> and finish with redirect_to_next('<default endpoint>'); report outcomes with flash(msg, 'success'|'error'|'info').
- Context data: extend member_dashboard_context()/admin_dashboard_context() in asme/blueprints/_context.py for data every page of that shell needs; pass page-specific data through _page(**extra). Global template variables must be added to _inject_globals() in asme/__init__.py (currently app_version, onboarding_enforced).
- Static copy / exec board: edit asme/content_data.py (EXEC_PROFILE_OVERRIDES keyed by 'email:'/'name:', MANUAL_EXECUTIVE_PROFILES, FRONT_* lists); headshots go under static/images/executive/ and must exist on disk or resolve_exec_headshot_url() falls back to asme_logo.png.
- Widgets: admin-ai.js / faq-bot.js are self-injecting IIFEs appended to body; new floating widgets should coordinate on the bottom-right 20px slot and z-index 1000 already used by both (and z-index 90 for .portal-mobile-toggle).
- Static asset versioning: site/base.html uses manual `v=` query params on url_for('static', ...) for cache busting; bump them when editing design-system.css/site.css/faq-bot.css; portal/base.html currently has none.
- 3D/animation stack already present for reuse: model-viewer (vendored), Spline viewer (CDN), GSAP 3.12.5 + ScrollTrigger (CDN) and Lenis (CDN) on site pages; vendored gsap 3.15.0 and three 0.185.1 under static/vendor for the unwired static/js/landing engine.
- Prebuilt SPA pattern: static/arm-sim shows how a Vite build output is committed and served via send_from_directory routes in public.py (/arm-sim, /stl, /hdri); apps/ops-web/ is an empty scaffold that could follow the same pattern if built into static/.

**Risks / known defects**

- No CSRF protection on any form or on /api/ask; only SameSite=Lax cookie (default; configurable to None) and X-Frame-Options SAMEORIGIN mitigate. Adding fetch()-based mutations from new JS would be trivially forgeable cross-site if SameSite is relaxed.
- Color tokens are defined twice (portal.css and site.css :root) with small drift (--accent-glow alpha .12 vs .15, --shadow-* alphas, site-only --bg-card/--shadow-lg, portal-only --sidebar-bg/--input-bg). design-system.css component rules depend on these but define none, so it cannot be loaded alone.
- portal.css applies element-level rules to bare button/label/input/select/textarea; any new component or third-party markup in the portal inherits accent pill buttons, 100%-width inputs and 10px uppercase labels unless overridden.
- Public identity is inconsistent: '/' is the black, scroll-hijacking landing.html (no header, no Login/Portal link, requires JS, preventDefault on wheel/touch, 192 JPEG preload); inner pages use the light shell. site/home.html (light shell + Spline hero) is unreachable, so any 'homepage' redesign must decide which file is canonical.
- Sidebar shows NFC Tags, Exports, Announcements links that redirect straight back to the dashboard (routes are stubs); templates admin_nfc/admin_exports/admin_announcements are orphaned. Members' Help (/portal/member/help), My Items (/portal/member/my-items) and Schedule pages have no nav entry (active_page keys member_help/member_my_items/member_schedule never highlight).
- templates/site/login.html uses var(--line-soft), which is defined nowhere (border falls back to currentColor). member_inventory.html category badge uses rgba(255,255,255,0.04) background (invisible on the white theme) — remnants of the pre-light-theme design.
- admin_dashboard_context() loads members, users, tags, all items, 120 transactions, 120 print requests, 120 events, 200 attendance records, projects, 60 contact messages, 150 audit logs, plus several counts on EVERY admin page including the 4-tile dashboard; adding admin pages via _page() inherits this cost.
- admin_inventory.html ignores the stock_discrepancies context the route provides, and there is no UI for POST /portal/admin/stock/discrepancies/<id>/resolve or /stock/reconcile even though README says discrepancies show on Admin -> Inventory.
- Legacy templates (ops/base.html -> css/ops_pages.css, front/portal.html -> css/front_portal.css) reference CSS files deleted in commit 674a9c3; enabling ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS=1 renders unstyled pages.
- static/js/landing/* Build Line WebGL engine (+ three/gsap vendors, tests/landing/*.test.mjs) is unreferenced by any template — dead weight with a node:test suite that has no runner config; do not assume it is live when changing the landing page.
- External CDN dependencies (unpkg spline-viewer, jsdelivr lenis, cdnjs gsap) and Google Fonts @import are hard requirements for the public shell; offline/CSP-restricted environments break the header animations, Spline heroes and fonts. landing.html also strips Spline's watermark from its shadow DOM, which may violate Spline's free-tier terms.
- Portal CSS links have no cache-busting; /static is served by nginx on Elastic Beanstalk with default caching, so portal.css/design-system.css changes may not reach returning users promptly.
- admin-ai widget and portal mobile toggle both sit fixed bottom-right (20px) — on admin screens <=1024px the 56px AI button (z 1000) covers the 48px nav toggle (z 90).
- content_data.py hard-codes exec members' personal emails and phone numbers that render on the public /executive-team page; MANUAL_EXECUTIVE_PROFILES and EXEC_PROFILE_OVERRIDES duplicate the same text in several places and must be edited in sync.
- apps/ops-web/ is untracked and incomplete (no vite/typescript/eslint/vitest/playwright devDependencies despite scripts referencing them, no src/config); `npm run dev` will fail as-is. Nothing in Flask serves it; committing it as-is would add a broken workspace.

## tests-deploy-config

The pytest suite (155 tests, 10 files, all passing locally on Python 3.13.14) builds the app through create_app(**overrides) with env="testing", an in-memory SQLite URL ("sqlite://"), Alembic disabled (schema via db.create_all + seed_default_tracks), the outbox worker off, onboarding in shadow mode, and a 100-attempt login limiter; three users (admin/team_leader/member, all @uiowa.edu, password "correct-horse-battery") plus one Item and one Project are seeded per test, and a login helper POSTs identifier+password to /login expecting a 302/303. All configuration is a frozen dataclass Settings in asme/config.py, resolved once from ASME_*/GOOGLE_*/CALENDAR_*/PORT/ANTHROPIC_API_KEY env vars via Settings.from_env(**overrides), with validate() refusing production startup on a default secret / SameSite=None-without-secure / half-configured Outlook or SMTP. Three deployment targets share one release step, `python manage.py upgrade` (Alembic upgrade with legacy-DB stamping + idempotent seeds): a Heroku-style Procfile (gunicorn :8000, 2 workers), render.yaml (Python 3.11.11 pinned, exact pins in requirements-render.txt, generated ASME_SECRET_KEY, no database block), and Elastic Beanstalk .ebextensions (WSGIPath app:app, nginx, /static mapping, leader_only migrate; no platform/Python pin in repo). docs/landing documents the Codex-built Three.js/GSAP landing engine (vendored Three 0.185.1 / GSAP 3.15.0, 21 Node tests, no Spline/Lenis/React/bundler) and four release-blocking JSON/JS shell edits; it states no backend, migration, or DB change was part of that branch.

**Key facts**

- TEST APP BUILD: tests/conftest.py inserts repo ROOT on sys.path, calls os.environ.setdefault("ASME_ENV","testing"), then make_app(**overrides) = create_app(env="testing", secret_key="test-secret", database_url="sqlite://", auto_migrate=False, outbox_worker_enabled=False, onboarding_enforce=False, session_cookie_secure=False, calendar_provider="google", login_rate_max_attempts=100, **overrides). Overrides are plain Settings field names passed through Settings.from_env(**overrides) -> dict.update -> cls(**values) (asme/__init__.py:32, asme/config.py:230).
- TEST DB: URL is literally "sqlite://" (SQLAlchemy in-memory). Schema is created with _db.create_all() and seeded with seed_default_tracks() inside an app_context in the `app` fixture (function scope); teardown = _db.session.remove() + _db.drop_all(). Alembic migrations (migrations/versions/0001_baseline..., 0002_launchpad...) are NEVER executed by the test suite because create_app skips bootstrap when cfg.is_testing (asme/__init__.py:86) and auto_migrate=False.
- FIXTURES (tests/conftest.py): app, enforced_app (same as app but onboarding_enforce=True), db (returns asme.extensions.db), users, item, project, client (app.test_client()), login_as(user) -> asserts login status in (302,303) and returns client, captured_events -> list of (name, payload) collected by subscribing a catch-all handler to asme.events "*" and unsubscribing on teardown. Module-level helpers importable as `from tests.conftest import PASSWORD, login, _db, _user` (tests/__init__.py exists so `tests` is a package).
- SEEDED USERS (users fixture, all is_active=True, password_hash=werkzeug generate_password_hash(PASSWORD) with PASSWORD="correct-horse-battery", username=email local-part): admin = User(name="Ada Admin", email="ada@uiowa.edu", username="ada", role="admin"); lead = User(name="Lee Lead", email="lee@uiowa.edu", username="lee", role="team_leader", major="ME", graduation_year=2027); member = User(name="Mo Member", email="mo@uiowa.edu", username="mo", role="member"). _user(name, email, role, **extra) adds+flushes; users fixture commits.
- SEEDED ROWS: item fixture = Item(name="Digital Calipers", category="Tools", location="Drawer 2", total_qty=3, available_qty=3, active=True); project fixture = Project(slug="rover", title="Rover", summary="s", description="d", status="Active", is_joinable=True).
- LOGIN HELPER: login(client, user, password=PASSWORD) = client.post("/login", data={"identifier": user.email, "password": password}, follow_redirects=False). The /login route (asme/blueprints/auth.py:39) reads form `identifier` (falls back to `email`), lower-cases it, checks LoginRateLimiter.is_limited(ip, identifier), calls identity.authenticate(identifier, password), on failure record_failure + flash "Invalid email or password." (200 re-render), on success limiter.clear + sign_in_user(user) + redirect to safe `next` or url_for("portal.portal_router") (/portal). Rate-limit message: "Too many login attempts. Try again in about {retry_after} seconds."
- SESSION MECHANICS relied on by tests: sign_in_user sets session keys auth_user_id, auth_user_role, auth_boot_token (= cfg.app_boot_token), auth_login_ts, auth_last_seen_ts, active_member_id (if legacy Member linked); current_auth_user() signs out when boot token mismatches cfg.app_boot_token, user inactive/missing, or idle > admin_session_idle_minutes (admin) / member_session_idle_minutes (others). after_request adds Cache-Control "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, max-age=0, private" + Pragma + Expires + Vary: Cookie for authenticated sessions, and X-Content-Type-Options nosniff, X-Frame-Options SAMEORIGIN, Referrer-Policy strict-origin-when-cross-origin for all responses.
- JSON DENIAL SHAPES (asme/auth/session.py): unauthenticated JSON -> 401 {"ok": false, "code": "login_required", "error": "Login required."}; role denial -> 403 {"ok": false, "code": "forbidden", ...}; entitlement denial (only when onboarding_enforce) -> 403 {"ok": false, "code": "entitlement_required", "entitlement": key, "phase": phase.key|null, "phase_name": ..., "error": ...}. _wants_json() is true for any path starting with /api/ or JSON Accept/body. ServiceError.to_dict() -> {"ok": false, "code", "error"} + extra; NotFound=404/"not_found", Conflict=409/"conflict", Forbidden=403/"forbidden", Validation=400/"validation" (+optional field).
- APP EXTENSIONS the tests mutate: app.extensions["asme_login_rate_limiter"] (LoginRateLimiter(window_seconds, max_attempts); test_login_rate_limit sets .max_attempts = 2) and app.extensions["asme_calendar_provider"] (tests replace with CalendarProvider subclasses overriding status()/free_busy()/create_event()). get_provider() lazily rebuilds if the key is missing.
- GLOBAL STATE ACROSS TESTS: asme.events._subscribers (module-level defaultdict; register_event_handlers() runs every create_app, subscribe() dedupes identical handler objects) and asme.jobs.outbox._handlers (module-level dict; test_outbox registers "test.echo" and "test.boom" kinds that persist for the process). LoginRateLimiter is per-app so fresh each test.
- pytest.ini: testpaths=tests; python_files=test_*.py; addopts="-q -p no:cacheprovider"; filterwarnings=ignore::DeprecationWarning. Running `python -m pytest -q` therefore yields -qq (no summary line, per-file counts only).
- SUITE RESULT: 155 tests collected and all passed via .venv (Python 3.13.14). Per file: test_api_v1 13, test_auth_rbac 11, test_config 4, test_identity 11, test_inventory 14, test_onboarding 16, test_outbox 5, test_roster 5, test_routes_smoke 72 (parametrized), test_scheduling 4. Local venv pins: Flask 3.1.3, Werkzeug 3.1.8, Flask-SQLAlchemy 3.1.1, Flask-Migrate 4.1.0, SQLAlchemy 2.0.52, Alembic 1.19.2, pandas 3.0.5, gunicorn 23.0.0, pytest 8.4.2, anthropic 0.125.0, psycopg2-binary 2.9.12.
