Asserts: /api/v1/health -> {ok, payload.api="v1"}; POST /api/v1/checkouts without Idempotency-Key -> 400 code "idempotency_required"; with header -> 201 payload.checkout.state "open", replay same key returns same id; GET /api/v1/checkouts?state=open; POST /api/v1/checkouts/{id}/return json {condition} -> 200 state "returned"; second checkout of same item -> 409 code "already_checked_out"; GET /api/v1/items?limit=2 returns ETag + payload.next_cursor, If-None-Match -> 304, cursor=!!! -> 400; POST /api/v1/checkins {} -> 201 payload.event.kind "open_shop", again -> 200 payload.created false; POST /api/v1/onboarding/tasks/complete_profile/complete -> 400 code "task_not_manual"; GET /api/v1/onboarding?user_id=X -> 200 for lead (payload.subject.id is a string), 403 for member; PATCH /api/v1/print-requests/{id} {status} -> 403 for member, 200 for admin with payload.print_request.runs[0].status; percent baseline == round(1/13*100) because @uiowa.edu email satisfies verify_email.

- SETTINGS PARSING HELPERS (asme/config.py): _str(name, default) strips; _bool(name, default) TRUTHY={1,true,yes,on,y} FALSY={0,false,no,off,n}, anything else -> default; _int(name, default, minimum) -> default on non-int or below minimum (silent). load_instance_env_file(path) loads KEY=value lines from instance/print_commands.env and OVERWRITES os.environ (unlike load_dotenv(ROOT/.env) in create_app which does not override existing vars). Settings is @dataclass(frozen=True); settings() returns current_app.config["SETTINGS"].
- SETTINGS runtime: env <- ASME_ENV (default "development" if FLASK_DEBUG truthy else "production"; lowercased; must be development|production|testing else production); secret_key <- ASME_SECRET_KEY (default "asme-dev-secret"; validate: production with ""/"asme-dev-secret"/"change-this-secret" -> startup refused); database_url <- ASME_DATABASE_URL (default "sqlite:///inventory.db"); port <- PORT (5000, min 1); auto_migrate <- ASME_AUTO_MIGRATE (default env != production); templates_auto_reload <- ASME_TEMPLATES_AUTO_RELOAD (default env == development).
- SETTINGS sessions/auth: session_cookie_secure <- ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE (default bool(os.environ.get("RENDER")), i.e. auto-on only on Render; dataclass default True is never used by from_env); session_cookie_samesite <- ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SAMESITE (default "Lax"; lax|strict|none else lax; stored "Lax"/"Strict"/"None"; validate: "None" requires secure); member_session_idle_minutes <- ASME_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES (240, min 0); admin_session_idle_minutes <- ASME_ADMIN_SESSION_IDLE_MINUTES (30, min 0); app_boot_token <- ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN or uuid4().hex generated per create_app call; login_rate_window_seconds <- ASME_LOGIN_RATE_WINDOW_SECONDS (900, min 1); login_rate_max_attempts <- ASME_LOGIN_RATE_MAX_ATTEMPTS (8, min 1); bulk_password_hash_method <- ASME_BULK_PASSWORD_HASH_METHOD ("pbkdf2:sha256:120000"); password_reset_hours = 2 (dataclass default only, NO env var wired in from_env).

| Method | Path | Endpoint | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| GET/POST | `/login` | `auth.login_page (asme/blueprints/auth.py)` | public; form fields identifier/email + password + optional next; rate-limited pe |
| GET/POST | `/logout` | `auth.logout_page` | public; clears session; redirects to public.public_home with no-store headers |
| GET/POST | `/signup` | `auth.signup_page` | public; identity.signup never grants admin |
| GET/POST | `/admin-login` | `auth.admin_login_page` | public; accepts env shared_admin_email/password or an admin-role user; rate-limi |
| GET | `/admin` | `auth.admin_home` | redirects to admin.dashboard or admin-login |
| GET/POST | `/forgot-password` | `auth.forgot_password_page` | public; flashes the reset link inline (no mail send) |
| GET/POST | `/reset-password/<token>` | `auth.reset_password_page` | public; token must be valid/unexpired |
| GET | `/healthz` | `public.health_check` | public; returns {"ok": true, "service": "asme-web"}; exempt from session guard a |
| GET | `/portal` | `portal.portal_router` | login; 302 to /portal/member or /portal/admin by role |
| GET | `/portal/member/launchpad` | `portal.member_launchpad` | login (admins may view); redirect target for HTML entitlement denials |
| GET | `/portal/admin` | `admin.dashboard` | require_role(admin); members get 302 to /portal |
| POST | `/portal/inventory/checkout` | `portal blueprint (function name not read)` | login + shop_access entitlement (shadow mode passes; enforced -> 403/redirect);  |
| GET | `/api/v1/health` | `api_v1 blueprint (function name not read)` | public; {ok, payload.api="v1"} |
| GET | `/api/v1/me` | `api_v1 (not read)` | login; 401 {code: login_required} otherwise; payload.user + payload.launchpad.cu |
| GET | `/api/v1/onboarding[?user_id=]` | `api_v1 (not read)` | login; user_id of another user requires team_leader+ (member -> 403) |
| POST | `/api/v1/onboarding/tasks/<key>/complete` | `api_v1 (not read)` | login; manual/sign-off tasks only, rule-driven -> 400 code task_not_manual |
| GET | `/api/v1/items?limit=&cursor=` | `api_v1 (not read)` | portal_access; ETag/If-None-Match -> 304; bad cursor -> 400 |
| POST | `/api/v1/checkouts` | `api_v1 (not read)` | shop_access; requires Idempotency-Key header (400 idempotency_required); 201 on  |
| GET | `/api/v1/checkouts?state=open` | `api_v1 (not read)` | portal_access |
| POST | `/api/v1/checkouts/<id>/return` | `api_v1 (not read)` | shop_access; json {condition}; 200 state returned |
| POST | `/api/v1/checkins` | `api_v1 (not read)` | event_checkin; 201 creates open_shop event when nothing scheduled, 200 created:f |
| PATCH | `/api/v1/print-requests/<id>` | `api_v1 (not read)` | print_approve / team_leader+ (member -> 403); returns payload.print_request.runs |
| GET | `/dashboard, /attendance, /inventory, /prints, /activity, /settings, /scan, /my-items, /admin/nfc, /calendar, /app` | `legacy_ops blueprint` | 302 redirect into portal unless ASME_ENABLE_LEGACY_OPS=1 |
| GET | `/api/checkout, /transact, /checkin, /pair/item, /export` | `api_legacy / kiosk blueprints` | existence asserted by test_route_count_is_complete; gates not read |

**Extension points**

- Add a config value: add a field to Settings (asme/config.py) with a default, add its _str/_bool/_int line in from_env, optionally extend validate()/warnings(), and document it in .env.example; every field is then overridable in tests via make_app(field=value) / create_app(field=value) with no other plumbing.
- Feature-flag tests: copy the enforced_app pattern in tests/conftest.py (make_app(onboarding_enforce=True)) to build an app with any Settings override; note only `app`/`enforced_app` create schema, so a new fixture must call _db.create_all() + seed_default_tracks() itself.
- Assert domain events: use the captured_events fixture (list of (name, payload)); new events must be added as constants + ALL_EVENTS in asme/events/__init__.py (comment says tests assert on the list).
- Swap integrations in tests: assign a CalendarProvider subclass to app.extensions["asme_calendar_provider"]; register fake outbox handlers with @outbox.handler("kind") and drive them with outbox.process_pending() (no thread needed).
- Add a background job: @handler("kind") in asme/jobs/handlers.py (imported by asme.jobs), enqueue via outbox.enqueue inside the service transaction; for periodic work add an ensure_recurring(...) call in outbox._loop or the worker/CLI.
- Add a page: append its path to PUBLIC/MEMBER/LEAD/ADMIN lists in tests/test_routes_smoke.py (and LEGACY_REDIRECTS if legacy); test_portal_requires_login automatically covers MEMBER+ADMIN paths for anonymous 302.
- Add API error codes: raise ServiceError subclasses with code=/status=/**extra; JSON blueprints serialize to_dict() so tests can assert body["code"].
- Add onboarding tasks/phases/modules: edit MEMBER_TRACK/CHAPTER_TRACK/TRAINING_MODULES in seeds.py (upsert by key, never deletes) and update the hard-coded counts (Track 2 / Phase 6 / TrainingModule 3 / 13 required tasks / percent math) in test_onboarding.py and test_api_v1.py.
- Schema changes: add an Alembic revision under migrations/versions (batch mode enabled); the release step `python manage.py upgrade` runs it on Render (startCommand), Heroku-style Procfile (release:), and EB (03_migrate.config leader_only). Tests will not exercise the migration (they use create_all).
- Operational commands: add to manage.py main() and/or the Flask CLI group in asme/__init__.py _register_cli.
- Deploy env: production requires ASME_SECRET_KEY (non-default), and should set ASME_DATABASE_URL (Postgres via psycopg2-binary), ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=1 (auto only on Render), ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN for multi-worker stability, ASME_AUTO_MIGRATE=0.

**Risks / known defects**

- Multi-worker session invalidation: app_boot_token defaults to uuid4().hex generated inside Settings.from_env at each create_app; gunicorn is run with --workers 2 and no --preload in Procfile and render.yaml, so each worker process computes a different token and current_auth_user() calls sign_out_user() whenever a request lands on the other worker. ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN is not set in render.yaml, .ebextensions, or .env.example. Verify in production; set ASME_APP_BOOT_TOKEN (or --preload) before relying on logins.
- Procfile semantics differ per target: `release:` is a Heroku/Render concept. On the EB Python (AL2) platform a root Procfile is honored instead of WSGIPath, and non-web process types are started as long-running processes and restarted when they exit, which would re-run `python manage.py upgrade` continuously on every instance (not leader_only). Confirm against the live EB platform; consider removing the Procfile from the EB bundle via .ebignore or dropping the release line.
- Tests never run Alembic: schema comes from db.create_all() on in-memory SQLite, so migration drift vs. models, Postgres-specific behavior (case sensitivity, NULLs in unique columns, UPDATE ... RETURNING/claim semantics, DateTime tz), and the legacy stamp path in bootstrap.sync_schema are untested.
- Dependency drift across targets: requirements.txt (used by EB and local dev) is range-pinned (Flask <4, pandas <3.1), requirements-render.txt pins Flask==3.0.3/Werkzeug==3.0.3/pandas==3.0.1, and the passing local venv has Flask 3.1.3/Werkzeug 3.1.8/pandas 3.0.5; SQLAlchemy/alembic are never pinned. pandas 3.x has breaking behavior changes vs 2.x, and the range permits either.
- render.yaml declares no database and no ASME_DATABASE_URL; without a dashboard override the free web service runs on sqlite:///inventory.db on ephemeral disk and loses data on redeploy/spin-down. Also no worker service is declared, so the in-process outbox thread (one per gunicorn worker) is the only job runner and dies with the dyno.
- Two gunicorn workers each start their own outbox thread on first request; claims are row-status UPDATEs (no SELECT ... FOR UPDATE), so duplicate job execution is possible under races; LoginRateLimiter is per-process by design so effective limit is 2x.
- Local .env is loaded by create_app during tests (load_dotenv does not override existing vars but does add e.g. ASME_ADMIN_EMAILS, ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE, ASME_SMTP_*); test behavior can vary by developer machine. instance/print_commands.env, if present, overrides os.environ outright.
- Production validate() runs inside the release step (manage.py -> create_app), so an unset/default ASME_SECRET_KEY, SameSite=None without secure, half-configured Outlook, or SMTP user-without-pass fails `python manage.py upgrade` before gunicorn even starts; EB has no generated secret (Render does).
- session_cookie_secure defaults to True only when the RENDER env var exists; on EB it is off unless ASME_SESSION_COOKIE_SECURE=1 is set (warning only). .ebextensions sets no application environment properties besides PYTHONPATH/PYTHONUNBUFFERED.
- Silent config coercion: _int returns the default on non-integer or below-minimum values and _bool returns the default on unrecognized strings, so typos in env vars are invisible at boot.
- Global test state: event subscribers and outbox handler registries are module-level and persist across tests in one process; test_outbox leaves test.echo/test.boom handlers registered. Reordering or parallelizing tests (pytest-xdist) could expose ordering assumptions.
- test_route_count_is_complete pins legacy routes (/api/checkout, /transact, /checkin, /pair/item, /export); removing api_legacy/kiosk/legacy_ops blueprints breaks the suite. README says the members table/legacy models are kept deliberately for one release.
- Onboarding math is hard-coded in tests (13 required tasks, 1/13 baseline from @uiowa.edu emails, 6 phases, 3 modules, 40-item and 6-location chapter thresholds); any seed change requires coordinated test edits.
- The default bootstrap admin (admin@uiowa.edu / ChangeMe123!) is re-created by seed_defaults on every release whenever no user has that email; in production this only logs a warning if the password is still the default.
- requirements-render.txt leaves anthropic and python-dotenv unpinned while pinning everything else, so Render builds are not fully reproducible; anthropic minor releases can change client APIs used by asme/integrations/assistant.py (not read).
